Exhibit TWENTY-ONE Hard Problem Registry v329

# Hard Problem Registry

&nbsp;

**An Open Registry of Hard Problems in Science**

&nbsp;

**This file holds every arrival whole in the fields' own words, which is the incoming face.** Resolving the Hard Problem Registry carries the same arrivals resolved in the living register. **One learning at two files.**

&nbsp;

---

# The conditions prior

Where there is science and a method for problems, five conditions hold before the method acts.

The statement holds across readings.

The terms have determinate reference.

The accounts hold their truth value while tested.

A procedure repeated returns the same outcome independently of what precedes it.

Nothing prior enters: what comes to the statement carries nothing of what it carried before.

The first is required by replication, the second by measurement, the third by the inference, the fourth by replication. The fifth is the condition on what meets the statement, and the first four stand on it: a statement holds across readings where nothing arriving at it brings a prior reading.

The five fix what enters and how it runs. A problem adds a sixth, its own, fixing what comes out: the outcome preserves a stated relation over a stated range. This condition is the problem's and not the method's, and it stands at Conserving relation and Conserving reach in every entry.

Everything below follows from these and from nothing else.

---

# 1 What stands open, and what stands fixed

This is an open registry: a set of entries on a stated population, each carrying the same properties.

**The problems stand open.** No entry carries a settled answer to the statement it holds, except where the record settles one and the entry says so.

**The intake stands open.** Nothing is turned away. An arrival is entered, and the collection is added to as arrivals come.

**The properties and the checks stand open.** Every property is stated at section 4 and every check at section 6. Any of them runs against the fields' literature, by anyone holding it.

**Every line stands open at an address.** A filled property keys to the place its statement is verified. A line without an address makes a claim that cannot be met, and check C1 tests for it.

Against these, one thing stands fixed: the properties themselves. Every entry carries the same properties, in the same order, whatever the field. Uniform elements are what make two entries comparable, and comparability is what a registry is for. The schema is closed once, at one place, so that nothing else has to be.

---

# 2 Hard problems in science

**Problems in science** are hypotheses. A hypothesis is an account with consequences following from it, tested together with the specifications it carries. Where a consequence fails, the conjunction of account and specifications is refuted, and no member of it is identified. Where a consequence holds, nothing follows about the account, a rival account entailing the same consequence.

**Hard** is where testing continues and returns no refutation: either no consequence separating the accounts is derivable, or the accounts entail the same consequences. Both are properties of the hypothesis together with what it carries, and neither is a property of what the hypothesis is about.

**Progress** is what stands: established, measured, proved, or ruled out. It accumulates whether or not a problem settles, every approach that ends returning a result about the approach.

**Persistence** is the remainder between what stands and a settling result.

Each entry states one problem as it is posed, and carries progress and persistence with the remainder between them visible. Every entry carries the same properties, filled from the record at which each is verified.

---

# 3 Derivation

## 3.1 What a refutation identifies

A refutation falls on the conjunction of account and specifications. No member of it is identified. A test therefore returns no indication of which specification is at issue where a settling result requires one not to hold.

## 3.2 Specification

A hypothesis carries every specification a consequence derives from. A hypothesis carrying fewer entails no consequence.

A specification is universal under a name where it holds across everything stated under that name, and local where it holds for one statement.

No specification is identified as the one to withdraw, and specifications stand at the number brought in.

## 3.3 Repetition

A result is replicated where the same procedure returns the same outcome independently of what precedes it. A test carrying its own history is not replicable, and no test reads what a prior test produced.

Accumulation stands in the specifications. A result that stands becomes a specification of tests that follow. The inference reads the specifications at the start of a test and writes to them at no point.

Where a problem is durable, the specifications stand unchanged across the tests run against them. The number of hypotheses tested measures the testing.

## 3.4 The two responses

Where a test returns no refutation, refinement or revision follows.

Refinement sharpens the statement, the accounts, or the instruments, within the specifications standing.

Revision withdraws a specification, retains the accounts, and tests again. The inference licenses neither, having refuted a conjunction and identified no member.

Both operate on what the specifications permit. A settling result the specifications do not permit is reached by neither.

---

# 4 What an entry carries

An entry carries two parts. The properties are of the problem, and stand whether or not work has been done on it. The report is of work done, and stands empty where none is recorded.

Each states the record at its address, or stands empty.

## 4.1 The properties

**Conditions prior.** The five, stated in every entry. Every unreachability result derives from these together with the specifications below.

**Statement.** The form in which the problem is posed, in the words it is posed in. Variant formulations stand, unranked.

**The field's account of its hardness.** Where a field states, in its own words, what is hard about its own problem — before any account is proposed. Stated from the field's review or methodological literature, or empty. No inference from the difficulty of the record.

**Accounts.** What each account proposes to exist, on its own content, at the strength its record carries. The number is as posed. No relation between accounts stands here. A statement about the condition of the effort proposes no account.

**Specifications.** Each specification the statement carries beyond the five, with the name it is universal under, or local. The number stands against the number of consequences derivable.

**Specifications withdrawn.** Each specification the record carries as given up with the accounts retained. No reason stands, a refutation identifying no member.

**Common requirement.** What the accounts require alike, where stated.

**Conserving relation.** What the outcome is required to preserve, stated as the problem states it: an ordering that does not cycle, a procedure that returns, a predicate that divides cases, a value standing prior to measurement.

**Conserving reach.** The range over which the relation is required, stated as the problem states it: all profiles, all programs, all statements of the language, all cases.

**Reach.** What the statement quantifies over. What its instruments return values over. Where a relation between the two is stated — an equivalence, a proved limitation, an account of what a method does not deliver — that statement stands.

**Unreachability.** A result establishing that the conserving relation is not preserved over the conserving reach. The result is about the conditions and stands whether or not an attempt was made. What was attempted stands at Approaches.

**Persistence.** The settling result specified.

**Addresses.** The location at which each property is verified, keyed property by property.

## 4.2 The report

**Approaches.** Each direction taken, with its present reach in the measures used, and its standing: refuted; blocked by a proved limitation; equivalent by construction to a rival; answering an adjacent question by the method's own account; instruments disagreeing on the object; narrowing; open.

**Narrowing recorded.** Where the record carries the reach narrowed, or the relation weakened, with results following. Each is stated as the record states it.

**Progress.** What stands, with its measures.

**Own record.** Where a statement about the problem's own standing is made, phrased as it is made.

## 4.3 Register

Properties carry present standing: it is proved that; no algorithm is known; the bound stands at; the parameter space is excluded below; the estimates disagree by measured amounts.

No person functioning as attribution, and no date. Eponyms that are technical vocabulary stand where that vocabulary puts them. Years stand at the addresses.

## 4.4 Statement rule

A precise statement carries nothing the original statement together with its own specifications does not carry.

Short verbatim phrases stand where an exact formulation is itself the object — a named thesis, a canonical question — marked as such.

## 4.5 What is not carried

Priority and sequence. Contested wherever recorded, and no standing rests on it. Names and years stand at the addresses, locating a text.

Importance, difficulty, promise. No statement carries these.

An account not proposed.

Any property of a problem read against another problem.

---

# 5 The entries

## The form

**Conditions prior.**

**Statement.**

**The field's account of its hardness.**

**Accounts.**

**Specifications.**

**Specifications withdrawn.**

**Common requirement.**

**Conserving relation.**

**Conserving reach.**

**Reach.**

**Unreachability.**

**Persistence.**

**Addresses.**

*The report.*

**Approaches.**

**Narrowing recorded.**

**Progress.**

**Own record.**

The five stand in every entry as they stand at the front, the problem being what was fixed in order for the method to act on it.

**A property standing empty is a reading and not a gap.** Nothing recorded at that property, and the entry says so; the record carrying it and the entry not, and check C1 through C19 finds it. **An emptiness is recorded at the entry and never at the door.**

**The entries are numbered from one, in the order they were entered.** **A number is where an arrival was entered and not what it is**, and the aggregate at section 7 counts over filled properties and not over numbers.

## 1 Sorites

**Conditions prior.** The statement holds across readings. The terms have determinate reference. The accounts hold their truth value while tested. A procedure repeated returns the same outcome independently of what precedes it. Nothing prior enters.

**Statement.** One grain of sand is not a heap. Adding one grain to something that is not a heap does not make it a heap. Therefore no number of grains makes a heap. Which step fails?

**The field's account of its hardness.** That no finite number of divisions seems adequate: the phenomenon driving the paradox suggests no more two sharp boundaries than one, and higher-order vagueness leaves no more ground for a sharp line between the true and the indeterminate than between the true and the false. Vague predicates are described in the field's literature as boundaryless.

**Accounts.** A number exists at which one grain makes the difference, unknowable, vagueness being ignorance of a sharp boundary rather than absence of one.

No such number exists, the predicate has no sharp extension, and classical logic does not apply to statements formed with it.

**Specifications.** A predicate has an extension — universal under classical semantics. Classical logic applies to statements formed with the predicate — universal under classical logic. The induction step preserves truth — local.

**Specifications withdrawn.**

**Common requirement.** The predicate divides cases into those it applies to and those it does not, so that a boundary either exists or is absent.

**Conserving relation.** The predicate divides cases into those it applies to and those it does not, the division standing without sway.

**Conserving reach.** All cases the predicate admits, the borderline included.

**Reach.** The statement quantifies over a boundary in the predicate's application. Uses of the predicate are what is measured, varying continuously between speakers and occasions. The two are not the same.

**Unreachability.** A treatment assigning intermediate values introduces a boundary between the values assigned, and that boundary admits the same argument. The division is not preserved across all cases by any treatment that assigns values.

**Persistence.** Exhibition of the boundary, or a demonstration that the predicate's use requires none.

**Addresses.** Statement, hardness, accounts, approaches: Hyde and Raffman, "Sorites Paradox", Stanford Encyclopedia of Philosophy, substantive revision 26 August 2025, §§2–3.4. Higher-order vagueness recurrence: §3.3.1 and §3.3.3. Boundarylessness: §3.3.3, citing Sainsbury 1990. Response taxonomy: §3 opening. Remaining properties: keyed at the next pass.

*The report.*

**Approaches.** Ideal language approaches — logic held not to apply to soritical terms, vagueness treated as a defect of natural language to be eliminated; abandoned with the ideal language doctrines, and recorded in the field's own history of the problem. Epistemicism — a sharp boundary posited unknowable, its location fixed by an unknowable function of unknowable patterns of use; consistent, bivalence retained, and its proponents granting the account is intuitively implausible. Supervaluationism and its relatives — truth on all admissible precisifications, classical laws preserved without bivalence; higher-order vagueness recurs against it, and the subvaluationist dual treats the indeterminacy as overdetermination instead. Degree and many-valued theories — three-valued and infinite-valued semantics, truth-functional, the response depending on which values are designated; higher-order vagueness recurs, and the totally ordered truth-set is recorded as too simple for multi-dimensional predicates. Contextualism and its relatives — the extension shifting with context so that no boundary is ever encountered where one looks; criticised as reaching the forced-march form rather than the paradox proper. The multiple range theory — multiple equally permissible arbitrary stopping places relative to a single context, classical logic and bivalence retained, the major premise false relative to each range. Embracing the paradox — vague predicates held incoherent, or the argument held sound and the predicates applying to nothing; the cost recorded as prohibitive on both sides.

The field states four broad response types available to any paradox of this form: denying that logic applies; rejecting a premise; denying validity; embracing the conclusion. Each approach above is placed by its own literature under one of the four.

**Narrowing recorded.** Contextualism narrows the reach: the division stands within a context, and the reach is one context rather than all cases. Degree-theoretic and supervaluationist treatments weaken the relation: the division admits intermediate standing, and results follow under the weakening.

**Progress.** The recurrence of higher-order vagueness against two families of treatment. Epistemicism's positing of an unknowable boundary, stated by the account itself. Whether every possible treatment faces one or the other is not established.

**Own record.** The argument contains no material of any kind: sand is incidental, and the same argument runs for any predicate admitting small increments.

## 2 The concept of disease

**Conditions prior.** The statement holds across readings. The terms have determinate reference. The accounts hold their truth value while tested. A procedure repeated returns the same outcome independently of what precedes it. Nothing prior enters.

**Statement.** What makes a condition a disease: natural dysfunction, or harm judged against values.

**The field's account of its hardness.**

**Accounts.** Disease is statistical-biological dysfunction against a reference class, value-free in principle.

Disease is essentially evaluative, harm belonging to the concept, no value-free account capturing the extension.

Dysfunction and harm both belong, the hybrid standing recorded between the two.

**Specifications.** Medicine's classificatory practice — universal under medicine. Function ascription as biology supplies it — universal under biology. The manuals' lists as the operational extension — local.

**Specifications withdrawn.**

**Common requirement.** A fact about the concept's content or its referent.

**Conserving relation.** A criterion sorting conditions into disease and not-disease, the sorting standing without appeal to the case.

**Conserving reach.** All conditions medicine classifies, the canonical cases and the borderline alike.

**Reach.** The statement quantifies over the criterion of disease. Physiological function measured against reference classes is what is measured, the choice of reference class standing as the crux. The two are not the same.

**Unreachability.** Measurement fixes function against a reference class and does not fix the class. The canonical cases sort differently under each account, and the sorting is not preserved across all conditions by any criterion that fixes function against a class.

**Persistence.** A criterion sorting the canonical cases alike under conceptual analysis, or a stipulation adopted as the method of settlement.

**Addresses.** Keyed property by property.

*The report.*

**Approaches.** The biostatistical formulation — stands, with its counterexample record beside it. Normativist formulations — stand, with their counterexample record. The hybrid — stands as the recorded middle, both records inherited. Revisionist proposals — elimination or stipulation-for-purposes, standing published.

**Narrowing recorded.** Stipulation-for-purposes narrows the reach: the criterion stands for a stated purpose rather than for all conditions, and results follow under the stipulation. Elimination weakens the relation: no single criterion is required, and the classifying stands as several.

**Progress.** The case taxonomy. The reference-class dependence.

**Own record.** The manuals' shifting borderline lists, standing as the dispute's data.

## 3 The population concept

**Conditions prior.** The statement holds across readings. The terms have determinate reference. The accounts hold their truth value while tested. A procedure repeated returns the same outcome independently of what precedes it. Nothing prior enters.

**Statement.** What a population is — the unit over which selection, drift, and statistical inference are defined.

**The field's account of its hardness.**

**Accounts.** Populations are bounded by rates of causal interaction, one criterion serving ecology and evolution alike.

Populations are relative to research questions, no single boundary criterion serving.

**Specifications.** Selection and drift defined over populations — universal under evolutionary theory. The statistical concept of a population co-founded with the biological one — universal under biometry. Interaction rates as what bounds a unit — local.

**Specifications withdrawn.**

**Common requirement.** A fact about whether population boundaries are objective.

**Conserving relation.** A boundary criterion dividing organisms into a population and its outside, the division standing independently of the question asked.

**Conserving reach.** All groupings over which selection and drift are defined.

**Reach.** The statement quantifies over the boundary of the unit. Interaction rates are what is measured, the thresholds that would bound a population from them standing as the dispute. The two are not the same.

**Unreachability.** Rates are measured and the boundary threshold is not fixed by them. The division is not preserved across all groupings by any criterion drawn from interaction rates.

**Persistence.** A threshold fixed from interaction rates, or the adoption of question-relative boundaries as the method of settlement.

**Addresses.** Keyed property by property.

*The report.*

**Approaches.** The unified proposal — stands defended against the published objections. Pluralist objections — stand published against it. The historical route — the co-founding of the biological and statistical concepts stands documented, the shared origin standing as the shared crux.

**Narrowing recorded.** The pluralist position narrows the reach: a boundary stands relative to a research question rather than across all groupings.

**Progress.** The measurement methods. The documented co-founding.

**Own record.** That arbitrary populations make the core processes arbitrary, standing as the field's statement of what rides on it.

## 4 The rule-following problem

**Conditions prior.** The statement holds across readings. The terms have determinate reference. The accounts hold their truth value while tested. A procedure repeated returns the same outcome independently of what precedes it. Nothing prior enters.

**Statement.** What fact about a person makes it the case that they mean one function rather than another by a term.

**The field's account of its hardness.**

**Accounts.** A fact supplies it: dispositions, or normative practice, or irreducible meaning-facts.

No such fact stands, meaning being attributed through communal practice rather than grounded in individual fact.

**Specifications.** Past use and mental history as the candidate ground — universal under the skeptical formulation. Every candidate fact is finite — universal under the founding argument. Deviant continuations fit any finite record — universal under the founding argument.

**Specifications withdrawn.**

**Common requirement.** A fact, or a licensed practice in its absence.

**Conserving relation.** A ground determining the term's application, the determination standing for applications not yet made.

**Conserving reach.** All continuations, the deviant included.

**Reach.** The statement quantifies over the ground of meaning. Use histories and dispositions are what is measured, finite by the argument's own point and fitting the deviant assignments. The two are not the same.

**Unreachability.** Every candidate fact is finite and fits deviant continuations. The determination is not preserved across all continuations by any ground drawn from a finite record.

**Persistence.** A fact of the required kind exhibited, or agreement on what a satisfying fact would be.

**Addresses.** Keyed property by property.

*The report.*

**Approaches.** Dispositionalism — stands defended, the finitude and error objections recorded. Normativist accounts — stand defended, the regress objection recorded. The skeptical solution — stands as the canonical alternative, its coherence disputed. Kin structure — the projectibility kinship with the new riddle of induction, recorded.

**Narrowing recorded.** The skeptical solution weakens the relation: no ground determines the application, and assertibility conditions within a community stand in its place, with results following.

**Progress.** The argument's canonical form and its case structure.

**Own record.** The canonical formulation and the standing replies, in the field's surveys.

## 5 Aggregation of preferences

**Conditions prior.** The statement holds across readings. The terms have determinate reference. The accounts hold their truth value while tested. A procedure repeated returns the same outcome independently of what precedes it. Nothing prior enters.

**Statement.** Can individual rankings of options be combined into a single group ranking that is fair by stated conditions?

**The field's account of its hardness.**

**Accounts.** No such rule exists: with unrestricted domain, unanimity, independence of irrelevant alternatives, and no dictator, none is available, and the proof is complete.

Groups combine preferences continually and the results are used. The independence condition excludes intensity information actual procedures use, and rules admitting cardinal comparability lie outside the stated conditions.

**Specifications.** Individual preferences are orderings — universal under social choice theory. Aggregation is a function from profiles of orderings to a social ordering — universal under social choice theory. The function satisfies conditions stated in advance — universal under social choice theory.

**Specifications withdrawn.**

**Common requirement.** That aggregation is a function from profiles of individual orderings to a social ordering, and that the dispute is over which conditions it must satisfy.

**Conserving relation.** A social ordering that does not cycle, produced by a rule meeting the stated conditions.

**Conserving reach.** All profiles of individual orderings — unrestricted domain.

**Reach.** The statement quantifies over all rules satisfying the stated conditions. Particular procedures and their outcomes are what is measured. The two are not the same.

**Unreachability.** No rule meeting the four conditions produces a non-cycling social ordering over all profiles. The relation is not preserved over the reach.

**Persistence.** Within the conditions, nothing: the results are proved. Outside them, which procedures satisfy which conditions, settled case by case.

**Addresses.** Keyed property by property.

*The report.*

**Approaches.** Weakening independence — admits Borda and scoring rules, which become manipulable, and any non-dictatorial rule over three or more options is manipulable. Restricting the domain — single-peaked preferences admit majority rule, holding where the restriction holds, an empirical matter case by case. Cardinal utility — escapes the stated conditions, requiring interpersonal comparison for which no basis is established. Probabilistic rules — escape some conditions, constrained by the random dictatorship result. Judgment aggregation — the same structure recurring for propositions, with its own impossibility results.

**Narrowing recorded.** Restricting the domain narrows the reach: single-peaked profiles admit majority rule, and results follow. Weakening independence weakens the relation: scoring rules produce an ordering, at manipulability, and results follow. Probabilistic rules weaken the relation to a distribution over outcomes.

**Progress.** The four proved limitations on classes of rules, and the doctrinal paradox in judgment aggregation. Each is proved.

**Own record.** That independence of irrelevant alternatives requires the social ranking of a pair to depend on individual rankings of that pair and nothing else, and that the proof proceeds by altering one individual's ranking with all others held fixed.

## 6 The halting problem

**Conditions prior.** The statement holds across readings. The terms have determinate reference. The accounts hold their truth value while tested. A procedure repeated returns the same outcome independently of what precedes it. Nothing prior enters.

**Statement.** Is there a general procedure that, given any program and input, decides whether the program eventually stops?

**The field's account of its hardness.**

**Accounts.** No such procedure exists, and this is proved, no non-trivial property of a program's behaviour being decidable from the program.

**Specifications.** A computation is a finite description operating on finite input — universal under computability theory. Properties of the computation are established from the description rather than by running it — universal under computability theory.

**Specifications withdrawn.**

**Common requirement.** That a property of an execution can be established without executing it.

**Conserving relation.** A decision procedure returning a value on every input, the return standing without running the program.

**Conserving reach.** All programs and inputs.

**Reach.** The statement quantifies over all programs and inputs. Finite descriptions, examined finitely, are what is measured. The two are not the same.

**Unreachability.** No procedure returns on all programs and inputs; verification succeeds on programs people write, termination checkers handle large classes with their incompleteness stated by their authors, and undecidable cases are constructed rather than encountered. The relation is not preserved over the reach.

**Persistence.** Nothing within the conditions: the result is proved. The disputed content is scope, settled case by case.

**Addresses.** Keyed property by property.

*The report.*

**Approaches.** Restricting the language — total languages guarantee termination and lose expressiveness in exact proportion, a known and exact trade. Termination checkers — size-change and ranking-function methods succeed widely and are incomplete by construction, stated by their authors. Bounded checking — answers for a bounded run, which is not the question. Interactive proof — the burden shifted to a person supplying the invariant, not forbidden by the result and not scaling automatically. Probabilistic and average-case decidability — results for restricted distributions, the general statement unaffected.

**Narrowing recorded.** Restricting the language narrows the reach: total languages guarantee termination, at an expressiveness cost stated exactly. Bounded checking narrows the reach to a bounded run. Interactive proof weakens the relation: the return requires an invariant supplied from outside the procedure.

**Progress.** The theorem itself, its generalisation to non-trivial behavioural properties, and the exact expressiveness cost of total languages. Termination checking succeeds on large classes of programs written in practice, incomplete by construction.

**Own record.** That the statement contains no subject matter, concerning procedures and their execution and nothing else.

## 7 The pseudogap

**Conditions prior.** The statement holds across readings. The terms have determinate reference. The accounts hold their truth value while tested. A procedure repeated returns the same outcome independently of what precedes it. Nothing prior enters.

**Statement.** What the pseudogap of the underdoped copper oxides is — the partial suppression of spectral weight above the superconducting transition.

**The field's account of its hardness.**

**Accounts.** Pairs form without coherence, the suppression being superconductivity's precursor.

A distinct state — density-wave, nematic, or loop-current — owns the suppression.

**Specifications.** The superconducting gap's framework as the reference — universal under superconductivity. The probes' onset determinations as the record — local.

**Specifications withdrawn.**

**Common requirement.** An identification of the state.

**Conserving relation.** One state identified as owning the suppression, the identification standing across the probes.

**Conserving reach.** All probes returning the suppression, and the doping and temperature range over which it is returned.

**Reach.** The statement quantifies over the state's identity. The suppression is what is measured, by every probe, the probes returning different onsets. The two are not the same.

**Unreachability.** The state stands measured by all probes and identified by none to the field's agreement, the onset disagreement standing. The identification is not preserved across the probes.

**Persistence.** An identification of the state holding across the probes, or an account of the onset disagreement.

**Addresses.** Keyed property by property.

*The report.*

**Approaches.** Pairing-precursor programs — supported by fluctuation measurements in ranges, the range's extent disputed. Competing-order programs — the orders detected, charge order established, their relation to the suppression standing as the question. The simulator route — the suppression realized in the cold-atom model, opening the model-side identification.

**Narrowing recorded.**

**Progress.** The suppression's universality in the family. The charge-order detections. The simulator realization.

**Own record.**

## 8 The measurement problem

**Conditions prior.** The statement holds across readings. The terms have determinate reference. The accounts hold their truth value while tested. A procedure repeated returns the same outcome independently of what precedes it. Nothing prior enters.

**Statement.** How the deterministic, linear evolution of the quantum state yields the definite outcomes observed in measurement. The canonical statement is a trilemma: the wave-function's completeness as a physical description, the universality of the linear dynamics, and the definiteness of measurement outcomes are jointly inconsistent, and at least one must be given up.

**The field's account of its hardness.** The standing objection that measurement should not appear as a primitive term in the formulation of a fundamental theory. The decoherence literature's own scope statement.

**Accounts.** The state description is complete and the unitary dynamics universal, definite outcomes to be recovered within the formalism.

The description or the dynamics is amended, by additional variables carrying definiteness, or by a stochastic modification under which superpositions collapse.

**Specifications.** Linear, unitary evolution of the state as the law of motion — universal under quantum mechanics. The Born rule linking states to outcome statistics — universal under quantum mechanics. The founding two-process structure, unitary evolution between measurements and reduction at them, as the structure the problem is posed against — local. The theory's writ running from the microscopic to the apparatus — universal under quantum mechanics.

**Specifications withdrawn.**

**Common requirement.** A fact about whether measurement outcomes are definite, and a description of measurement within the theory rather than alongside it.

**Conserving relation.** Definite outcomes, recovered from the dynamics, the recovery standing without measurement entering as a primitive term.

**Conserving reach.** All measurement processes, from the microscopic to the apparatus.

**Reach.** The statement quantifies over all measurement processes. Matter-wave interference establishes superposition for objects of increasing mass, to beyond twenty-five kilodaltons; the parameter space of the spontaneous-collapse models is bounded and progressively excluded, with no collapse signature observed. The two are not the same.

**Unreachability.** Interpretations reproducing the quantum statistics are empirically equivalent within the theory's domain, and no experiment definable within standard quantum mechanics discriminates them. The recovery is not preserved over all measurement processes by any interpretation the statistics admit.

**Persistence.** Detection of a spontaneous-collapse signature, settling against universal unitarity. No null result at any sensitivity settles, the null bounding parameters and standing compatible with both accounts.

**Addresses.** Keyed property by property.

*The report.*

**Approaches.** The decoherence program — environmental decoherence established, its rates measured and its suppression of interference confirmed, the program's own literature stating that it explains the appearance of definiteness and the preferred basis and does not resolve which outcome occurs. Spontaneous-collapse models — empirically distinguishable in principle, the excluded parameter region growing, no signature observed. Interference at scale — superposition established at growing mass and complexity, proposals extending toward mechanical and biological scales. No-go and reconstruction results — the constraint structure settled: impossibility results on local hidden variables and on non-contextual value assignments, the theorem on the reality of the quantum state under its assumptions, and the extended observer-chain results bounding interpretations treating agents as systems. Interpretation programs — each carrying its own recorded internal disputes, the probability and preferred-basis questions within the relative-state program, the relativistic and field-theoretic extension within the pilot-wave program.

**Narrowing recorded.**

**Progress.** Decoherence theory and its measured confirmations. Superposition at mesoscopic scale. The no-go constraint structure. The collapse-model exclusion bounds.

**Own record.** The trilemma as the problem's canonical form.

## 9 P versus NP

**Conditions prior.** The statement holds across readings. The terms have determinate reference. The accounts hold their truth value while tested. A procedure repeated returns the same outcome independently of what precedes it. Nothing prior enters.

**Statement.** Whether every decision problem whose solutions can be verified in polynomial time can also be solved in polynomial time — whether the complexity classes P and NP coincide. Variant formulations stand alongside the discrete statement: an analogue over the complex numbers, and average-case formulations in which hardness is asked of random instances rather than worst cases.

**The field's account of its hardness.** That the absence of lower-bound proofs is the general condition of the field, of which this question is the sharpest case.

**Accounts.** The classes are distinct, on the recorded grounds that decades of algorithm engineering and the parallel effort of cryptanalysis have produced no polynomial-time algorithm for any complete problem.

The classes coincide, not excluded and defended as live: a proof of equality could be nonconstructive, the failure to prove lower bounds is pervasive across complexity theory rather than special to this question, a linear-time algorithm for satisfiability is consistent with present knowledge, and the known chain of class inclusions has provably distinct endpoints while no single adjacent inclusion in it has been proved proper.

**Specifications.** The machine model of computation defines the computable functions — universal under computability theory, accepted since the model's introduction. Polynomial time defines feasible computation — universal under complexity theory, the class robust and coinciding across machine models. The feasibility identification's recorded caveats — problem families proposed as potential counterexamples, and the randomized and quantum models held as standing reservations against the thesis — local.

**Specifications withdrawn.**

**Common requirement.** A fact of the matter about the classes' relation, expressible in the framework.

**Conserving relation.** A proof separating the classes or identifying them, the proof standing by the field's stated acceptance conditions.

**Conserving reach.** All algorithms.

**Reach.** The statement quantifies over all algorithms. The best proven algorithms for the standard complete problem run in exponential time; for unrestricted Boolean circuits computing explicit functions no super-linear lower bound stands, the strongest proven bounds being linear-factor. The two are not the same.

**Unreachability.** Three obstructions stand as proved results about the methods themselves. Diagonalization's lower bounds relativize, and oracles exist relative to which the classes coincide and relative to which they differ, so the method alone cannot resolve the question by its own proved property. The natural-proofs obstruction stands over the lower-bound methods used so far: a property they share would, if extended to separate the classes, break cryptographic pseudorandomness widely believed secure. Algebrization extends the barrier over the interactive-proof-era techniques. The proof is not reached over all algorithms by any method the obstructions cover.

**Persistence.** An accepted proof in either direction, by the stated settling discipline: publication in a qualifying outlet, a two-year period, and general acceptance in the mathematics community.

**Addresses.** Keyed property by property.

*The report.*

**Approaches.** Algorithm construction — a standard toolkit stands, no polynomial-time algorithm for any complete problem stands. Diagonalization — closed as a sole route by a proved limitation. Circuit lower bounds — strong bounds in restricted models, monotone circuits and bounded depth; against unrestricted circuits the natural-proofs obstruction stands. Algebraic methods — the interactive-proof-era techniques evade relativization, with algebrization standing over them. The geometric program — a separation program through representation theory and algebraic geometry, with its architects' own stated assessment that its completion lies far off.

**Narrowing recorded.** Restricted models narrow the reach: strong lower bounds stand for monotone circuits and for bounded depth, and results follow within the restriction. Average-case formulations narrow the reach: hardness is asked of random instances rather than of the worst case.

**Progress.** The completeness theory: thousands of problems across the sciences are complete, and a polynomial algorithm for any one is a polynomial algorithm for all. Primality testing is in P. The hardness-of-approximation theory on the probabilistically checkable proofs theorem. The three obstructions as proved results about the methods.

**Own record.** That the polynomial class is the robust choice for the question. That the feasibility identification carries stated reservations.

## 10 The problem of induction

**Conditions prior.** The statement holds across readings. The terms have determinate reference. The accounts hold their truth value while tested. A procedure repeated returns the same outcome independently of what precedes it. Nothing prior enters.

**Statement.** What justifies inference from observed instances to unobserved ones. The canonical statement: no demonstrative argument establishes that unobserved cases resemble observed ones, and any argument from experience for the reliability of experience presupposes the very inference in question. The canonical modern extension: which predicates are projectible, syntactic uniformity not selecting, since deviant predicates fit past observations equally.

**The field's account of its hardness.** That the argument is a dilemma with both horns closed: a demonstrative argument establishes a conclusion whose negation is a contradiction, and the negation of the uniformity principle is not a contradiction; while any probable argument for that principle proceeds on the supposition it is meant to establish. The field's record carries a significant number who embrace the conclusion that the problem is insoluble.

**Accounts.** Inductive inference is justified or justifiable, through de facto reliability, through pragmatic vindication, through probabilistic coherence and convergence, or through grounding in local matters of fact.

It is not: the skeptical conclusion stands, and scientific method neither needs nor possesses inductive justification, corroboration being non-inductive.

**Specifications.** Deductive validity as the standard of justification, against which the inductive gap is posed — universal under the framework. Matters of fact known through experience — universal under empiricism. The uniformity assumption, named in the founding statement as what the inference would require and cannot non-circularly obtain — local.

**Specifications withdrawn.**

**Common requirement.** A fact about whether inductive methods are truth-conducive, expressible in the framework's terms.

**Conserving relation.** A justification of the inference, the justification standing without employing the inference it justifies.

**Conserving reach.** All inductive inferences, those not yet made included.

**Reach.** The statement quantifies over all inductive inferences. Arguments and observations return the past performance of the methods, and the founding statement records that employing past performance is the circle itself. The two are not the same.

**Unreachability.** No argument definable within the framework discriminates the accounts without presupposing one. Averaged over all target functions, no learner outperforms any other, and uniform assumption-free justification is provably unavailable. The justification is not preserved over all inferences by any argument that does not employ the inference.

**Persistence.** The record carries the split as found: one account holds that demonstrated reliability is justification and nothing further is owed; the other holds that any demonstration cites the method's outputs and the question survives it.

**Addresses.** Statement, hardness, accounts, approaches: Henderson, "The Problem of Induction", Stanford Encyclopedia of Philosophy, substantive revision 22 November 2022, §§1–6. The dilemma's reconstruction as premises and conclusions: §2. The response structure by horn: §§3, 4, 5, 6. Learning-theoretic results: §3.4. Remaining properties: keyed at the next pass.

*The report.*

**Approaches.** The field structures its responses by which step of the dilemma is rejected.

Against the first horn, that no demonstrative argument reaches the principle: synthetic a priori knowledge, the uniformity secured as a condition of experience; the nomological-explanatory route, inference to the best explanation taken as an a priori mode distinct from extrapolation, its independence from inductive inference disputed; the Bayesian route, the coherence and convergence results standing conditional on priors the framework does not select, with the recorded judgement that the choice of prior introduces empirical assumptions; partial solutions, Bayesian conditioning read as a consistency constraint given an empirical premise, with the learning-theoretic results establishing that no algorithm outperforms another averaged over all target functions and that model-relative guarantees stand where universal ones do not; the combinatorial route, the proportional syllogism applied to samples matching populations, its final step recorded as equivocating between two senses of high probability.

Against the second horn, that any probable argument is circular: inductive justifications of induction, the circularity argued to be rule-circular rather than premise-circular and benign for a fundamental rule, with the counterinductive rule shown to support itself in the same fashion; and the no-rules route, each inductive inference resting on its own empirical presupposition so that the circularity becomes a regress, with the field divided on whether a regress is better.

Against the conception of justification itself: postulates and hinges, principles held as entitlements requiring no supporting argument; the ordinary language dissolution, an inference counting as justified where it conforms to inductive standards, with the objection that conformity does not establish likely truth; the pragmatic vindication, the inductive rule justified as a necessary condition of success if success is possible, recorded as tied to the long run while inductive practice occurs in the short; formal learning theory, methods justified by optimality toward stated epistemic ends, relative to a given hypothesis space; and meta-induction, a weighted strategy over accessible prediction methods proved long-run optimal, with the transfer of that justification to object-level induction disputed.

And accepting the skeptical conclusion: the deductivist account of science, holding that science makes no extrapolative inferences at all, met by the objection that unrefuted hypotheses are left on an equal footing and corroboration takes it beyond strict deductivism.

**Narrowing recorded.** Assumption-relative learning narrows the reach: guarantees stand conditional on sampling assumptions rather than over all inferences, and results follow. Pragmatic vindication weakens the relation: the justification is asymptotic rather than for the inference at hand. The material theory narrows the reach: inductions are licensed locally rather than by a universal schema.

**Progress.** The circle, as a meta-result of the founding statement. The failure of syntactic projectibility. The conditional convergence theorems. The no-free-lunch limitation and the assumption-relative learning guarantees.

**Own record.** The founding fork between relations of ideas and matters of fact. The restatement that the classical problem is displaced into projectibility rather than dissolved.

## 11 The reference class problem

**Conditions prior.** The statement holds across readings. The terms have determinate reference. The accounts hold their truth value while tested. A procedure repeated returns the same outcome independently of what precedes it. Nothing prior enters.

**Statement.** A probability is assigned to a single case by placing it in a class of similar cases. Every case belongs to many classes with different frequencies. Which class?

**The field's account of its hardness.**

**Accounts.** The narrowest class for which reliable statistics exist, refined by a homogeneity requirement: narrow until no further partition changes the frequency, and stop there. The rule is applied daily in actuarial practice, medicine, and forecasting, where classes are chosen by this criterion and the resulting probabilities are calibrated against outcomes.

No rule selects the class. Narrowing terminates in the single case, where the frequency is one or zero, and stopping earlier requires a judgment the rule does not supply. The problem recurs in every interpretation of probability, including subjectivist ones, where it appears as the choice of what to condition on.

**Specifications.** A single case's probability derives from a frequency in a class — universal under frequentist probability. Classes are given prior to the assignment — local.

**Specifications withdrawn.**

**Common requirement.** That the single case has a probability and a class determines it.

**Conserving relation.** A selection of the class, the selection standing without a judgment of relevance supplied from outside the rule.

**Conserving reach.** All single cases to which a probability is assigned.

**Reach.** The statement quantifies over which of many true descriptions of a case governs. Frequencies in classes, once a class is chosen, are what is measured. The two are not the same.

**Unreachability.** Narrowing terminates at the single case, and every interpretation absorbs the problem at its own site, which each interpretation's own literature records. The selection is not preserved across all cases by any rule that does not supply a judgment of relevance.

**Persistence.** A principled selection of the class, not itself resting on a judgment of relevance.

**Addresses.** Keyed property by property.

*The report.*

**Approaches.** Narrowest homogeneous class — terminates at the single case unless stopped by a judgment about relevance. Propensity interpretations — probability assigned to the case's own physical setup, the class removed, facing the difficulty of identifying the setup, which is the same question restated. Subjective probability — the class removed in favour of degrees of belief, the choice of conditioning information being the same problem, as its own literature notes. Statistical model selection — chooses among candidate classes by fit and penalty, returning a class relative to a criterion. Legal and actuarial practice — classes fixed by convention and stipulation, which works, the stipulation being the thing in question.

**Narrowing recorded.** Statistical model selection narrows the reach: a class stands relative to a stated criterion rather than across all cases, and results follow. Legal and actuarial practice narrows the reach by stipulation: classes are fixed by convention within a domain, and the practice is calibrated within it.

**Progress.** That the problem recurs in every interpretation, documented across the literature, including by proponents of each.

**Own record.**

## 12 The origin of life

**Conditions prior.** The statement holds across readings. The terms have determinate reference. The accounts hold their truth value while tested. A procedure repeated returns the same outcome independently of what precedes it. Nothing prior enters.

**Statement.** Living systems come from living systems. How did the first one arise from chemistry that was not living?

**The field's account of its hardness.** That no criteria exist for evaluating the competing models: the field's own review states that what is needed is not a definition of life but a theory of life from which such criteria might emerge, and that currently no such criteria exist, with the historical particulars stated in the review literature as probably forever lost in the mists of time.

**Accounts.** Genetic material first: replication with variation makes everything else possible, so a self-copying molecule came first and metabolism was recruited around it. Ribozymes are demonstrated, and an RNA world couples information and catalysis in one molecule.

Metabolism first: a self-copying molecule of the required length is prohibitively improbable without a chemistry already running to build and concentrate its parts. Autocatalytic cycles at mineral interfaces supply that chemistry, and heredity arises within it.

**Specifications.** Living systems and non-living chemistry are distinguishable by criteria stated in advance — local. The transition between them occurred as an event, with prebiotic conditions before it — local.

**Specifications withdrawn.**

**Common requirement.** That there is a threshold separating non-living chemistry from living, and that the question is which capacity crossed it first.

**Conserving relation.** A continuous sequence from prebiotic conditions to a self-maintaining, heritably varying system, the sequence standing without a step supplied from outside the chemistry.

**Conserving reach.** The transition as a single event, prebiotic chemistry before it and inheritance after.

**Reach.** The statement quantifies over a transition, with prebiotic chemistry before it and inheritance after. Present chemistry, and preserved traces from later than the event, are what is measured. The two are not the same.

**Unreachability.** The event is one occurrence, and what is measured is present chemistry and traces postdating it. The sequence is not reached over the transition by any measurement returning present chemistry or later traces.

**Persistence.** A demonstrated route from plausible prebiotic conditions to a self-maintaining, heritably varying system, in one continuous sequence.

**Addresses.** Hardness: Walker, "Origins of Life: A Problem for Physics, A Key Issues Review", Reports on Progress in Physics 80(9):092601, 2017, §1. Historical loss: Pross and Pascal, "The origin of life: what we know, what we can know and what we will never know", Open Biology 3:120190, 2013, introduction. Remaining properties: keyed at the next pass.

*The report.*

**Approaches.** Prebiotic synthesis — amino acids, nucleobases, and activated nucleotides made under plausible conditions, some in one pot, ending short of a self-maintaining system, the gap stated by the field. In vitro evolution of ribozymes — polymerases copying substantial lengths, not yet themselves. Autocatalytic set theory — such sets form above a diversity threshold in model chemistries, the models not being the chemistry of the early Earth, which their authors state. Hydrothermal and mineral surface chemistry — gradients and organics produced, establishing plausibility of conditions rather than the transition. Search for traces — isotopic and morphological evidence contested at the relevant age, the earliest rocks metamorphosed. Panspermia — relocates the event without addressing it, as its proponents acknowledge.

**Narrowing recorded.** Autocatalytic set theory narrows the reach: the result stands over model chemistries rather than over the transition, and results follow within the models.

**Progress.** That the building blocks form abiotically under plausible conditions. That RNA can be both information and catalyst. That autocatalytic sets form above a threshold in model chemistries.

**Own record.**

## 13 Underdetermination of theory by evidence

**Conditions prior.** The statement holds across readings. The terms have determinate reference. The accounts hold their truth value while tested. A procedure repeated returns the same outcome independently of what precedes it. Nothing prior enters.

**Statement.** Whether evidence can fail in principle to select among rival theories.

**The field's account of its hardness.**

**Accounts.** Yes, significantly: confirmation reaches theories only in blocks, auxiliary hypotheses absorb refutation, and empirically equivalent rivals stand constructible.

No, not in any scientifically significant form: the equivalent rivals are artifacts or notational variants, and transient underdetermination resolves as inquiry proceeds.

**Specifications.** Hypotheses meet experience in bodies, not singly — universal under the founding holism.

**Specifications withdrawn.**

**Common requirement.** A fact about the reach of evidential discrimination.

**Conserving relation.** Evidence selecting among rivals, the selection standing on evidence rather than on a criterion supplied beside it.

**Conserving reach.** All rival theories, the constructed equivalents included.

**Reach.** The statement quantifies over the limits of evidence. The case record — historical rivalries and their resolutions — and the constructed-equivalence results are what is measured. The two are not the same.

**Unreachability.** The construction results stand as existence results, and the case record is cited by both accounts, as evidence of transience and as evidence of theory-choice by extra-evidential virtue. The selection is not preserved over all rivals by evidence alone.

**Persistence.** The record carries the split over what significance requires.

**Addresses.** Keyed property by property.

*The report.*

**Approaches.** Constructed equivalents — stand as existence results, their scientific significance disputed. Historical cases — recorded as resolving, cited by both accounts. The virtues route — simplicity and unification stand as working criteria, their epistemic standing disputed.

**Narrowing recorded.** The virtues route weakens the relation: selection stands on evidence together with simplicity and unification rather than on evidence alone, and the criteria are in working use.

**Progress.** The holism as a logical point. The construction results. The case record.

**Own record.** The founding statement of holism and the reply literature's transience statements.

## 14 The demarcation problem

**Conditions prior.** The statement holds across readings. The terms have determinate reference. The accounts hold their truth value while tested. A procedure repeated returns the same outcome independently of what precedes it. Nothing prior enters.

**Statement.** What distinguishes science from non-science.

**The field's account of its hardness.**

**Accounts.** A criterion exists: falsifiability as the canonical proposal, with multicriterial refinements standing in the later literature.

No single criterion exists: the problem stands dissolved or replaced, in the canonical dissolution statement and the family-resemblance accounts that followed.

**Specifications.** Science's own boundary-keeping — journals, institutions, and courts demarcating operationally, whatever the theory of the boundary — local, and standing as the practice the problem is posed against.

**Specifications withdrawn.**

**Common requirement.** A fact about whether the operational practice tracks a principled boundary.

**Conserving relation.** A criterion sorting cases into science and non-science, the sorting standing over the practice rather than reading off it.

**Conserving reach.** All cases the practice sorts.

**Reach.** The statement quantifies over the boundary of science itself. The case record is what is measured: institutional and legal demarcations, entered and functioning, and the criteria's counterexample record, standing in both directions. The two are not the same.

**Unreachability.** The cases sort under practice, and the criterion dispute stands over the practice. The sorting is not preserved across all cases by any criterion standing over the practice, the counterexample record running in both directions.

**Persistence.** The record carries the split over whether a principled criterion is owed or the practice suffices.

**Addresses.** Keyed property by property.

*The report.*

**Approaches.** Falsifiability — stands as the canonical proposal, with its counterexample record standing in the literature. The dissolution — stands as the canonical counter-statement. The multicriterial revival — stands published, case-driven. The legal record — court demarcations standing as operational data, cited by the revival literature.

**Narrowing recorded.** The multicriterial revival weakens the relation: several criteria stand together and case by case rather than one criterion over all cases, and results follow.

**Progress.** The counterexample taxonomy. The operational record.

**Own record.** The dissolution statement, the revival literature, and the operational record standing together.

## 15 The quantum interpretation debate

**Conditions prior.** The statement holds across readings. The terms have determinate reference. The accounts hold their truth value while tested. A procedure repeated returns the same outcome independently of what precedes it. Nothing prior enters.

**Statement.** Several interpretations reproduce every prediction of quantum mechanics and describe reality differently. Which is correct?

**The field's account of its hardness.**

**Accounts.** The question is not empirical and is set aside. Interpretations agreeing on every prediction differ only in what they add beyond the formalism, and adding unobservable structure is not what physics is for. The formalism is complete as an instrument, saying what will be measured with unmatched precision, a century of successful application having required no interpretive commitment.

The interpretations differ in more than presentation — in whether the state is physical, whether there is one world, whether the theory is deterministic — and these are questions about what the theory says is the case. Setting them aside is not answering them, and the differences bear on extending the theory to new domains.

**Specifications.** The formalism admits multiple descriptions of what is the case — universal under quantum mechanics. Theory choice is settled by prediction — universal under the framework.

**Specifications withdrawn.**

**Common requirement.** That the interpretations are genuinely empirically equivalent.

**Conserving relation.** A selection among the interpretations, the selection standing on prediction.

**Conserving reach.** All interpretations reproducing the formalism's predictions.

**Reach.** The statement quantifies over what the world is like given the formalism. Predictions are what is measured, and they are identical across interpretations. The two are not the same.

**Unreachability.** The interpretations are empirically equivalent by construction. The selection is not preserved over the interpretations by prediction, no discriminating prediction standing where the equivalence is built in.

**Persistence.** A discriminating prediction, which empirical equivalence excludes by construction.

**Addresses.** Keyed property by property.

*The report.*

**Approaches.** Looking for a discriminating prediction — collapse models make one and are in the empirical arena; the interpretations proper, by construction, do not. Ontological models framework — the state-reality theorem constrains epistemic readings under stated assumptions, a genuine constraint, its assumptions contested. Extended observer-chain arguments — an inconsistency derived for certain single-world assumptions, contested in detail, the interpretations differing on which premise fails. Simplicity and coherence — each interpretation claiming economy on a different measure. Extension to gravity and cosmology — interpretations differing in fruitfulness, argued on both sides without resolution so far.

**Narrowing recorded.** Simplicity and coherence weaken the relation: the selection stands on economy on a stated measure rather than on prediction, each interpretation claiming it on a different measure.

**Progress.** The state-reality theorem under its assumptions. The observer-chain result and the mapping of which interpretation rejects which premise. That collapse models are distinguishable and interpretations are not.

**Own record.**

## 16 Natural kinds

**Conditions prior.** The statement holds across readings. The terms have determinate reference. The accounts hold their truth value while tested. A procedure repeated returns the same outcome independently of what precedes it. Nothing prior enters.

**Statement.** Whether scientific classifications track mind-independent kinds, or serve purposes by convention.

**The field's account of its hardness.**

**Accounts.** Kinds carve nature at joints, essences or homeostatic property clusters grounding the classifications' inductive success.

Classifications answer to interests: many cross-cutting groupings serve, and none is privileged by nature.

**Specifications.** Induction's reliance on kinds — universal under inductive practice. Classification as the working instrument of every science — universal under scientific practice.

**Specifications withdrawn.**

**Common requirement.** A fact about what grounds projectibility.

**Conserving relation.** A ground for the classifications, the ground standing independently of the interests the classifications serve.

**Conserving reach.** All scientific classifications.

**Reach.** The statement quantifies over the ground of kinds. The classificatory successes of the sciences are what is measured, recorded in the literature as compatible with both accounts. The two are not the same.

**Unreachability.** The successes fit both accounts and the case record is shared, each case cited by both. The ground is not preserved across all classifications by any account the successes admit.

**Persistence.** The record carries the split over what would count as settling.

**Addresses.** Keyed property by property.

*The report.*

**Approaches.** Essentialism — carries the chemical kinds as its exemplar, the record showing biology as resistant, with no universal essentialism standing. Cluster accounts — the homeostatic-property-cluster position standing as the recorded middle. Pluralist accounts — standing published, with the case studies as their evidence. Case studies — the species and element cases, each cited by both accounts.

**Narrowing recorded.** Cluster accounts weaken the relation: the ground stands as a homeostatic cluster rather than an essence, and results follow. Pluralist accounts narrow the reach: a grouping stands relative to an interest rather than across all classifications.

**Progress.** The case-study record. The failure of universal essentialism in biology, by the field's own record.

**Own record.** The standing statements of the accounts, and this problem's own reflexive place: it is the ordering dispute about ordering disputes.

## 17 The definition of planet

**Conditions prior.** The statement holds across readings. The terms have determinate reference. The accounts hold their truth value while tested. A procedure repeated returns the same outcome independently of what precedes it. Nothing prior enters.

**Statement.** What a planet is — by which criterion bodies are classified as planets.

**The field's account of its hardness.**

**Accounts.** Dynamically: the adopted definition requires orbit-clearing, and the classification follows the architecture of the system.

Geophysically: planethood is intrinsic, hydrostatic equilibrium and geological character, under which the dwarf bodies and the large satellites classify as planets.

**Specifications.** Taxonomy tracks scientifically useful kinds — universal under scientific classification. The union's chartered authority over nomenclature — local.

**Specifications withdrawn.**

**Common requirement.** A fact about which properties classify naturally or usefully.

**Conserving relation.** A criterion classifying bodies as planets or not, the criterion standing on measurement rather than on adoption.

**Conserving reach.** All bodies the classification applies to, within the solar system and beyond it.

**Reach.** The statement quantifies over the criterion of classification. The bodies' orbits and intrinsic properties are what is measured; membership under each criterion follows from measurement, and the choice between criteria does not. The two are not the same.

**Unreachability.** Measurement decides membership under each criterion and does not decide between criteria. The criterion is not reached over the bodies by measurement.

**Persistence.** No measurement selects between the definitions on the record; adoption and usage stand as the recorded mechanisms of settlement.

**Addresses.** Keyed property by property.

*The report.*

**Approaches.** Adoption by vote — the definition adopted, with the dissent entered into the record at adoption. The geophysical definition — published and defended, used in the planetary-science literature's own practice, by that literature's account of itself. Exoplanet extension — the adopted definition's solar-system scope recorded as its limit, the dissenting literature recording the definition unusable beyond it. Usage studies — mixed usage across communities, recorded.

**Narrowing recorded.** The adopted definition narrows the reach: the criterion stands within the solar system, its scope recorded as its limit. Adoption weakens the relation: the criterion stands by chartered decision rather than on measurement.

**Progress.** The bodies' properties. Membership under each definition.

**Own record.**

## 18 Biological individuality

**Conditions prior.** The statement holds across readings. The terms have determinate reference. The accounts hold their truth value while tested. A procedure repeated returns the same outcome independently of what precedes it. Nothing prior enters.

**Statement.** What counts as one organism — the criterion of biological individuality.

**The field's account of its hardness.** The reviews' statements that individuality is plural in the science's own use.

**Accounts.** Individuals are the units of selection and reproduction: bottlenecked lineages with germ-soma separation.

Individuals are units of metabolic and immune integration, under which hosts with their symbionts count as one.

**Specifications.** Biology counts individuals — fitness, development, and physiology attributed per individual — universal under biology, and standing as the science's presupposition.

**Specifications withdrawn.**

**Common requirement.** A criterion, the field's own case tables recording that the standing criteria cross-classify.

**Conserving relation.** A criterion counting organisms, the count standing alike across the canonical cases.

**Conserving reach.** All cases biology counts, the colonies, lichens, holobionts, and chimeras included.

**Reach.** The statement quantifies over the unit of life's organization. The canonical cases are measured in full, and their sorting differs by criterion, by the field's own tables. The two are not the same.

**Unreachability.** The hard cases sort differently under each criterion, and the measurements stand and do not select. The count is not preserved across the canonical cases by any criterion the measurements admit.

**Persistence.** The record carries the split over whether one criterion is owed.

**Addresses.** Keyed property by property.

*The report.*

**Approaches.** Evolutionary accounts — carry the paradigm cases, the symbiont cases standing against them. Physiological and immunological accounts — carry the symbiont cases, the colonial cases standing against them. Pluralism — standing published, the concept plural in use, by the reviews' own statements.

**Narrowing recorded.** Pluralism narrows the reach: a criterion stands for a purpose rather than across all cases, and the reviews record the plurality as the science's own use.

**Progress.** The case record. The criteria's extensions, tabulated in the field's literature.

**Own record.**

## 19 The units of selection

**Conditions prior.** The statement holds across readings. The terms have determinate reference. The accounts hold their truth value while tested. A procedure repeated returns the same outcome independently of what precedes it. Nothing prior enters.

**Statement.** At what level natural selection operates — genes, organisms, groups — and whether that question has a factual answer or an accounting one.

**The field's account of its hardness.**

**Accounts.** Gene-level accounting suffices: higher-level selection is re-describable in gene-level terms, and the inclusive-fitness framework carries the cases.

Multilevel selection is real and causally distinct: group-level selection operates under stated conditions, and the levels are not mere bookkeeping.

**Specifications.** Natural selection as the framework — universal under evolutionary biology. Fitness attributed to entities at a level — universal under evolutionary biology. The covariance formalism as the general accounting instrument — universal under the field's formal practice.

**Specifications withdrawn.**

**Common requirement.** A fact about causal structure beyond the accounting, the field's own equivalence literature disputing whether there is one.

**Conserving relation.** A level identified as where selection operates, the identification standing on trait dynamics rather than on choice of framework.

**Conserving reach.** All cases the two frameworks describe.

**Reach.** The statement quantifies over the causal level of selection. Trait dynamics that the inclusive-fitness and multilevel frameworks both fit are what is measured, with formal translations between them standing proved. The two are not the same, and the equivalence results state the gap.

**Unreachability.** The frameworks are intertranslatable over the shared cases, by the field's own proved results, and fit the same data by construction. The identification is not preserved over those cases by trait dynamics.

**Persistence.** The record carries the split over whether any empirical result could distinguish accounting schemes, the causal-versus-bookkeeping statements standing on both sides of the reviews.

**Addresses.** Keyed property by property.

*The report.*

**Approaches.** Kin-selection formalism — carries the social-evolution cases. Multilevel formalism — carries the same cases under the covariance partition. The equivalence results — the translations between frameworks standing proved, the route ending in accounting-equivalence. Empirical test cases — the canonical cases recorded under both descriptions.

**Narrowing recorded.**

**Progress.** The covariance framework. The formal translations. The case record.

**Own record.**

## 20 Species boundaries

**Conditions prior.** The statement holds across readings. The terms have determinate reference. The accounts hold their truth value while tested. A procedure repeated returns the same outcome independently of what precedes it. Nothing prior enters.

**Statement.** Where does one species end and another begin?

**The field's account of its hardness.**

**Accounts.** At reproductive isolation: populations not exchanging genes under natural conditions are distinct species, and the criterion is testable.

No single criterion works: ring species, asexual lineages, hybridising taxa, and pervasive introgression defeat it, and species concepts number in the dozens, chosen for the question at hand.

**Specifications.** Organisms sort into species — universal under systematics. A criterion identifies the sorting — universal under systematics. The criterion applies across the relevant lineages — local.

**Specifications withdrawn.**

**Common requirement.** That populations divide into kinds, and that a criterion identifies the division.

**Conserving relation.** A criterion dividing populations into species, the division standing across the lineages.

**Conserving reach.** All lineages, sexual and asexual, extant and extinct.

**Reach.** The statement quantifies over a boundary holding across all lineages. Populations varying continuously in every relevant respect are what is measured. The two are not the same.

**Unreachability.** Concept choice changes species counts by large and measured factors, which is the instruments disagreeing on the same populations. The division is not preserved across all lineages by any criterion the measurements admit.

**Persistence.** A criterion applying without exception across sexual and asexual, extant and extinct lineages.

**Addresses.** Keyed property by property.

*The report.*

**Approaches.** Biological species concept — works for sexual, sympatric, extant populations, undefined for asexual and fossil lineages, which its author acknowledged. Phylogenetic concepts — apply universally and multiply species counts by large factors depending on the resolution chosen. Genomic thresholds — a percentage divergence stipulated, differing by taxon. Integrative taxonomy — combines criteria and returns agreement where they agree, which is where the question was not hard. Species as a pragmatic unit — abandons the boundary, conservation law and biodiversity accounting requiring one, which the field notes.

**Narrowing recorded.** The biological species concept narrows the reach: the criterion stands for sexual, sympatric, extant populations, its author acknowledging it undefined elsewhere. Genomic thresholds weaken the relation: the division stands by a stipulated percentage, differing by taxon. The pragmatic unit weakens the relation: the boundary stands for a purpose rather than as a division.

**Progress.** That introgression is documented between taxa standardly treated as distinct species, including in humans and hominins. That concept choice changes species counts by large and measured factors.

**Own record.**

## 21 Group three of the periodic table

**Conditions prior.** The statement holds across readings. The terms have determinate reference. The accounts hold their truth value while tested. A procedure repeated returns the same outcome independently of what precedes it. Nothing prior enters.

**Statement.** Which elements compose group three: scandium and yttrium with lanthanum and actinium, or with lutetium and lawrencium.

**The field's account of its hardness.** The report's statement of no objective means to adjudicate.

**Accounts.** Lanthanum and actinium, on chemical behaviour, and as the majority representation of the textbooks by the field's own survey.

Lutetium and lawrencium, on electron-configuration regularity and the undivided d-block.

**Specifications.** The periodic law, arrangement by recurring properties — universal under chemistry. The union's chartered role over the table's presentation — local.

**Specifications withdrawn.**

**Common requirement.** A criterion of arrangement that selects one composition.
