**Conserving relation.** A criterion of arrangement selecting one composition, the selection standing on the elements' measured properties.

**Conserving reach.** All four candidate elements.

**Reach.** The statement quantifies over the criterion of arrangement. The configurations and properties of all four elements are measured, and the chartered project's report states there is no objective means to adjudicate between the compositions. The two are not the same, and the field's own body states it.

**Unreachability.** Both candidates carry a d-differentiating electron; measurement stands and does not select. The chartered body states that no objective criterion within the field decides the field's own arrangement. The selection is not reached over the four elements by measurement.

**Persistence.** A criterion the field adopts; the project's report stands as the field's own statement that none is objectively forced.

**Addresses.** Keyed property by property.

*The report.*

**Approaches.** Measuring the configurations — both candidates carry a d-differentiating electron, measurement standing and not selecting. The chartered project — convened to recommend, its provisional report stating the non-adjudicability and noting a compromise form, no recommendation standing adopted. Regularity arguments — published on both sides across the education and philosophy-of-chemistry literatures.

**Narrowing recorded.** The compromise form noted in the project's report weakens the relation: a presentation stands without the selection being made.

**Progress.** The configurations. The properties. The usage survey.

**Own record.**

## 22 Rank-based and clade-based nomenclature

**Conditions prior.** The statement holds across readings. The terms have determinate reference. The accounts hold their truth value while tested. A procedure repeated returns the same outcome independently of what precedes it. Nothing prior enters.

**Statement.** How taxa are to be named: by the ranked hierarchy of the standing codes, or by phylogenetic definition under the clade-based code.

**The field's account of its hardness.** The recorded statement that ranks carry no equivalence across groups.

**Accounts.** By ranks and types: the standing codes govern practice, stability of names being the stated purpose, secured through typification.

By ancestry: names defined by phylogenetic relations, ranks being arbitrary and non-equivalent across groups, the phylogenetic code standing published and activated as the alternative instrument.

**Specifications.** Names serve stable communication — universal under nomenclature. Taxa are evolutionary units — universal under systematics. The codes' institutional governance of naming — local.

**Specifications withdrawn.**

**Common requirement.** A fact about what the science's naming should track.

**Conserving relation.** A naming instrument tracking what the science requires of names, the tracking standing on phylogeny.

**Conserving reach.** All taxa named.

**Reach.** The statement quantifies over the instrument of naming. Phylogenies are measured, and rank assignment is recorded as not fixed by them, ranks non-equivalent across groups. The two are not the same.

**Unreachability.** Phylogeny is measured and the choice of naming instrument is not fixed by the measurement, by the field's own record of rank non-equivalence. The tracking is not reached over the taxa by phylogeny.

**Persistence.** The record carries the split between stability and systematic correspondence as the criterion of good naming.

**Addresses.** Keyed property by property.

*The report.*

**Approaches.** Revising the rank codes — successive editions, the rank structure standing. The phylogenetic code — drafted, published, activated, adopted by part of the field's practice. Dual usage — both systems in concurrent use, recorded.

**Narrowing recorded.** Dual usage narrows the reach: each instrument stands over the part of practice adopting it rather than over all taxa named.

**Progress.** The phylogenies. The non-equivalence of ranks across groups.

**Own record.** The codes' own scope statements.

## 23 Psychiatric nosology

**Conditions prior.** The statement holds across readings. The terms have determinate reference. The accounts hold their truth value while tested. A procedure repeated returns the same outcome independently of what precedes it. Nothing prior enters.

**Statement.** How mental disorder is to be classified: as discrete diagnostic categories, or as dimensions of continuous variation.

**The field's account of its hardness.**

**Accounts.** Categorically: the diagnostic manuals' categories organize clinical practice, communication, records, and treatment, their validity carried by clinical utility and by iterative revision.

Dimensionally: symptom measures distribute continuously, the measured comorbidity and within-category heterogeneity being the categorical structure misdescribing the variation, with the hierarchical-taxonomy and research-domain frameworks standing published as the alternative instruments.

**Specifications.** Diagnosis tracks conditions of medical kind — universal under medicine. Inter-rater reliability as the founding standard of the modern manuals — local. The institutional authority of the manuals over practice — local.

**Specifications withdrawn.**

**Common requirement.** A fact about the structure of psychopathology, expressible by a classification.

**Conserving relation.** A classification tracking the structure of psychopathology, the tracking standing on measured structure.

**Conserving reach.** All conditions the manuals classify.

**Reach.** The statement quantifies over the structure of disorder. Symptom covariation and reliability statistics are what is measured, with biomarkers not diagnostic at the category level, by the field's own record. The two are not the same.

**Unreachability.** The instruments return covariation that both structures fit, and the institutional documents carry both accounts. The tracking is not preserved across the classified conditions by measured structure, no measurement recorded selecting between the criteria of a good classification.

**Persistence.** The record carries the split: one account holds clinical utility the criterion of a good classification, the other holds measured structure the criterion; no measurement recorded selects between the criteria.

**Addresses.** Keyed property by property.

*The report.*

**Approaches.** Revising the categories — successive editions improving reliability, the validity disputes standing across revisions. Biomarker search — decades of search, no biomarker diagnostic at the category level, the research-domain framework's founding statement posing this as its reason. Dimensional frameworks — published, adopted in research practice, clinical adoption partial, the categorical instruments remaining the governing ones. Hybrids — the manuals carrying dimensional supplements, the two structures standing side by side in the field's own instruments.

**Narrowing recorded.** Dimensional frameworks narrow the reach: the alternative instruments stand over research practice rather than over clinical classification. Hybrids weaken the relation: the categories stand with dimensional supplements beside them rather than tracking the structure alone.

**Progress.** The reliability record. The measured comorbidity and heterogeneity. The continuous distribution of the major symptom measures.

**Own record.**

## 24 Race as a biological category

**Conditions prior.** The statement holds across readings. The terms have determinate reference. The accounts hold their truth value while tested. A procedure repeated returns the same outcome independently of what precedes it. Nothing prior enters.

**Statement.** Whether human races are biological kinds.

**The field's account of its hardness.**

**Accounts.** No: human genetic variation is predominantly within-group and clinal, no subspecies structure stands, and the category is social — the account carried in the field's institutional statements.

A minimal biological realism: statistical clusters corresponding to ancestry groups stand in the measured structure, and a deflated kind-claim is defended on them in the published literature.

**Specifications.** Population genetics' measures of variation — universal under population genetics. The population concept — universal under population genetics. The criterion of biological kindhood — local.

**Specifications withdrawn.**

**Common requirement.** A criterion of kindhood applied to the measured structure, the debate literature recording that the measurements themselves are shared between the accounts.

**Conserving relation.** A criterion of kindhood applied to the measured structure, the application standing on the measurements.

**Conserving reach.** The measured structure of human genetic variation.

**Reach.** The statement quantifies over the category's biological standing. The apportionment of variation, the clinality, and the clustering behaviour under sampling are measured, and cited by both accounts. The two are not the same, the dispute being the criterion and not the data, by the field's own statement.

**Unreachability.** The measurements stand and are shared, and the kindhood criterion is not fixed by them. The application is not reached over the measured structure by the measurements.

**Persistence.** The record states the split is over the criterion of kindhood; the data are recorded as common ground.

**Addresses.** Keyed property by property.

*The report.*

**Approaches.** Apportionment results — measured, cited by both accounts. Clustering analyses — measured, with their sampling dependence recorded, cited by both accounts. Philosophical formulations — the minimal-realist and anti-realist positions standing at strength in the published debate. Institutional statements — the professional associations' statements standing in the record as field documents.

**Narrowing recorded.** The minimal-realist position weakens the relation: a deflated kind-claim stands on statistical clusters rather than a kindhood criterion applied in full.

**Progress.** The variation structure. The clinality. The clustering behaviour and its sampling dependence.

**Own record.**

## 25 The language and dialect boundary

**Conditions prior.** The statement holds across readings. The terms have determinate reference. The accounts hold their truth value while tested. A procedure repeated returns the same outcome independently of what precedes it. Nothing prior enters.

**Statement.** What distinguishes a language from a dialect — and whether anything linguistic does.

**The field's account of its hardness.**

**Accounts.** Linguistic criteria can ground the boundary: sufficient structural distance makes a variety a language regardless of standardization, in the founding framework's distance concept, stated with the claim that a linguist would have to call such a variety a language even unwritten, and pursued in the intelligibility-measurement literature.

No linguistic criterion suffices or is likely to be found: the boundary is sociopolitical, autonomy, standardization, and institutional roofing deciding, as the field's aphorism has long carried and its critics of the distance concept argue directly.

**Specifications.** Varieties are counted and named — universal under the field's surveys, catalogues, and policy instruments, all requiring a unit. The dialect continuum as the measured background against which any boundary is drawn — universal under dialectology.

**Specifications withdrawn.**

**Common requirement.** A criterion of language-hood applied to the measured variation.

**Conserving relation.** A criterion cutting the continuum into languages, the cut standing on the measured variation.

**Conserving reach.** All varieties counted and named.

**Reach.** The statement quantifies over the boundary of the field's own counting unit. Intelligibility is measured, and measured as gradient and asymmetric; structural distance is measured as continua. The record carries standardized varieties mutually intelligible across the boundary, and unstandardized varieties structurally distant within it, sorting oppositely under the two criteria sets. The two are not the same, the measurements returning continua the cut is not fixed by.

**Unreachability.** The measurements return continua and asymmetries the cut is not fixed by, and the same varieties sort oppositely under the two criteria sets, which is the field's instruments disagreeing on its own unit. The cut is not preserved across the varieties by the measured variation.

**Persistence.** The record carries the split over whether a linguistic criterion is owed or the sociopolitical account suffices.

**Addresses.** Keyed property by property.

*The report.*

**Approaches.** The two-criteria framework — distinguishes distance-languages from elaboration-languages, making the boundary double rather than deciding it, with the opposing classifications of the same varieties standing in its own literature. Intelligibility measurement — quantifies comprehension across varieties, returning gradients and asymmetries, with the threshold a stipulation. The autonomy-heteronomy analysis — systematizes the sociopolitical reading, accounting for the standard cases and giving up the linguistic criterion, which is the account. The no-criteria argument — argues directly that final, reliable, uniform linguistic criteria do not exist and are unlikely to be discovered. Catalogue practice — the language catalogues drawing the boundaries operationally and revising them, the revisions standing as the practice's own record of the criterion's unsettledness.

**Narrowing recorded.** The two-criteria framework weakens the relation: two criteria stand where one cut was required, the boundary doubled rather than decided. Intelligibility measurement weakens the relation: the cut stands at a stipulated threshold on a gradient. Catalogue practice narrows the reach: boundaries stand operationally within a catalogue and are revised.

**Progress.** The gradient and asymmetric structure of intelligibility. The continuum structure of variation. The opposing classifications under the two frameworks, documented in the field's own literature.

**Own record.**

## 26 Dark matter

**Conditions prior.** The statement holds across readings. The terms have determinate reference. The accounts hold their truth value while tested. A procedure repeated returns the same outcome independently of what precedes it. Nothing prior enters.

**Statement.** What constitutes the nonluminous mass whose gravitational effects stand measured at galactic, cluster, and cosmological scales — or whether the measured effects instead indicate a modification of the gravitational dynamics at low accelerations.

**The field's account of its hardness.** The assessment that the hypothesis stands on a century of accumulating and consistent gravitational evidence while the constitution stands unidentified. The experiments' own statements of the neutrino floor as the instrument class's boundary. The galactic-regularity literature's statement that the regularities constrain any account, particle or modified.

**Accounts.** A new particle species beyond the standard model constitutes the component, its abundance standing measured at roughly a quarter of the cosmic energy density within the standing cosmological framework.

The galactic-scale phenomenology follows a modified acceleration law without new matter, the tight regularities of galaxy dynamics, including the radial acceleration relation, standing as its evidence.

**Specifications.** General relativity, with its Newtonian limit, as the gravitational law across scales — universal under physics. The standard model as the established particle inventory, containing no cold dark component — universal under particle physics. The concordance cosmology as the framework within which the component's abundance and coldness are inferred — universal under cosmology.

**Specifications withdrawn.**

**Common requirement.** An account of the measured mass discrepancies expressible in the framework, as constitution or as law.

**Conserving relation.** An account of the discrepancy holding at every scale it is measured at.

**Conserving reach.** All scales the discrepancy is measured at — galactic, cluster, and cosmological.

**Reach.** The statement quantifies over the constitution of the discrepancy. Direct-detection experiments exclude candidate couplings down to the neutrino fog, the leading experiment recording the fog entered and observing the coherent solar-neutrino signal; collider and indirect searches bound production and annihilation channels; the cosmological instruments measure the component's abundance and coldness. The two are not the same.

**Unreachability.** The instruments return different verdicts at different scales, a standing scale-split recorded as the field records it: the cluster-collision separations of lensing mass from baryonic mass standing as evidence for constitution, the galactic regularities standing as evidence for modification-like phenomenology. The account is not preserved across the scales by the measurements.

**Persistence.** A laboratory detection consistent with the astrophysical abundance, settling the constitution. In the other direction no terminal null stands recorded: candidates are excluded one by one, the parameter spaces closing without closing the question, the asymmetry stated in the field's own assessments.

**Addresses.** Keyed property by property.

*The report.*

**Approaches.** Direct detection of weakly interacting candidates — the exclusion regions reaching the neutrino fog, the fog standing entered, the instrument class recording its own floor. Axion searches — the haloscope program scanning the motivated mass ranges. Collider and indirect bounds — production and annihilation channels bounded. Astrophysical discrimination — the lensing-baryon separations recorded as evidence for constitution, the galactic regularities recorded as evidence for modification-like phenomenology, the small-scale challenges standing with baryonic-feedback resolutions recorded. Cosmological inference and relativistic completion — the abundance and coldness measured, the multimessenger constraint on the speed of gravity excluding a class of relativistic modification theories, the surviving completions' cosmological fits recorded as the modification program's standing difficulty.

**Narrowing recorded.** The modification account narrows the reach: the acceleration law stands at galactic scales, its relativistic completion recorded as constrained.

**Progress.** The discrepancy itself, measured across scales and a century of instruments. The component's abundance and coldness within the standing framework. The lensing-baryon separations. The exclusion regions to the neutrino fog. The galactic regularities.

**Own record.**

## 27 Heritability and the determination of traits

**Conditions prior.** The statement holds across readings. The terms have determinate reference. The accounts hold their truth value while tested. A procedure repeated returns the same outcome independently of what precedes it. Nothing prior enters.

**Statement.** For a trait that varies across people, is the variation produced by genetic differences or by environmental differences?

**The field's account of its hardness.**

**Accounts.** Genetic differences dominate: twin and adoption designs return substantial heritability for nearly every measured trait, replicated across cohorts, decades, and countries, and molecular data recover polygenic signal in the same direction.

The statement as posed has no answer: heritability is a ratio specific to a population and its range of environments, not a property of a trait. Change the range and the ratio changes, and environmental interventions produce large trait changes no genetic account predicts.

**Specifications.** A trait's variation in a population decomposes into components attributable to sources — universal under quantitative genetics. A design holding one source fixed estimates the other's contribution — universal under the design literature.

**Specifications withdrawn.**

**Common requirement.** That the outcome is to be attributed to a determining source, and that a design holding one source fixed while apportioning variance to the other bears on that attribution.

**Conserving relation.** An attribution of the variation to a source, the attribution standing across environments and genetic compositions.

**Conserving reach.** All populations and all ranges of environments.

**Reach.** The statement quantifies over an attribution invariant across environments and genetic compositions. Conditional quantities specific to the range sampled are what is measured. The two are not the same.

**Unreachability.** A ratio is specific to the population and range sampled, and larger samples return a more precise conditional rather than an invariant. The attribution is not preserved across environments and genetic compositions by any design apportioning variance within a sampled range.

**Persistence.** An attribution holding across environments and genetic compositions, or the adoption of the conditional reading as what the designs deliver.

**Addresses.** Keyed property by property.

*The report.*

**Approaches.** Larger twin samples — estimates stabilise, measuring more of what was reached, a more precise ratio for the sampled range. Genome-wide association at scale — recovers increasing but still partial variance, the gap itself standing as an entry in this collection. Adoption and migration designs — vary the environmental range and the estimates move, which is what the second account predicts. Gene-environment interaction models — estimate interaction terms, design-dependent and typically underpowered, which the field states. Within-family designs — remove population stratification and reduce estimated effects, showing part of the earlier estimate was not what it was taken to be.

**Narrowing recorded.** The conditional reading narrows the reach: a ratio stands for a population and a range of environments rather than across all of them, and the design literature states the restriction.

**Progress.** The replicated estimates across cohorts, decades, and countries. The polygenic signal. The within-family reductions. The measured movement of estimates with environmental range.

**Own record.**

## 28 Simultaneity and the present

**Conditions prior.** The statement holds across readings. The terms have determinate reference. The accounts hold their truth value while tested. A procedure repeated returns the same outcome independently of what precedes it. Nothing prior enters.

**Statement.** Is there a single present moment shared by everything, or is "now" only relative to an observer's motion?

**The field's account of its hardness.**

**Accounts.** There is a present: change is the most evident feature of experience, and an account on which all times are equally real must reconstruct change as a relation among tenseless facts, which its critics take to be no reconstruction of change.

There is no shared present: the relativity of simultaneity is established and precisely confirmed, a universal present requires a privileged slicing, and no measurement identifies one.

**Specifications.** The laws are the same in every inertial frame — universal under relativity. No measurement identifies a preferred frame — universal under relativity. Simultaneity is defined by a signalling convention within a frame — universal under relativity.

**Specifications withdrawn.**

**Common requirement.** That the question is which description is correct, and that one of them is.

**Conserving relation.** A slicing identified as the real one, the identification standing on measurement.

**Conserving reach.** All frames.

**Reach.** The statement quantifies over which slicing, if any, is the real one. Measurements taken in a frame are what is measured. The two are not the same.

**Unreachability.** Neo-Lorentzian reconstruction reproduces every prediction of relativity with a privileged frame added, so the accounts are empirically equivalent. The identification is not reached over the frames by measurement.

**Persistence.** A measurement identifying a slicing, which the empirical equivalence excludes, or the adoption of one description as the field's settled reading.

**Addresses.** Keyed property by property.

*The report.*

**Approaches.** Looking for a preferred frame — the microwave background rest frame exists and is measurable, and is not dynamically privileged, nothing in the laws referring to it. Neo-Lorentzian reconstruction — reproduces every prediction of relativity with a privileged frame added. Presentism with a preferred foliation — consistent, and requiring a foliation no measurement identifies. Tenseless reconstruction of passage — accounts for the temporal structure of experience within a block, its critics holding that it accounts for the appearance rather than the passage. Quantum gravity — some programmes reintroduce a preferred slicing, none established.

**Narrowing recorded.** Presentism with a preferred foliation weakens the relation: a slicing stands by stipulation rather than by measurement.

**Progress.** The relativity of simultaneity, established and precisely confirmed. The microwave background rest frame, measurable and not dynamically privileged. The empirical equivalence of the neo-Lorentzian reconstruction.

**Own record.**

## 29 Free will and determinism

**Conditions prior.** The statement holds across readings. The terms have determinate reference. The accounts hold their truth value while tested. A procedure repeated returns the same outcome independently of what precedes it. Nothing prior enters.

**Statement.** If every event is fixed by prior events and physical law, can a person act otherwise than they do?

**The field's account of its hardness.**

**Accounts.** No: a person is a physical system, and physical systems evolve by law from prior states. Indeterminacy does not help, a randomly produced action being no more the person's own.

Persons act otherwise in the sense that matters: deliberation and reasons are features of how a system's own states produce its actions, unaffected by whether those states were produced. The statement conflates being caused with being compelled.

**Specifications.** Every physical event is fixed by prior physical events and law, or is not — universal under physics. A person is a physical system to which the same description applies — universal under the framework.

**Specifications withdrawn.**

**Common requirement.** That the action has a determining source, and that locating it settles the question.

**Conserving relation.** A determination of what would have happened otherwise, standing on what did happen.

**Conserving reach.** All actions.

**Reach.** The statement quantifies over what would have happened under conditions that did not obtain. What did happen is what is measured. The two are not the same.

**Unreachability.** Timing experiments address an adjacent question and their interpretation stands revised; indeterminism supplies randomness, which both accounts reject as agency. The determination is not reached over the actions by what did happen.

**Persistence.** The record carries the split over whether the ability in dispute is the one the compatibilist analysis defines.

**Addresses.** Keyed property by property.

*The report.*

**Approaches.** Neuroscience of decision — preparatory activity found preceding reported intention, the interpretation contested within the field, and later work showing the signal partly an artefact of averaging. Quantum indeterminacy — supplies randomness, which both accounts agree is not agency. Compatibilist analysis — redefines the ability in dispute, its critics holding that the redefinition changes the subject. Frankfurt cases — argue that alternative possibilities are not required for responsibility, widely discussed. Experimental philosophy — reports what people judge, which is not the statement.

**Narrowing recorded.** The compatibilist analysis weakens the relation: the ability stands as a feature of how a system's own states produce actions rather than as a determination of what would have happened otherwise.

**Progress.** The timing record and its revision. The mapping of which account rejects which premise in the Frankfurt cases.

**Own record.**

## 30 Goodhart's law

**Conditions prior.** The statement holds across readings. The terms have determinate reference. The accounts hold their truth value while tested. A procedure repeated returns the same outcome independently of what precedes it. Nothing prior enters.

**Statement.** A statistical regularity used as a target for control ceases to hold. Why does it break exactly when it is relied on?

**The field's account of its hardness.**

**Accounts.** It was never a mechanism, only a correlation obtained where nobody optimised against it. Applying pressure changes those conditions, and the lesson is not to use correlations as targets.

It was a mechanism, and pressure alters the mechanism. Those measured respond to being measured, and the response is part of the system. No care in selecting the measure avoids this, because the response is to being measured at all.

**Specifications.** A statistical relation estimated over observed instances holds over instances not yet occurring — universal under statistical practice. Acting on the relation does not alter it — local.

**Specifications withdrawn.**

**Common requirement.** That a relation estimated over past instances can be asserted over instances not yet occurring, and acted on there.

**Conserving relation.** A regularity holding under the pressure applied to it.

**Conserving reach.** All instances occurring after the assertion.

**Reach.** The statement quantifies over instances occurring after the assertion. Instances occurring before it are what is measured. The two are not the same.

**Unreachability.** Each replacement measure is gamed in turn, the record across public administration, education, and health standing consistent, and the general mechanism-design case is bounded by the manipulability result. The regularity is not preserved over instances after the assertion by any measure selected before it.

**Persistence.** A measure holding under pressure, or a mechanism-design scheme applying where the setting supplies the required knowledge of preferences.

**Addresses.** Keyed property by property.

*The report.*

**Approaches.** Picking a better measure — each replacement gamed in turn, the record consistent across sectors. Using multiple measures — distributes the response without removing it, composite indices gamed at the weights. Keeping the measure secret — works while secret, secrecy recorded as unstable in institutions and foreclosing accountability, which was the purpose. Measuring outcomes rather than proxies — outcomes also responded to, more slowly, delaying rather than removing. Mechanism design — incentive-compatible schemes existing for restricted settings and requiring knowledge of preferences the setting does not supply, the general case bounded.

**Narrowing recorded.** Mechanism design narrows the reach: incentive-compatible schemes stand for restricted settings where preferences are known, and results follow within them. Secrecy narrows the reach: the regularity stands while the measure is unpublished.

**Progress.** The consistency of the record across sectors. The bound on the general mechanism-design case. The measured gaming of composite indices at the weights.

**Own record.**

## 31 The Lucas critique

**Conditions prior.** The statement holds across readings. The terms have determinate reference. The accounts hold their truth value while tested. A procedure repeated returns the same outcome independently of what precedes it. Nothing prior enters.

**Statement.** Relations estimated from economic data change when policy acts on them. Can any estimated relation be used for policy?

**The field's account of its hardness.**

**Accounts.** Not as estimated: the relations aggregate decisions made under the regime in force during estimation, and a new policy changes the decisions. Policy evaluation requires parameters invariant to policy, and estimated relations are not those.

In practice, with care: some relations are stable over relevant horizons, and the programmes built to supply invariant parameters have not supplied them. A critique no available method satisfies is a counsel of perfection.

**Specifications.** An economy is described by parameters estimable from data — universal under econometrics. Some of those parameters are invariant to the policy regime — local, and what policy evaluation requires.

**Specifications withdrawn.**

**Common requirement.** That there exist, or could exist, parameters describing the system that are invariant to the policies acting on it.

**Conserving relation.** Parameters describing the system, standing invariant under the policies acting on it.

**Conserving reach.** All policy regimes, those not yet enacted included.

**Reach.** The statement quantifies over behaviour under policies not yet enacted. Behaviour under policies enacted is what is measured. The two are not the same.

**Unreachability.** Natural experiments return the estimation problem one step out, and the microfoundations programme assumes the invariance it was to supply. The invariance is not reached over the regimes by estimation from enacted regimes.

**Persistence.** Parameters demonstrated invariant across regimes rather than assumed so.

**Addresses.** Keyed property by property.

*The report.*

**Approaches.** Deep parameters — microfoundations in preferences and technology, held policy-invariant, the invariance assumed rather than tested, estimates shifting across samples, which the field states. Natural experiments — identify effects of enacted changes, bearing on regimes that occurred. Structural vector autoregression — requires identifying restrictions themselves not estimated. Reduced-form forecasting — abandons policy evaluation and forecasts, performing adequately until a regime change. Robustness to model uncertainty — designs policies performing acceptably across a set of models, requiring the set to be specified.

**Narrowing recorded.** Reduced-form forecasting narrows the reach: the relation stands within a regime rather than across regimes. Robustness to model uncertainty weakens the relation: acceptable performance across a specified set stands in place of invariance.

**Progress.** The measured shifts of deep-parameter estimates across samples. The identified effects of enacted changes.

**Own record.**

## 32 The replication crisis

**Conditions prior.** The statement holds across readings. The terms have determinate reference. The accounts hold their truth value while tested. A procedure repeated returns the same outcome independently of what precedes it. Nothing prior enters.

**Statement.** A large fraction of published findings fail to reproduce when the study is repeated. Are the original findings wrong?

**The field's account of its hardness.**

**Accounts.** Many are: publication incentives, analytic flexibility, and small samples enrich the literature in false positives, and direct replication is the corrective. Failure rates are measured and mechanisms identified.

Failure to replicate does not establish error: effects are conditional on populations, settings, and procedures that differ between original and replication, and a null replication is one study with its own error rate.

**Specifications.** A finding obtained in one sample estimates a population effect — universal under statistical inference. An independent sample from the same population estimates the same effect — universal under statistical inference.

**Specifications withdrawn.**

**Common requirement.** That a finding obtained in one study is assertable at studies not yet run, and that the second bears on the first.

**Conserving relation.** A finding holding at studies not yet run, the holding standing independently of the setting sampled.

**Conserving reach.** All studies, those not yet run included.

**Reach.** The statement quantifies over studies not run. Each study's own sample is what is measured. The two are not the same.

**Unreachability.** Replication returns more studies, each with its own error rate, and the interpretation of a failed replication is where the field's instruments disagree on the same finding. The holding is not reached over studies not run by any study run.

**Persistence.** Agreement on what a failed replication establishes about the original finding.

**Addresses.** Keyed property by property.

*The report.*

**Approaches.** Large multi-site replication — reproducibility projects returning rates well below expectation, the interpretation of a failed replication remaining the disputed point. Preregistration — removes analytic flexibility, preregistered studies reporting lower effect sizes, which is evidence about the mechanism, and does not settle the status of the earlier literature. Raising thresholds — reduces false positives and raises false negatives by a computable amount, a known trade. Meta-analysis — pools studies and inherits the publication bias it corrects for by models that are themselves assumptions. Many-analysts studies — the same data to independent teams returning divergent results, quantifying analytic flexibility without adjudicating any single finding.

**Narrowing recorded.** The conditional reading narrows the reach: an effect stands for the population, setting, and procedure sampled rather than across studies not yet run. Raising thresholds weakens the relation: the holding stands at a stipulated error rate, at a computable cost in false negatives.

**Progress.** The measured failure rates. The identified mechanisms. The lower effect sizes under preregistration. The quantified analytic flexibility.

**Own record.**

## 33 Levinthal's paradox

**Conditions prior.** The statement holds across readings. The terms have determinate reference. The accounts hold their truth value while tested. A procedure repeated returns the same outcome independently of what precedes it. Nothing prior enters.

**Statement.** A polypeptide chain has more possible conformations than could be sampled in the age of the universe, and it reaches its native fold in seconds. How?

**The field's account of its hardness.** That the problem is three questions rather than one, by the field's fiftieth-year review: the physical code by which a sequence dictates its native structure, how proteins fold so fast, and whether an algorithm can predict structure from sequence, with the review's own journal stating that it is no longer useful to talk about solving the protein-folding problem, the problem having grown into a field of research.

**Accounts.** Folding is not a search: random or exhaustive sampling would require times exceeding the age of the universe by many orders of magnitude, and the arithmetic is not disputed.

Folding is a search on a structured landscape: funnelled energy landscapes, local structure formation, and nucleation reduce the accessible volume enormously, and the question becomes an empirical one about landscape shape.

**Specifications.** A protein's conformations form a space defined by its degrees of freedom — universal under structural biology. The native state is the global minimum of a free energy defined over that space — universal under the thermodynamic hypothesis.

**Specifications withdrawn.**

**Common requirement.** That there is a conformational space, enumerable in principle, over which folding is defined, the dispute concerning the measure on it.

**Conserving relation.** The native state determined from a sequence, the determination standing in advance of folding it.

**Conserving reach.** All sequences, orphan sequences and novel folds included.

**Reach.** The statement quantifies over the native state of an arbitrary sequence, in advance of folding it. Solved structures and simulated trajectories are what is measured. The two are not the same.

**Unreachability.** Simulation reaches further timescales without reaching general sequences; landscape theory accounts for how fast folding is possible and does not supply the native state for a given sequence, by its authors' statement; learned prediction is refinement within the observed domain, disputed as such. The determination is not reached over all sequences by any method drawing on solved structures.

**Persistence.** The native state determined for an arbitrary sequence, orphan sequences and novel folds included.

**Addresses.** Hardness and the three questions: Dill and MacCallum, "The Protein-Folding Problem, 50 Years On", Science 338(6110):1042-1046, 2012, abstract and the issue's editorial summary. Deep learning from the structure database and the dispute over what it reaches: Jumper et al., "Highly accurate protein structure prediction with AlphaFold", Nature 596:583-589, 2021; the 2024 Nobel Prize in Chemistry citation. Co-translational folding, the chain folding as it is made: reviewed at Cassaignau, Cabrita and Christodoulou, Annual Review of Biochemistry 89:389-415, 2020. Remaining properties: keyed at the next pass.

**Which of the three the properties key to.** Reach, unreachability and persistence key to the third question — whether an algorithm returns the native state for an arbitrary sequence. The second question, how folding is so fast, is answered at the resolving (2.3) and is not what the properties quantify over. The first, the physical code by which a sequence dictates its structure, is addressed at no property here and stands unworked in this entry.

*The report.*

**Approaches.** Molecular dynamics from first principles — reaches microseconds to milliseconds for small proteins with special-purpose hardware, general sequences remaining out of range. Energy landscape theory — accounts for how fast folding is possible, its authors stating it does not supply the native state for a given sequence. Homology modelling — predicts structures from solved relatives, refinement within the observed domain by construction. Deep learning from the structure database — predicts many structures at experimental accuracy, obtained by learning correlations across solved structures, whether it constitutes a solution or a very good interpolation disputed within the field, its failures on orphan sequences and novel folds standing as the evidence in that dispute. De novo design — designs sequences that fold as intended, the inverse problem, which succeeds, the field distinguishing the two directions. Deep learning past the structure database — diffusion architectures extending prediction to complexes with ligands, nucleic acids and other proteins, and the hardest ligand cases at low similarity to training data remaining the reported edge; the failures on orphan sequences and novel folds standing where they stood.

**What the approaches leave untouched.** Every one of them returns a single structure. The measurement is an ensemble: many models fit the same data equally, and the disordered proteins fold to no single native state at all. The single-native-state assumption the statement inherits from the thermodynamic hypothesis is carried by every approach listed and by the statement itself.

**Narrowing recorded.** Homology modelling narrows the reach: the determination stands for sequences with solved relatives. Molecular dynamics narrows the reach: the determination stands for small proteins at reachable timescales.

**Progress.** The arithmetic, undisputed. Structures predicted at experimental accuracy across the observed domain. The measured timescales reached by simulation. The success of the inverse direction.

**Own record.**

## 34 Aging

**Conditions prior.** The statement holds across readings. The terms have determinate reference. The accounts hold their truth value while tested. A procedure repeated returns the same outcome independently of what precedes it. Nothing prior enters.

**Statement.** Do organisms age because damage accumulates, or because aging is produced by the organism's own regulation?

**The field's account of its hardness.**

**Accounts.** Accumulation: damage occurs continuously, repair is imperfect, and the failure to repair is what aging is. Selection weakens with age, so no programme is needed and none would be maintained.

Regulation: aging shows species-typical timing, regulated pathways alter lifespan reproducibly across taxa, and single-gene interventions produce large coordinated changes.

**Specifications.** Aging is a change in an organism's state over time with a determinable cause — local. Damage and regulation are separable sources of that change — local.

**Specifications withdrawn.**

**Common requirement.** That aging has a determining source, and that identifying it as damage or as regulation settles the question.

**Conserving relation.** One of two coupled processes identified as driving the other, the identification standing on measurement.

**Conserving reach.** All organisms measured.

**Reach.** The statement quantifies over which of two coupled processes drives the other. The two together, in every organism measured, are what is measured. The two are not the same.

**Unreachability.** Interventions establish modulation of rate rather than the driver, and the epigenetic clocks restate the dispute in a new instrument, whether the clock reads damage or a programme being the dispute itself. The identification is not reached over the organisms by measurements returning the two together.

**Persistence.** A measurement separating the two coupled processes, or agreement that modulation of rate is what the question asks.

**Addresses.** Keyed property by property.

*The report.*

**Approaches.** Single-gene lifespan mutants — extend lifespan severalfold in nematodes, less in mice, showing regulation modulates rate, which both accounts accept and read differently. Caloric restriction — extends lifespan across taxa, primate results mixed and diet-dependent. Damage-clearance interventions — senolytics and repair-directed approaches producing measurable improvements in some tissues, bearing on rate rather than the driver. Comparative biology — negligible-senescence species existing, showing aging is not compulsory without parting the two accounts. Epigenetic clocks — predict age accurately from methylation, whether the clock reads damage or a programme being the dispute restated in a new instrument.

**Narrowing recorded.**

**Progress.** The lifespan extensions and their taxon dependence. The measured tissue improvements. The negligible-senescence species. The predictive accuracy of the clocks.

**Own record.**

## 35 The glass transition

**Conditions prior.** The statement holds across readings. The terms have determinate reference. The accounts hold their truth value while tested. A procedure repeated returns the same outcome independently of what precedes it. Nothing prior enters.

**Statement.** A cooling liquid becomes rigid over a narrow range with no change in its structure. Is there a phase transition there?

**The field's account of its hardness.** That supercooled liquids fall out of equilibrium well before the putative transition is reached, whether that transition is dynamic or thermodynamic, so the regime the question concerns is not equilibrated in any experiment. The field's reviews record that the leading thermodynamic account reproduces the salient experimental facts while direct and indisputable experimental validation is missing.

**Accounts.** Yes, an underlying one: relaxation times grow by fourteen orders of magnitude over a modest temperature range, and theories positing an ideal transition below the accessible range fit that growth.

No: nothing identifying a phase transition is observed, no diverging static correlation length and no thermodynamic discontinuity, and the glass temperature depends on cooling rate, which a genuine transition would not.

**Specifications.** Phases are distinguished by order parameters and thermodynamic discontinuities — universal under statistical mechanics. A transition is identified by those signatures — universal under statistical mechanics. Measurements are taken on equilibrated systems — universal under thermodynamics.

**Specifications withdrawn.**

**Common requirement.** That rigidity is either a phase transition or it is not, and that the structural signatures determine which.

**Conserving relation.** A transition identified by its signatures, the identification standing on equilibrated measurement.

**Conserving reach.** All temperatures the question concerns, those where equilibration exceeds any experiment's duration included.

**Reach.** The statement quantifies over behaviour at temperatures where equilibration exceeds any experiment's duration. Equilibrated states above that range are what is measured. The two are not the same.

**Unreachability.** Equilibration time grows faster than any experiment can be extended, so slower cooling is bounded by construction, and two theories fit the accessible range and diverge only outside it. The identification is not reached over the temperatures concerned by equilibrated measurement.

**Persistence.** A signature observed at a temperature the theories diverge at, which equilibration bounds by construction.

**Addresses.** Hardness, and the out-of-equilibrium statement: the colloidal random-pinning literature, arXiv:1406.6478, introduction. Thermodynamic account's standing and its dynamical extension: Biroli and Bouchaud, "The RFOT Theory of Glasses: Recent Progress and Open Issues", arXiv:2208.05866, abstract and §1. Facilitation as a purely dynamical account: the same, and the dynamic facilitation reviews, arXiv:2003.03700. Mode-coupling range and its relation to facilitation: the colloid review, arXiv:1608.08924, §3. Structural approaches and point-to-set: the hard-sphere review, arXiv:2305.02452, §A. Remaining properties: keyed at the next pass.

*The report.*

**Approaches.** Cooling more slowly — equilibration time growing faster than any experiment can be extended. Mode-coupling theory — predicts a dynamical transition at nonzero temperature, recovered as a crossover beyond mean field, its range of validity recorded as extending from the onset of glassy dynamics to that crossover. Random first-order transition theory — an ideal transition below the accessible range, its static aspects broadly supported by recent investigation, its standard dynamical extension recorded by its own reviewers as struggling in relation to facilitation. Dynamical facilitation and kinetically constrained models — the slowdown reproduced with no transition at any temperature and thermodynamics assigned no role, the approach rooted in finite dimensions, and the colloid record reporting facilitation growing with density and with the fraction of pinned particles. Structural and geometric approaches — locally favoured structures inhibiting crystallisation, a distinct mechanism for arrest. Point-to-set correlations — a static length measured by the effect of a frozen disordered boundary, one of the instruments by which a growing static length is sought. Search for a growing correlation length — dynamic heterogeneity measured and growing modestly, no diverging static length found. Random pinning — freezing a subset of particles as an alternative route to the transition, with simulation supporting the thermodynamic account's predictions and the dynamical account recorded as less explored. Vapour-deposited ultrastable glasses — states equivalent to millennia of cooling, above the proposed ideal temperature. Computational equilibration at long effective timescales — modern methods reaching deeper into the supercooled regime than direct simulation.

**Narrowing recorded.** Vapour deposition narrows the reach: states equivalent to millennia of cooling are reached, above the proposed ideal temperature, and results follow within that range.

**Progress.** The fourteen orders of magnitude of relaxation growth. The dynamic heterogeneity, measured. The ultrastable states reached. The absence of a diverging static length in the accessible range.

**Own record.**

## 36 Efficient markets

**Conditions prior.** The statement holds across readings. The terms have determinate reference. The accounts hold their truth value while tested. A procedure repeated returns the same outcome independently of what precedes it. Nothing prior enters.

**Statement.** Do asset prices reflect all available information?

**The field's account of its hardness.** That the joint hypothesis problem makes every test a test of a conjunction, stated by the field as its own limitation.

**Accounts.** Substantially yes: prices respond to news within moments, active managers do not outperform indexes net of costs over sustained periods, and anomalies shrink after publication.

No: persistent deviations — momentum, value, volatility clustering, bubbles with identifiable dynamics — exceed what risk adjustment explains, and the behavioural mechanisms producing them are demonstrated experimentally.

**Specifications.** An asset's price reflects expected future payoffs discounted by a model of required return — universal under asset pricing. Tests of price behaviour are conducted against such a model — universal under asset pricing.

**Specifications withdrawn.**

**Common requirement.** That prices either do or do not incorporate information, and that returns data can distinguish these.

**Conserving relation.** Efficiency established alone, separated from the model of expected returns it is tested with.

**Conserving reach.** All tests of price behaviour.

**Reach.** The statement quantifies over efficiency alone. The conjunction of efficiency and a model of expected returns is what is measured. The two are not the same.

**Unreachability.** Any rejection is a rejection of the joint hypothesis, which the field names and does not dispute, and each anomaly can be absorbed by a factor, the factor zoo documented in the field's own literature as the consequence. Efficiency is not separated from the return model by any test of returns data.

**Persistence.** A test bearing on efficiency alone, which the joint hypothesis excludes.

**Addresses.** Keyed property by property.

*The report.*

**Approaches.** Testing an anomaly — any rejection being a rejection of the joint hypothesis, which the field names and does not dispute. Adding risk factors — each anomaly absorbable by a factor, the factor zoo documented as the consequence. Event studies — measure response speed to news, which both accounts accept and read differently. Experimental markets — produce bubbles reliably under controlled conditions, disputed as to transfer. Post-publication decay of anomalies — consistent with efficiency arriving and with data mining, both readings fitting.

**Narrowing recorded.** Adding risk factors weakens the relation: efficiency stands relative to a stated factor model rather than alone, and results follow within the model.

**Progress.** The measured response speed to news. The net-of-costs performance record. The documented factor proliferation. The reliably produced laboratory bubbles.

**Own record.**

## 37 The tragedy of the commons

**Conditions prior.** The statement holds across readings. The terms have determinate reference. The accounts hold their truth value while tested. A procedure repeated returns the same outcome independently of what precedes it. Nothing prior enters.

**Statement.** When a resource is open to all and each user's gain is private while the cost is shared, is depletion inevitable?

**The field's account of its hardness.**

**Accounts.** Yes, absent assigned rights or an external authority: each user's individually rational choice is to take more, the aggregate exceeds renewal, and depletion follows from the payoff structure.

No: long-lived commons are documented, governed neither by private property nor external authority, using locally devised rules with monitoring and graduated sanctions. The outcome depends on institutional arrangements the payoff model omits.

**Specifications.** Each user acts to maximise its own return — universal under the payoff model. The resource has a renewal rate — local. The outcome follows from the payoff structure facing individual users — local.

**Specifications withdrawn.**

**Common requirement.** That the outcome is determined by the payoffs facing individual users, so that arrangements either alter the payoffs or are irrelevant.

**Conserving relation.** An outcome determined by the payoff structure, the determination standing across arrangements.

**Conserving reach.** All arrangements.

**Reach.** The statement quantifies over outcomes across all arrangements. Cases, each with one arrangement, are what is measured. The two are not the same.

**Unreachability.** The folk theorems establish that cooperation is sustainable in equilibrium and equally that defection is, the multiplicity being the result; and design principles extracted from surviving commons face a selection objection. The determination is not preserved across arrangements by cases each carrying one.

**Persistence.** A determination of the outcome from the payoff structure holding across arrangements, or agreement that arrangements are what determines it.

**Addresses.** Keyed property by property.

*The report.*

**Approaches.** Formal modelling — yields depletion under the stated payoffs, by construction. Repeated-game folk theorems — establish that cooperation is sustainable in equilibrium, and equally that defection is, the multiplicity being the result. Field studies — durable self-governance established, with design principles extracted, whether the principles are predictive or descriptive of survivors disputed as a selection question. Laboratory commons games — communication and sanctioning raising cooperation substantially, transfer to field settings contested. Privatisation and state control — both working in some cases and failing in others, which is the field's own summary.

**Narrowing recorded.** Design principles narrow the reach: the account stands over the documented long-lived cases rather than across all arrangements, and the selection objection stands against the extension.

**Progress.** The documented long-lived commons. The design principles, extracted. The measured effect of communication and sanctioning. The record of privatisation and state control working and failing.

**Own record.**

## 38 The arrow of time

**Conditions prior.** The statement holds across readings. The terms have determinate reference. The accounts hold their truth value while tested. A procedure repeated returns the same outcome independently of what precedes it. Nothing prior enters.

**Statement.** The laws of physics are symmetric in time. The world is not. Why?

**The field's account of its hardness.**

**Accounts.** The past was low in entropy: the asymmetry is in the initial condition, not the laws, and everything asymmetric follows from it plus statistics.

Granting the initial condition is the problem: a state of that specificity is overwhelmingly improbable under any standard measure, and naming it an initial condition relocates the puzzle.

**Specifications.** The fundamental laws are time-symmetric — universal under physics. Thermodynamic behaviour follows from those laws together with a specification of the boundary conditions — universal under statistical mechanics.

**Specifications withdrawn.**

**Common requirement.** That the asymmetry lies either in the laws or in the boundary condition, and that assigning it settles the matter.

**Conserving relation.** The asymmetry accounted for, the account standing without stipulating the condition it accounts for.

**Conserving reach.** The initial condition of the universe.

**Reach.** The statement quantifies over the initial condition of the universe. Its consequences are what is measured. The two are not the same.

**Unreachability.** Statistical mechanics derives the second law given the past hypothesis, requiring what is in question, and the cosmological proposals each contain a stipulation at the same place. The account is not reached over the initial condition by measurements of its consequences.

**Persistence.** An account of the initial condition not itself stipulating the specificity in question.

**Addresses.** Keyed property by property.

*The report.*

**Approaches.** Statistical mechanics — derives the second law given the past hypothesis. Inflation — accounts for homogeneity, whether it accounts for low entropy disputed within cosmology, the inflationary state itself being special. Measured symmetry violation in weak decays — real and measured, too small and too specific to produce the thermodynamic arrow, which the field states. Cosmological accounts of the initial state — proposals standing, none established, each containing a stipulation at the same place. Two-time boundary conditions — consistent constructions existing, not favoured by evidence.

**Narrowing recorded.**

**Progress.** The measured symmetry violation and its magnitude. The derivation of the second law given the past hypothesis. The homogeneity accounted for by inflation.

**Own record.**

## 39 The frame problem

**Conditions prior.** The statement holds across readings. The terms have determinate reference. The accounts hold their truth value while tested. A procedure repeated returns the same outcome independently of what precedes it. Nothing prior enters.

**Statement.** When something changes, most other things do not. How can a system determine what is relevant to update without checking everything?

**The field's account of its hardness.**

**Accounts.** By explicit machinery: default assumptions, persistence axioms, and circumscription formalise what stays the same unless stated otherwise, and working systems use them.

The technical answers address the narrow logical version and leave the general one: relevance depends on the whole context including what was not anticipated, and no enumeration of defaults reaches this.

**Specifications.** A system's knowledge is a set of represented propositions — universal under the representational framework. Action changes some and leaves others — universal under the framework. What changes must be derivable from a specification made in advance — local.

**Specifications withdrawn.**

**Common requirement.** That relevance can be settled by a procedure specifiable in advance of the situation it applies to.

**Conserving relation.** Relevance settled by a procedure, the settling standing for situations not anticipated when the procedure was specified.

**Conserving reach.** All situations, those not anticipated included.

**Reach.** The statement quantifies over situations not anticipated. Specifications made in advance are what is measured. The two are not the same.

**Unreachability.** The formalisms solve the logical and representational versions, distinguished by their authors from the general one, and learned models generalise within the distribution sampled, out-of-distribution behaviour standing as the open case the field measures. The settling is not reached over unanticipated situations by any procedure specified in advance.

**Persistence.** Relevance settled for situations not anticipated at specification, or agreement that the specified versions are what the question asks.

**Addresses.** Keyed property by property.

*The report.*

**Approaches.** Circumscription and default logics — solve the logical formulation, their authors distinguishing this from the general problem. Successor state axioms — solve the representational version efficiently, for a specified set of fluents. Probabilistic state estimation — handles uncertainty over a specified state space, the space being specified. Learned world models — acquire relevance from data and generalise within the distribution sampled, out-of-distribution behaviour standing as the open case, which the field measures and does not solve. Embodied approaches — argue relevance is fixed by the task and the body rather than represented, reframing rather than settling, as its proponents accept.

**Narrowing recorded.** Successor state axioms narrow the reach: the settling stands for a specified set of fluents. Probabilistic state estimation narrows the reach: the settling stands over a specified state space. Learned world models narrow the reach: the settling stands within the distribution sampled.

**Progress.** The logical and representational versions, solved. The measured out-of-distribution behaviour of learned models.

**Own record.**

## 40 Simpson's paradox

**Conditions prior.** The statement holds across readings. The terms have determinate reference. The accounts hold their truth value while tested. A procedure repeated returns the same outcome independently of what precedes it. Nothing prior enters.

**Statement.** An association between two variables can reverse when the data are divided into subgroups. Which direction is correct?

**The field's account of its hardness.**

**Accounts.** The subgroup reading: the aggregate mixes populations differing in ways relevant to the outcome, and conditioning on the relevant variable gives the effect.

Neither, in general: which reading answers a question depends on the causal structure, and identical numbers support opposite conclusions under different structures.

**Specifications.** An association between variables is estimated from data — universal under statistical practice. A correct estimate requires conditioning on the variables relevant to the outcome — universal under statistical practice.

**Specifications withdrawn.**

**Common requirement.** That there is a correct reading to be identified.

**Conserving relation.** A reading identified as correct, the identification standing on the data.

**Conserving reach.** All causal structures consistent with the data.

**Reach.** The statement quantifies over the causal structure. The data, identical under both structures, are what is measured. The two are not the same.

**Unreachability.** Conditioning on everything available can introduce collider bias, which is proved; the back-door criterion determines which sets to condition on given the graph, and the graph is an input; causal discovery recovers structures only up to an equivalence class, which is proved. The identification is not reached over the structures by the data.

**Persistence.** A structure identified from the data alone, which the equivalence-class limit excludes, or a graph supplied from outside the data.

**Addresses.** Keyed property by property.

*The report.*

**Approaches.** Conditioning on everything available — can introduce collider bias, which is proved, more conditioning not being monotonically better. Graphical criteria — the back-door criterion determining which sets to condition on given the graph, the graph being an input. Randomisation — settles it where feasible, by removing the structure in question, not available for many questions. Sensitivity analysis — bounds the conclusion over a range of assumed structures, returning a range. Causal discovery algorithms — recover structures up to an equivalence class from data, the equivalence class being the limit, and proved.

**Narrowing recorded.** Randomisation narrows the reach: the identification stands where randomisation is feasible, by removing the structure in question. Sensitivity analysis weakens the relation: a bounded range stands over a set of assumed structures rather than one reading identified.

**Progress.** The proof of collider bias. The back-door criterion. The equivalence-class limit of causal discovery.

**Own record.**

## 41 Externalities

**Conditions prior.** The statement holds across readings. The terms have determinate reference. The accounts hold their truth value while tested. A procedure repeated returns the same outcome independently of what precedes it. Nothing prior enters.

**Statement.** Production imposes costs on people outside the transaction. Can those costs be brought into the price?

**The field's account of its hardness.**

**Accounts.** Yes, by pricing them: assign the right or levy the charge and the cost enters the calculation, magnitudes being estimable and the mechanisms working where tried.

Not in general: which effects count as external is set by where the transaction's boundary is drawn, and the boundary is chosen rather than found. Internalising some prices those and leaves the rest.

**Specifications.** A transaction has parties, and effects falling on parties to it and on others — universal under the framework. The boundary between the two is determinable — local.

**Specifications withdrawn.**

**Common requirement.** That there is a boundary distinguishing effects internal to a transaction from effects external to it.

**Conserving relation.** All effects of a production brought into the price, the bringing standing without a boundary chosen from outside.

**Conserving reach.** All effects of a production.

**Reach.** The statement quantifies over all effects of a production. Effects inside a drawn boundary are what is measured. The two are not the same.

**Unreachability.** Each boundary extension reveals further effects at the new edge, and the efficiency result holds under zero transaction costs, a condition that does not obtain, which its author states. The pricing is not reached over all effects by any boundary drawn.

**Persistence.** A boundary determined rather than chosen, or agreement that pricing within a chosen boundary is what the question asks.

**Addresses.** Keyed property by property.

*The report.*

**Approaches.** Pigouvian taxation — prices the identified effect, requiring the magnitude, estimated under assumptions about the boundary. Coasean bargaining — establishes that allocation is efficient under zero transaction costs regardless of assignment, transaction costs not being zero, which its author states. Tradable permits — work well for a bounded pollutant with measurable emissions, the boundedness being the condition. Full-cost accounting — extends the boundary, each extension revealing further effects at the new edge. Contingent valuation — prices non-market effects by survey, the estimates sensitive to elicitation format by amounts the field measures.

**Narrowing recorded.** Tradable permits narrow the reach: the pricing stands for a bounded pollutant with measurable emissions. Pigouvian taxation narrows the reach: the pricing stands for the identified effect under assumptions about the boundary.

**Progress.** The efficiency result under its stated condition. The measured sensitivity of contingent valuation to elicitation format. The performance record of permit markets for bounded pollutants.

**Own record.**

## 42 The Collatz conjecture

**Conditions prior.** The statement holds across readings. The terms have determinate reference. The accounts hold their truth value while tested. A procedure repeated returns the same outcome independently of what precedes it. Nothing prior enters.

**Statement.** Take any positive integer. Halve it if even; triple it and add one if odd. Repeat. Does every starting number reach 1?

**The field's account of its hardness.**

**Accounts.** Every starting number reaches 1: every number tested reaches 1, past 10²⁰, and heuristics from the average multiplicative effect predict decrease over long runs.

**Specifications.** The map is defined on all positive integers — universal under arithmetic. A question about the behaviour of all trajectories is well posed — universal under arithmetic. The iterate composes into a single reaching — local.

**Specifications withdrawn.**

**Common requirement.** That the iterate composes into a single reaching, so a question can be posed about all starting values at once.

**Conserving relation.** A proof that every trajectory reaches 1, the proof standing over all starting values at once.

**Conserving reach.** All positive integers.

**Reach.** The statement quantifies over all positive integers. Finitely many, and the structure of the map, are what is measured. The two are not the same.

**Unreachability.** No quantity is known to decrease monotonically along trajectories, so no induction runs; and a class of generalised maps is proved undecidable, which the field records as indicating the difficulty is structural. The proof is not reached over all positive integers by computation or by the known monotone quantities.

**Persistence.** A proof that every trajectory reaches 1, or a counterexample.

**Addresses.** Keyed property by property.

*The report.*

**Approaches.** Computation — verified past 10²⁰, returning more of the same domain. Density results — almost all orbits attain almost bounded values, a genuine advance and explicitly not all. Cycle exclusion — non-trivial cycles excluded below very large lengths, bounds rather than exclusion. Generalised Collatz maps — a class proved undecidable, indicating the difficulty is structural. Reformulation as a stopping-time problem — restates it, the same obstacle appearing at the new form.

**Narrowing recorded.** Density results narrow the reach: the statement stands for almost all orbits attaining almost bounded values rather than for all starting values reaching 1. Cycle exclusion narrows the reach: the exclusion stands below very large lengths.

**Progress.** The verification past 10²⁰. The density results. The cycle-exclusion bounds. The undecidability of the generalised class.

**Own record.**

## 43 The neural code

**Conditions prior.** The statement holds across readings. The terms have determinate reference. The accounts hold their truth value while tested. A procedure repeated returns the same outcome independently of what precedes it. Nothing prior enters.

**Statement.** Neurons signal with discrete spikes. Is the information carried by how many spikes occur, or by when they occur?

**The field's account of its hardness.**

**Accounts.** Rate: rates correlate reliably with stimuli and behaviour across systems, decoding from rates succeeds, and the system is noisy enough that fine timing would not survive transmission.

Timing: millisecond-precision timing carries information rates discard, is reproducible across trials in several systems, and is read by coincidence-sensitive mechanisms downstream.

**Specifications.** Neurons carry information about stimuli in their spiking — universal under systems neuroscience. The code is identified by which feature of the spiking carries the information — local. A window over which to define a rate is available — local.

**Specifications withdrawn.**

**Common requirement.** That there is a code, and that identifying its variable settles the question.

**Conserving relation.** The variable the downstream system uses, identified from the spiking.

**Conserving reach.** All systems the code is asked of.

**Reach.** The statement quantifies over what the downstream system uses. Spike trains, analysed over a window the analyst chooses, are what is measured. The two are not the same.

**Unreachability.** Decoding returns amounts of information in rate and in timing, both non-zero, rather than the used variable; and the rate window is set by the analyst, which is where the instruments differ. The identification is not reached over the systems by decoding from spike trains.

**Persistence.** The used variable identified downstream rather than the information available upstream, generalised beyond the systems where timing manipulation has behavioural effects.

**Addresses.** Keyed property by property.

*The report.*

**Approaches.** Information-theoretic decoding — quantifies information in rate and in timing, both non-zero, returning amounts rather than the used variable. Optogenetic timing manipulation — alters timing with rate controlled, with behavioural effects in specific systems, system-specific so far. Downstream biophysics — coincidence detection demonstrated in auditory brainstem, establishing timing is usable there, generalisation standing open. Population decoding — shifts the question to population states, where the same choice recurs at the population level. Metabolic argument — precise timing being expensive, so used where it pays, a constraint rather than a determination.

**Narrowing recorded.** Optogenetic timing manipulation narrows the reach: the identification stands for the specific systems manipulated. Downstream biophysics narrows the reach: usability stands for the auditory brainstem.

**Progress.** The measured information in rate and in timing. The behavioural effects of timing manipulation in specific systems. The demonstrated coincidence detection.

**Own record.**

## 44 Missing heritability

**Conditions prior.** The statement holds across readings. The terms have determinate reference. The accounts hold their truth value while tested. A procedure repeated returns the same outcome independently of what precedes it. Nothing prior enters.

**Statement.** Twin studies attribute a large share of variance in a trait to genetics. Genome studies find variants accounting for much less. Where is the rest?

**The field's account of its hardness.** That the two estimates are of a ratio whose numerator is measured directly from observed effects and whose denominator is inferred indirectly from population data, so the gap can lie in either term. The field records that the indirect estimate assumes no interaction among loci, that models with interaction are also consistent with the observable data, and that under such models the total may be much smaller — a portion of the gap arising from the denominator rather than the numerator. Several mechanisms have been proposed and none accounts for the gap in full.

**Accounts.** In variants not yet found: effects are small and numerous, rare variants are undersampled, and larger studies recover more of the gap steadily.

In the estimate: twin-based heritability assumes equal environments and no gene-environment interaction, and where these fail the estimate is inflated and there is less to find.

**Specifications.** There is a single quantity, the genetic contribution to variance, that both twin designs and molecular designs estimate — local. The designs' assumptions hold in the populations sampled — local.

**Specifications withdrawn.**

**Common requirement.** That a single quantity — the genetic share of variance — exists and is estimated by both methods.

**Conserving relation.** One quantity estimated alike by both designs, the agreement standing across the designs' assumptions.

**Conserving reach.** All traits the two designs are applied to.

**Reach.** The statement quantifies over a variance component defined for a population and environment range. Two designs with different assumptions, each returning its own estimate, are what is measured. The two are not the same.

**Unreachability.** Both designs return estimates and they do not agree, and the intermediate estimates are consistent with both accounts. The agreement is not preserved across the traits by designs carrying different assumptions.
