**Persistence.** The gap closed by variants recovered, or the twin estimate shown inflated by a measured amount sufficient to account for it.

**Addresses.** Hardness, the numerator-denominator structure, and the phantom-heritability argument: Zuk, Hechter, Sunyaev, and Lander, "The mystery of missing heritability: Genetic interactions create phantom heritability", PNAS 109(4), 2012, abstract and introduction. Rare-variant contribution in height and gene expression: the eLife commentary on the yeast diallel panel, eLife 2019;8:e53018, and the studies it reviews. Stratification confounding rare-variant estimates: the stratification analysis, bioRxiv 048181. Non-additive partition in controlled crosses: the diallel panel of 3025 hybrids, reported at the same eLife address. Proposed mechanisms not accounting for the gap in full: the microbiome review, PMC5468393. Remaining properties: keyed at the next pass.

*The report.*

**Approaches.** Larger association studies — recover increasing variance as power rises, the newly found variants having smaller average effect so their contribution is small relative to those of moderate effect. Whole-genome sequencing for rare variants — rare variants with effect sizes an order larger than common ones, recorded as accounting for most of the gap in height and a substantial share of gene-expression heritability, with subtle population stratification recorded as confounding rare-variant estimates. Estimation from all markers jointly — returns values between the two, consistent with both accounts. Within-family designs — reduce estimates substantially, showing part of the earlier figure was population structure rather than direct genetic effect. Interaction modelling — underpowered at available sample sizes, which the field states. The phantom-heritability argument — the indirect estimate shown to be inflated where loci interact, relocating part of the gap to the denominator, with the interaction models recorded as consistent with the same observable data. Model-organism crosses — panels of controlled hybrids partitioning additive from non-additive contribution directly, with a substantial non-additive share measured and dominance recorded as having a key role. Other proposed contributions — epigenetic transmission, sequencing depth, and the host microbiome, each proposed and each recorded as not accounting for the gap in full.

**Narrowing recorded.** Within-family designs narrow the reach: the estimate stands with population structure removed rather than across the sampled population.

**Progress.** The variance recovered by association studies. The measured reduction under within-family designs. The intermediate SNP-based values.

**Own record.**

## 45 Is and ought

**Conditions prior.** The statement holds across readings. The terms have determinate reference. The accounts hold their truth value while tested. A procedure repeated returns the same outcome independently of what precedes it. Nothing prior enters.

**Statement.** Can a statement about what ought to be done follow from statements about what is the case?

**The field's account of its hardness.**

**Accounts.** No: a conclusion containing an evaluative term cannot be validly derived from premises containing none, since validity preserves only what the premises contain. The point is logical rather than substantive, holds whatever the facts turn out to be, and is what makes moral criticism of any factual arrangement possible at all — if ought followed from is, whatever is the case would be what ought to be.

The gap is bridged by the meanings of terms: institutional and functional facts carry evaluative content, and inferences from them to obligations are ordinary. The prohibition assumes a separation of vocabularies language does not observe.

**Specifications.** Statements sort into descriptive and evaluative — local. Validity is a relation between premises and conclusion preserving content in the premises — universal under logic.

**Specifications withdrawn.**

**Common requirement.** That descriptive and evaluative statements are two kinds, and that the question is whether one entails the other.

**Conserving relation.** A determination of whether the entailment holds, standing over all possible derivations.

**Conserving reach.** All possible derivations.

**Reach.** The statement quantifies over all possible derivations. Exhibited derivations, one at a time, are what is measured. The two are not the same.

**Unreachability.** The prohibition is proved for restricted logical languages, its scope being exactly the restriction; and each exhibited derivation is met by locating an evaluative premise in it. The determination is not reached over all derivations by exhibited derivations.

**Persistence.** A derivation whose premises are agreed to contain no evaluative term, or a proof of the prohibition for an unrestricted language.

**Addresses.** Keyed property by property.

*The report.*

**Approaches.** Exhibiting a derivation — the promising argument, its critics locating an evaluative premise in the institutional description. Naturalising the evaluative terms — defines goodness in natural terms, the open question argument pressed against each definition, the dispute old and live. Denying the evaluative side has truth values — expressivism, facing the embedding problem, which its own proponents work on and have not closed. Thick concepts — terms carrying description and evaluation together, claimed by both accounts, whether they can be factored being the dispute restated. Formalising the prohibition — proofs existing for restricted logical languages, their scope being exactly the restriction.

**Narrowing recorded.** Formalisation narrows the reach: the prohibition stands proved for restricted logical languages. Expressivism weakens the relation: the evaluative side carries no truth value, and the entailment question is replaced by an account of what evaluative utterance does.

**Progress.** The proofs for restricted languages. The counterexample record against each naturalising definition. The embedding problem, stated and open.

**Own record.**

## 46 Placebo effects

**Conditions prior.** The statement holds across readings. The terms have determinate reference. The accounts hold their truth value while tested. A procedure repeated returns the same outcome independently of what precedes it. Nothing prior enters.

**Statement.** Patients given an inert substance sometimes improve. Is the improvement produced by the administration?

**The field's account of its hardness.**

**Accounts.** Yes, and measurably: open-label trials show improvement without deception, ritual dose-response is documented, and imaging shows endogenous opioid release in placebo analgesia.

Much of what is called a placebo effect is not an effect: regression to the mean, natural history, and reporting bias account for a large share, and trials with no-treatment arms find the difference small outside pain and subjective outcomes.

**Specifications.** A trial's arms differ only in the substance administered — local. The difference between arms estimates the substance's effect — universal under trial methodology.

**Specifications withdrawn.**

**Common requirement.** That the inert substance's administration either does or does not produce the improvement, and that a comparison isolates which.

**Conserving relation.** The administration's contribution isolated, the isolation standing with context removed.

**Conserving reach.** All outcomes the effect is asked of.

**Reach.** The statement quantifies over what the administration contributes. Arms of a trial, each including a context that cannot be removed while the trial runs, are what is measured. The two are not the same.

**Unreachability.** No-treatment is itself a context, so the comparison does not remove what it is to isolate. The contribution is not isolated over the outcomes by any arm of a running trial.

**Persistence.** The contribution isolated with context removed, or agreement that context is part of what the question asks about.

**Addresses.** Keyed property by property.

*The report.*

**Approaches.** Three-arm trials with no-treatment control — small effects except for pain and subjective outcomes, the strongest route, its own limitation being that no-treatment is itself a context. Open-label placebo — improvement without deception, replicated, narrowing the mechanism while the control problem persists. Neuroimaging and antagonist blockade — blockade of placebo analgesia establishing a mechanism for that outcome, specific to it. Conditioning paradigms — measurable immune and endocrine responses in animals, where reporting bias is absent, the strongest evidence for a real effect, in a different setting. Nocebo studies — symmetric findings, same methodological position.

**Narrowing recorded.** Antagonist blockade narrows the reach: the mechanism stands established for placebo analgesia. Conditioning paradigms narrow the reach: the effect stands established in animals, where reporting bias is absent.

**Progress.** The measured effect sizes by outcome type. The replicated open-label improvement. The blockade result. The animal conditioning responses.

**Own record.**

## 47 Turbulence and the Navier–Stokes equations

**Conditions prior.** The statement holds across readings. The terms have determinate reference. The accounts hold their truth value while tested. A procedure repeated returns the same outcome independently of what precedes it. Nothing prior enters.

**Statement.** Do smooth solutions of the equations of fluid motion in three dimensions remain smooth for all time, or can they develop a singularity?

**The field's account of its hardness.** Supercriticality, in the field's own formalisation: the a priori bounds available are not scale-invariant in three dimensions, classical energy methods do not prevent concentration at small scales, and any proposed positive solution to the regularity problem which does not use the finer structure of the nonlinearity cannot possibly be successful, established through finite-time blow-up of an averaged equation sharing the known bounds.

**Accounts.** They remain smooth: no singularity has been observed in any physical fluid or reliable simulation, and the equations describe fluids accurately across the range where they are tested.

Not established, and blow-up is plausible: the scaling of the nonlinear term against dissipation is supercritical in three dimensions, which is exactly the regime where the available methods fail, two dimensions being settled precisely because it is not supercritical.

**Specifications.** The equations describe fluid motion at all scales of the flow — universal under fluid dynamics. A solution means a function satisfying them for all time from given initial data — universal under the framework.

**Specifications withdrawn.**

**Common requirement.** That the equations continue to describe the fluid at every scale, so that a question about all time and all initial data is well posed.

**Conserving relation.** Smoothness holding for all time from given initial data, the holding established by proof.

**Conserving reach.** All initial data, for all time.

**Reach.** The statement quantifies over all initial data, for all time. Finite simulations at finite resolution, and specific classes of initial data, are what is measured. The two are not the same.

**Unreachability.** An averaged version of the equations admits blow-up, showing that any proof of regularity must use structure the averaged version discards, so a class of arguments is proved unable to reach it. The holding is not reached over all initial data by methods that do not use that structure.

**Persistence.** A proof of global regularity, or a constructed solution developing a singularity.

**Addresses.** Hardness and the barrier: Tao, "Finite time blowup for an averaged three-dimensional Navier-Stokes equation", Journal of the American Mathematical Society 29:601-674, 2016, §1.1. Scaling exposition: the partial-regularity and quantitative-regularity literature's own state-of-the-art statements. Remaining properties: keyed at the next pass.

*The report.*

**Approaches.** Energy methods — give global weak solutions, uniqueness and smoothness of weak solutions standing open, which is the same question. Partial regularity — the singular set bounded to parabolic Hausdorff dimension zero, a strong constraint that does not exclude. Numerical search for blow-up — candidate scenarios proposed and repeatedly resolved as resolution improved, recent computer-assisted work establishing blow-up for related equations and boundary cases, not for the free three-dimensional problem. Modified and averaged equations — an averaged construction with blow-up, showing that any proof of regularity must use structure the averaged version discards. Two-dimensional case — settled, its methods not carrying to three.

**Narrowing recorded.** Partial regularity narrows the reach: the singular set stands bounded rather than excluded. The two-dimensional case narrows the reach: the holding stands proved in two dimensions, its methods not carrying to three. Weak solutions weaken the relation: global existence stands without uniqueness or smoothness.

**Progress.** The global weak solutions. The parabolic Hausdorff bound on the singular set. The two-dimensional result. The blow-up results for related equations. The averaged construction and what it proves about admissible arguments.

**Own record.**

## 48 Black hole information

**Conditions prior.** The statement holds across readings. The terms have determinate reference. The accounts hold their truth value while tested. A procedure repeated returns the same outcome independently of what precedes it. Nothing prior enters.

**Statement.** Matter falls into a black hole and the hole evaporates into radiation. Is the information about what fell in preserved?

**The field's account of its hardness.**

**Accounts.** Yes: quantum evolution is unitary, and abandoning unitarity would break the framework everywhere it is tested. Calculations of the entropy curve reproduce the expected turnover.

The semiclassical calculation says no: the derivation gives thermal radiation independent of what formed the hole, and it uses only physics well established at the horizon, where curvature is low.

**Specifications.** Quantum evolution is unitary — universal under quantum mechanics. Semiclassical gravity applies where curvature is low, including at the horizon of a large black hole — universal under the framework. Both hold together in one consistent theory — local.

**Specifications withdrawn.**

**Common requirement.** That the question has one answer determinable from a consistent theory covering the horizon and the interior together.

**Conserving relation.** One answer determinable from a theory covering horizon and interior together.

**Conserving reach.** The interior, and the correlations between interior and exterior.

**Reach.** The statement quantifies over the interior, and the correlations between interior and exterior. No evaporation has been observed, and all routes are theoretical. The two are not the same.

**Unreachability.** Unitarity, effective field theory at the horizon, and no drama at the horizon are proved not all able to hold, with no consensus on which to give up; and evaporation timescales for astrophysical holes exceed the age of the universe by enormous factors. The one answer is not reached over the interior by any route standing.

**Persistence.** A theory covering horizon and interior together from which the answer follows, the premises proved incompatible being resolved by giving one up.

**Addresses.** Keyed property by property.

*The report.*

**Approaches.** Semiclassical calculation — gives information loss, its own domain of validity at late times disputed. Holographic duality — evolution unitary in the dual description where the duality is established, whether the setting applies to astrophysical holes standing open. Island formulas and the entropy curve — reproduce the expected turnover using gravitational path integral techniques, what they imply about the interior actively disputed. Firewalls — the proved incompatibility of unitarity, effective field theory at the horizon, and no drama, with no consensus on which to give up. Remnants and baby universes — consistent constructions with acknowledged costs. Observation — evaporation timescales for astrophysical holes exceeding the age of the universe by enormous factors.

**Narrowing recorded.** Holographic duality narrows the reach: unitarity stands in settings where the duality is established rather than for astrophysical holes.

**Progress.** The entropy curve turnover, reproduced. The proved incompatibility of the three premises. The unitarity of the dual description where the duality is established.

**Own record.**

## 49 Bell correlations

**Conditions prior.** The statement holds across readings. The terms have determinate reference. The accounts hold their truth value while tested. A procedure repeated returns the same outcome independently of what precedes it. Nothing prior enters.

**Statement.** Two particles prepared together are measured far apart. The results are correlated more strongly than any account giving each particle its own properties allows. What produces the correlation?

**The field's account of its hardness.**

**Accounts.** Nothing local does, and the theorem settles it: an inequality is satisfied by any theory in which each particle carries its own state and no influence travels faster than light, experiments violate it, and loophole-free experiments close detection and locality simultaneously.

The theorem constrains a class of theories, and which assumption to give up is not settled: nonlocality, denial of measurement independence, retrocausality, and superdeterminism each preserve the correlations at a different cost, and the field has no agreed criterion for choosing among them.

**Specifications.** Each system carries its own state — universal under the local framework. Measurement outcomes are fixed by that state and the local setting — universal under the local framework. Settings are chosen independently of the state — universal under experimental practice. No influence propagates faster than light — universal under relativity.

**Specifications withdrawn.** The conjunction of the four is refuted by the theorem together with loophole-free experiment, and the record carries no agreement on which is given up.

**Common requirement.** That the correlation is produced by something specifiable at one side or transmitted between the sides.

**Conserving relation.** An account of what produces the correlation, the account standing on the coincidence counts.

**Conserving reach.** All accounts preserving the observed correlations.

**Reach.** The statement quantifies over what produces the correlation. Coincidence counts at two stations are what is measured. The two are not the same.

**Unreachability.** The surviving accounts are empirically equivalent to each other, each preserving the correlations at a different cost, and the field carries no agreed criterion for choosing among them. The account is not reached over the surviving class by the coincidence counts.

**Persistence.** A criterion for choosing among the surviving accounts, or a distinguishing prediction between them.

**Addresses.** Keyed property by property.

*The report.*

**Approaches.** Closing the loopholes — detection, locality, and freedom-of-choice loopholes closed, the last using cosmic photons and human random choices, the correlation standing and the interpretation not following. Local hidden variables — excluded by the theorem plus experiment, the route closed outright. Nonlocal hidden variables — reproduce everything, explicitly nonlocal, empirically equivalent. Superdeterminism — preserves locality by denying setting independence, not excluded by any experiment, its critics holding it undermines the practice of experiment. Retrocausal accounts — consistent constructions existing, no distinguishing prediction so far. Information-theoretic reconstructions — derive the correlations from principles such as information causality and reach the observed bound, accounting for the magnitude without settling what produces it.

**Narrowing recorded.** Information-theoretic reconstructions weaken the relation: the observed bound is derived from principles rather than the producing account identified.

**Progress.** The theorem. The loophole-free violations. The exclusion of local hidden variables. The derived bound from information-theoretic principles.

**Own record.**

## 50 Wave and particle

**Conditions prior.** The statement holds across readings. The terms have determinate reference. The accounts hold their truth value while tested. A procedure repeated returns the same outcome independently of what precedes it. Nothing prior enters.

**Statement.** The same entity shows interference in one experiment and localized detection in another. Which is it?

**The field's account of its hardness.**

**Accounts.** Neither, and the statement is malformed: the formalism is complete, wave and particle are classical pictures, and the two descriptions apply to mutually exclusive experimental arrangements.

Complementarity names the situation rather than accounting for it: delayed-choice and quantum-eraser experiments show the arrangement can be settled after the entity has passed, and accounts differ on what this establishes.

**Specifications.** An entity has a determinate nature specifiable independently of the measurement made on it — local. The two classical pictures exhaust the available descriptions — local.

**Specifications withdrawn.**

**Common requirement.** That there is a fact about what the entity is, or a demonstration that there is not.

**Conserving relation.** A fact about what the entity is between preparation and detection, standing independently of the arrangement.

**Conserving reach.** All arrangements.

**Reach.** The statement quantifies over what the entity is between preparation and detection. Preparation settings and detection events are what is measured. The two are not the same.

**Unreachability.** Obtaining path information destroys interference, quantitatively, by measured duality relations, which establishes the trade exactly without settling the nature; and the interpretations are empirically equivalent by construction. The fact is not reached over the arrangements by preparation settings and detection events.

**Persistence.** A determination of the entity's nature between preparation and detection, which the empirical equivalence of the interpretations excludes.

**Addresses.** Keyed property by property.

*The report.*

**Approaches.** Which-path measurement — obtaining path information destroys interference, quantitatively, by measured duality relations, establishing the trade exactly. Delayed choice — the experiment performed, the outcome matching quantum prediction under every arrangement, the interpretation disputed. Quantum eraser — interference recoverable by correlation with the marker, widely misreported as retrocausal, the field stating the correlations require the joint measurement. Weak measurement of trajectories — reconstructs average trajectories in a two-slit apparatus, what an average trajectory establishes about individual entities being disputed. Interpretation — each giving its own answer, and they are empirically equivalent.

**Narrowing recorded.** The duality relations weaken the relation: the trade between path information and interference visibility stands quantified in place of the nature determined.

**Progress.** The measured duality relations. The delayed-choice outcomes. The reconstructed average trajectories.

**Own record.**

## 51 Incompleteness and Hilbert's programme

**Conditions prior.** The statement holds across readings. The terms have determinate reference. The accounts hold their truth value while tested. A procedure repeated returns the same outcome independently of what precedes it. Nothing prior enters.

**Statement.** Can a formal system strong enough for arithmetic prove every arithmetical truth, and prove its own consistency?

**The field's account of its hardness.**

**Accounts.** No, proved: a true sentence is unprovable in the system, and consistency is unprovable within it. The programme as stated cannot be carried out.

The programme survives in modified form: the consistency of arithmetic is proved by transfinite induction to a specified ordinal, and reverse mathematics has established exactly which axioms are needed for which theorems. What was lost is the finitary requirement, not the enterprise.

**Specifications.** Mathematical truth is captured by derivability in a formal system — local. Consistency can be established by means weaker than the system itself — local.

**Specifications withdrawn.** The finitary requirement stands given up in the modified programme, by that programme's own account, the enterprise retained.

**Common requirement.** That there is a determinate set of arithmetical truths, and a system either captures it or does not.

**Conserving relation.** A system capturing the arithmetical truths and proving its own consistency.

**Conserving reach.** All arithmetical truths.

**Reach.** The statement quantifies over all arithmetical truths. Derivations in a specified system are what is measured. The two are not the same.

**Unreachability.** Each strengthening carries its own unprovable sentence, by construction at every stage, and consistency is unprovable within the system. The capture is not reached over the arithmetical truths by derivation in any system strong enough for arithmetic.

**Persistence.** Within the conditions, nothing: the results are proved. Outside them, which axioms suffice for which theorems, settled case by case.

**Addresses.** Keyed property by property.

*The report.*

**Approaches.** Strengthening the system — each strengthening carrying its own unprovable sentence, by construction at every stage. The transfinite consistency proof — succeeds, using induction to an ordinal not available within arithmetic, requiring at a higher level what it was meant to secure at a lower. Reverse mathematics — maps exactly which axioms suffice for which theorems, with striking regularity across five subsystems, a substantial positive result addressing a different question. Ordinal analysis — extends the transfinite route upward, each system's proof-theoretic ordinal established, each needing a stronger metatheory. Set-theoretic realism — holds truth outruns provability and the unprovable sentences are true, consistent, and supplying no method.

**Narrowing recorded.** The transfinite consistency proof weakens the relation: consistency stands proved by means stronger than the system rather than weaker. Reverse mathematics narrows the reach: which axioms suffice stands theorem by theorem rather than for the truths as a whole.

**Progress.** The two theorems. The transfinite consistency proof. The reverse-mathematics map across five subsystems. The established proof-theoretic ordinals.

**Own record.**

## 52 The continuum hypothesis

**Conditions prior.** The statement holds across readings. The terms have determinate reference. The accounts hold their truth value while tested. A procedure repeated returns the same outcome independently of what precedes it. Nothing prior enters.

**Statement.** Is there a set larger than the integers and smaller than the real numbers?

**The field's account of its hardness.**

**Accounts.** The statement has no answer: the hypothesis is consistent with the standard axioms and so is its negation. It is independent, and independence is the answer.

Independence from one axiom set is not the end: the axioms are incomplete, and the question is which further axioms are true. Programmes on inner models and on forcing axioms aim at settling it, and some favour a definite value.

**Specifications.** The sets form a determinate universe about which questions have answers — local. The standard axioms describe that universe — local.

**Specifications withdrawn.**

**Common requirement.** That there is a fact about the universe of sets, or a demonstration that there is not.

**Conserving relation.** A determination of the hypothesis, standing on the axioms.

**Conserving reach.** All models of the standard axioms.

**Reach.** The statement quantifies over whether a further axiom is true of the sets, beyond being consistent with them. Consequences of chosen axiom systems are what is measured. The two are not the same.

**Unreachability.** Independence is proved twice over, and large-cardinal axioms provably do not settle it, forcing preserving them. The determination is not reached over the models by the standard axioms or by the large-cardinal extensions.

**Persistence.** A criterion for the truth of a further axiom, beyond its consistency.

**Addresses.** Keyed property by property.

*The report.*

**Approaches.** Proving it from the axioms — excluded, closed by proof. Large cardinal axioms — settle many questions, provably not this one, since forcing preserves them. Forcing axioms — imply a definite value for the continuum, the choice of forcing axiom itself unsettled. Inner model programme — aims at a canonical model for large cardinals, ongoing, technically formidable, its architects describing it as incomplete. Further criterion programmes — more than one criterion proposed and revised, including reversals of direction, the field not regarding the matter as decided.

**Narrowing recorded.** Forcing axioms narrow the reach: a definite value stands under a chosen forcing axiom rather than over the models.

**Progress.** The two independence results. The proof that large-cardinal axioms do not settle it. The values implied under specific forcing axioms.

**Own record.**

## 53 Gettier

**Conditions prior.** The statement holds across readings. The terms have determinate reference. The accounts hold their truth value while tested. A procedure repeated returns the same outcome independently of what precedes it. Nothing prior enters.

**Statement.** Knowledge was defined as justified true belief. Cases exist in which a belief is justified, true, and by accident. What is missing?

**The field's account of its hardness.**

**Accounts.** A fourth condition: candidate additions — no false lemmas, defeasibility, causal connection, reliability, safety, sensitivity — each handle the original cases, and the project is to find the right one.

No fourth condition works: each proposal has generated counterexamples within a few years, for fifty years, and the analysis project itself is argued to be misconceived, knowledge being unanalysable.

**Specifications.** Knowledge decomposes into individually necessary and jointly sufficient conditions — local. Intuitions about cases adjudicate proposals — local.

**Specifications withdrawn.**

**Common requirement.** That cases sort into knowledge and non-knowledge, and that a correct analysis reproduces the sorting.

**Conserving relation.** An analysis reproducing the sorting, the reproduction standing over cases not yet constructed.

**Conserving reach.** The concept's extension over all possible cases.

**Reach.** The statement quantifies over the concept's extension over all possible cases. Cases constructed and judged, one at a time, are what is measured. The two are not the same.

**Unreachability.** Each added condition has been met by a counterexample, the pattern documented and consistent over decades. The reproduction is not reached over all possible cases by analyses adjudicated against cases constructed one at a time.

**Persistence.** An analysis surviving the construction of further cases, or agreement that the concept admits none.

**Addresses.** Keyed property by property.

*The report.*

**Approaches.** Adding a condition — each met by a counterexample, the pattern documented and consistent over decades. Knowledge first — knowledge taken as primitive and the others analysed from it, reversing the direction and contested, supplying no analysis. Contextualism — standards varying with context, accounting for variation, the original cases persisting within a fixed context. Experimental philosophy — reports variation in case judgments across groups, bearing on the adjudication method rather than the analysis. Denying the phenomenon — few take it, the cases widely found compelling.

**Narrowing recorded.** Contextualism narrows the reach: the sorting stands within a context rather than over all cases. Knowledge first weakens the relation: knowledge stands primitive and the other terms are analysed from it, in place of an analysis of knowledge.

**Progress.** The counterexample record across five decades of proposals. The measured variation in case judgments across groups.

**Own record.**

## 54 The Münchhausen trilemma

**Conditions prior.** The statement holds across readings. The terms have determinate reference. The accounts hold their truth value while tested. A procedure repeated returns the same outcome independently of what precedes it. Nothing prior enters.

**Statement.** Any claim can be asked for its grounds. The grounds can be asked in turn. Where does this end?

**The field's account of its hardness.**

**Accounts.** It ends at basic beliefs: some are self-justifying or justified non-inferentially, and the regress stops there.

It does not end: every proposed foundation either takes its warrant from something further, which continues the regress, or from itself, which is circular, or from stipulation, which is arbitrary. Circularity is accepted in one form, regress in another.

**Specifications.** Justification is a relation between claims — local. A claim's warrant derives from its relation to other claims or from itself — local.

**Specifications withdrawn.**

**Common requirement.** That justification terminates, circles, or continues, and that these three exhaust the options.

**Conserving relation.** A termination of the regress, standing without warrant taken from further, from itself, or from stipulation.

**Conserving reach.** The structure of justification in general.

**Reach.** The statement quantifies over the structure of justification in general. Particular justifications, each already inside the structure, are what is measured. The two are not the same.

**Unreachability.** Each candidate class of basic belief has been argued to require justification of its own, and each horn is developed and carries a standing objection. The termination is not reached over the structure by particular justifications taken from inside it.

**Persistence.** A class of basic beliefs surviving the demand for its own justification, or agreement on which horn is to be accepted.

**Addresses.** Keyed property by property.

*The report.*

**Approaches.** Foundationalism — identifies basic beliefs, each candidate class argued to require justification of its own. Coherentism — accepts mutual support without foundations, facing the isolation objection, that a coherent set may be unconnected to anything outside it. Infinitism — accepts infinite chains, facing finite-mind objections, its proponents arguing these confuse having a chain with traversing one. Contextualism — grounds stopping where the context does not question them, accounting for practice, the trilemma recurring when the context is questioned. Rejecting the demand — holds the regress question malformed, its critics replying that the demand arises from ordinary practice.

**Narrowing recorded.** Contextualism narrows the reach: the termination stands within a context rather than over the structure in general.

**Progress.** The taxonomy of the three horns. The standing objection against each. The documented failure of each candidate class of basic belief.

**Own record.**

## 55 The liar

**Conditions prior.** The statement holds across readings. The terms have determinate reference. The accounts hold their truth value while tested. A procedure repeated returns the same outcome independently of what precedes it. Nothing prior enters.

**Statement.** "This sentence is false." If it is true it is false, and if it is false it is true. What is wrong?

**The field's account of its hardness.**

**Accounts.** The sentence is not well formed for truth evaluation: a consistent truth predicate for a language cannot be defined within that language, and the hierarchy of metalanguages removes the sentence from the domain.

Natural language has one level and speakers use "true" of their own sentences constantly: hierarchies are stipulated rather than found, and revenge sentences reconstruct the paradox at every level of every proposed solution.

**Specifications.** Sentences bear truth values — universal under semantics. A truth predicate applies to sentences of the language it occurs in — local.

**Specifications withdrawn.**

**Common requirement.** That the sentence either has a truth value or is shown to fall outside the domain.

**Conserving relation.** A treatment removing the paradox, the removal standing against sentences constructed within the treatment.

**Conserving reach.** The truth predicate as used, at every level of every treatment.

**Reach.** The statement quantifies over the truth predicate as used. Formal languages, each with a stipulated structure, are what is measured. The two are not the same.

**Unreachability.** A consistent truth predicate for a language is proved undefinable within that language, and every family of solution has been met by a revenge sentence, which its own authors record. The removal is not preserved over the treatments by any treatment of a stipulated language.

**Persistence.** A treatment against which no revenge sentence is constructible, or agreement that the hierarchy's cost is to be accepted.

**Addresses.** Keyed property by property.

*The report.*

**Approaches.** The hierarchy — proved and consistent, at the cost of denying that a language contains its own truth predicate. Fixed points — assign the liar no truth value, consistently, a revenge sentence reconstructing the problem, which its author states. Paraconsistent treatments — accept the sentence as both true and false and restrict inference to prevent explosion, consistent as a system, their critics holding the cost is the inference rules everyone uses. Contextual treatments — the truth predicate shifting with context of evaluation, accounting for the reasoning and facing their own revenge cases. Denying truth-bearing — each form met by a strengthened liar.

**Narrowing recorded.** The hierarchy narrows the reach: a consistent truth predicate stands for an object language, defined in a metalanguage, rather than for the predicate as used. Paraconsistent treatments weaken the relation: inference is restricted so that both truth values stand without explosion.

**Progress.** The undefinability theorem. The consistent fixed-point constructions. The graph of which revenge sentence meets which treatment.

**Own record.**

## 56 Yablo's paradox

**Conditions prior.** The statement holds across readings. The terms have determinate reference. The accounts hold their truth value while tested. A procedure repeated returns the same outcome independently of what precedes it. Nothing prior enters.

**Statement.** An infinite list of sentences, each saying only that all the ones after it are untrue. No sentence refers to itself. The paradox arises anyway. What produces it?

**The field's account of its hardness.**

**Accounts.** Self-reference is not required: the construction is explicitly free of it, so whatever produces liar-type paradox is more general than circularity, quantification over an unbounded tail being enough.

Circularity is present in a hidden form: the construction requires a fixed point and is therefore circular at the level of the property defined.

**Specifications.** Paradox arises from a sentence's reference structure — local. Self-reference is identifiable in a construction — local.

**Specifications withdrawn.**

**Common requirement.** That there is a fact about whether the construction is circular.

**Conserving relation.** A determination of what produces paradox, standing over constructions generally.

**Conserving reach.** All constructions producing paradox.

**Reach.** The statement quantifies over what produces paradox in general. Particular constructions, analysed one at a time, are what is measured. The two are not the same.

**Unreachability.** Whether the construction is circular is where the field's analyses disagree on one object, the graph-theoretic criteria classifying both liar and this construction while whether the graph property is circularity is the same dispute in new terms. The determination is not reached over constructions by analyses of particular ones.

**Persistence.** A criterion of circularity the analyses agree on, applied to the construction.

**Addresses.** Keyed property by property.

*The report.*

**Approaches.** Inspecting for self-reference — no sentence refers to itself, by construction, which is agreed. Fixed-point analysis — locates circularity in the defining property, contested and not resolved. Graph-theoretic analysis — characterises paradoxicality by properties of the reference graph, giving a criterion that classifies both liar and this construction, whether the graph property is circularity being the same dispute in new terms. Arithmetical versions — constructed, showing the phenomenon in a formal setting.

**Narrowing recorded.** Graph-theoretic analysis weakens the relation: paradoxicality stands characterised by a graph property in place of circularity determined.

**Progress.** The construction. Its arithmetisation. The graph-theoretic criteria. The circularity question, documented as open.

**Own record.**

## 57 Newcomb's problem

**Conditions prior.** The statement holds across readings. The terms have determinate reference. The accounts hold their truth value while tested. A procedure repeated returns the same outcome independently of what precedes it. Nothing prior enters.

**Statement.** A reliable predictor has already filled a box based on a prediction of your choice. Take one box or two?

**The field's account of its hardness.**

**Accounts.** Take one: those who take one box receive far more, reliably, and a decision theory whose recommendation is refuted by the outcome distribution is not a good decision theory.

Take two: the contents are already fixed and the choice does not change them, and taking both dominates — whatever is in the opaque box, more is got by taking both.

**Specifications.** The predictor is reliable — local. The contents are fixed before the choice — local. Both principles — maximise expected utility given evidence, and dominance — are correct — local.

**Specifications withdrawn.**

**Common requirement.** That the two principles are both valid and that one applies here.

**Conserving relation.** A decision principle governing where the two conflict, the governing standing across the cases.

**Conserving reach.** All cases the principles are applied to.

**Reach.** The statement quantifies over which decision principle governs when they conflict. Cases, judged by the principles in dispute, are what is measured. The two are not the same.

**Unreachability.** Each decision theory handles the cases the other fails, which is two instruments returning opposite verdicts on the same cases. The governing is not preserved across the cases by either principle.

**Persistence.** A principle handling the cases both theories are pressed with, or a criterion for which principle applies where.

**Addresses.** Keyed property by property.

*The report.*

**Approaches.** Evidential decision theory — recommends one box, facing the smoking lesion case, where it gives an answer widely judged wrong. Causal decision theory — recommends two, facing this case, and facing cases where it recommends against outcomes it can foresee. Functional and updateless variants — developed to handle both, each with its own cases, none settled. Denying the setup — argues perfect prediction is incompatible with free choice, the problem restated with a merely reliable predictor. Empirical study — reports what people choose, split, which is not the statement.

**Narrowing recorded.** Functional and updateless variants weaken the relation: a principle stands for a stated class of cases rather than governing where the two conflict.

**Progress.** The case map of which theory fails which case. The constructed variants and the cases each meets.

**Own record.**

## 58 Zeno's dichotomy

**Conditions prior.** The statement holds across readings. The terms have determinate reference. The accounts hold their truth value while tested. A procedure repeated returns the same outcome independently of what precedes it. Nothing prior enters.

**Statement.** To cross a distance one must first cross half, and half of the remainder, without end. How is the crossing completed?

**The field's account of its hardness.**

**Accounts.** The series converges: the infinite sum of intervals is finite, and the mathematics of limits dissolves the puzzle entirely.

Convergence shows the sum is finite, not how infinitely many tasks are completed: the supertask question is distinct from the arithmetic and has its own literature, including constructions where a supertask yields a determinate but paradoxical final state.

**Specifications.** A distance is composed of parts — local. Traversal is composed of traversals of parts — local.

**Specifications withdrawn.**

**Common requirement.** That the traversal decomposes into the stated sequence.

**Conserving relation.** The final state of an infinite sequence of tasks, determined by the sequence.

**Conserving reach.** All supertasks the decomposition admits.

**Reach.** The statement quantifies over the completion of an infinite sequence of tasks. The arithmetic of the sequence, and physical measurements at finite resolution, are what is measured. The two are not the same.

**Unreachability.** A supertask can leave the final state undetermined by the sequence, which the lamp constructions establish. The determination is not preserved over the supertasks by the arithmetic of the sequence.

**Persistence.** A determination of the final state from the sequence for supertasks generally, or agreement that the arithmetic is what the question asks.

**Addresses.** Keyed property by property.

*The report.*

**Approaches.** Convergence — settles the arithmetic completely, the supertask literature holding this to be a distinct question. Lamp constructions and successors — show that a supertask can leave the final state undetermined by the sequence, establishing that the arithmetic does not always fix the end state. Discrete space or time — removes the decomposition, not established, and facing its own difficulties in relativistic settings. Denying the decomposition — holds the motion is not composed of sub-motions, reframing, the decomposition being what the argument constructs.

**Narrowing recorded.** Convergence narrows the reach: the determination stands for the arithmetic of the sequence rather than for the completion of the tasks.

**Progress.** Convergence. The supertask results, showing final states can be underdetermined.

**Own record.**

## 59 The three-body problem

**Conditions prior.** The statement holds across readings. The terms have determinate reference. The accounts hold their truth value while tested. A procedure repeated returns the same outcome independently of what precedes it. Nothing prior enters.

**Statement.** Three bodies attract each other by gravity. Is there a formula giving their positions at any later time?

**The field's account of its hardness.**

**Accounts.** No: there are no sufficient integrals for the general problem, and the dynamics is chaotic, nearby initial conditions diverging exponentially, so no closed form of the sought kind exists.

**Specifications.** A solution means a closed-form expression in known functions — local. The initial conditions are exactly specified — local.

**Specifications withdrawn.**

**Common requirement.** That the system's future is determined by its present state, and that the question is whether that determination is expressible.

**Conserving relation.** An expression giving positions at arbitrary later times, usable for arbitrary initial data.

**Conserving reach.** All initial data, at arbitrary later times.

**Reach.** The statement quantifies over positions at arbitrary later times, for arbitrary initial data. Numerical integration at finite precision, over finite times, is what is measured. The two are not the same.

**Unreachability.** No additional algebraic integrals exist, proved; and the convergent series converges so slowly as to be useless for computation, which the literature states, answering the letter and not the use. The expression is not reached over the initial data by integrals or by the series.

**Persistence.** An expression usable at arbitrary later times, or agreement on which sense of solution the question asks.

**Addresses.** Keyed property by property.

*The report.*

**Approaches.** Search for integrals — no additional algebraic integrals, closed by proof. The convergent series — converges, and so slowly as to be useless for computation, which is stated in the literature. Numerical integration — accurate over intervals set by the Lyapunov time, after which precision requirements grow exponentially. Perturbation and stability theory — establish stability for most initial conditions in near-integrable regimes, a major positive result covering a restricted region. Restricted and special cases — solved exactly, each a measure-zero set.

**Narrowing recorded.** Stability theory narrows the reach: the result stands for most initial conditions in near-integrable regimes. Special cases narrow the reach: exact solutions stand on measure-zero sets. Numerical integration narrows the reach: accuracy stands over intervals set by the Lyapunov time.

**Progress.** The non-existence proof for additional integrals. The convergent series. The stability results in near-integrable regimes. The exact special solutions.

**Own record.**

## 60 The Fermi paradox

**Conditions prior.** The statement holds across readings. The terms have determinate reference. The accounts hold their truth value while tested. A procedure repeated returns the same outcome independently of what precedes it. Nothing prior enters.

**Statement.** The galaxy is old and large. If technological life is common, some should be evident. None is. Why?

**The field's account of its hardness.** That the paradox's premise is a search completeness the field has quantified as extremely low: the fraction of the multidimensional search volume examined to date is stated as similar to the ratio of the volume of a large hot tub or small swimming pool to that of the Earth's oceans, with the earlier estimate placing the sampled fraction between 5 × 10⁻²⁶ and 1.5 × 10⁻¹⁸ of the nine-dimensional haystack.

**Accounts.** Life or technology is rare: a step in the sequence from chemistry to detectable technology is improbable enough to make us close to alone, and the absence of evidence is what rarity looks like.

The absence is not evidence yet: the volume searched, in frequency, sky coverage, sensitivity, and duration, is a negligible fraction of the space, and quantitative accounting of the searched fraction shows it close to zero.

**Specifications.** Technological civilisations would produce detectable signatures — local. Expansion or emission over galactic timescales is the expected behaviour — local.

**Specifications withdrawn.**

**Common requirement.** That a civilisation would be detectable by the means used, so that absence bears on abundance.

**Conserving relation.** An inference from absence to abundance, standing on the fraction searched.

**Conserving reach.** The search space in frequency, sky coverage, sensitivity, and duration.

**Reach.** The statement quantifies over abundance. A small and quantified fraction of the search space is what is measured. The two are not the same.

**Unreachability.** The searched fraction is quantified and small, which both accounts accept, and every route narrows without closing. The inference is not reached over the search space by the fraction searched.

**Persistence.** A detection, or a searched fraction large enough that absence bears on abundance.

**Addresses.** Hardness and the searched fraction: Wright, Kanodia and Lubar, "How Much SETI Has Been Done? Finding Needles in the n-Dimensional Cosmic Haystack", Astronomical Journal 156:260, 2018, abstract and conclusions. Prior estimate: Tarter et al. 2010, as restated in the decadal search literature. Remaining properties: keyed at the next pass.

*The report.*

**Approaches.** Radio search — sixty years, no confirmed detection, the searched fraction quantified as a small volume of a large space, which both accounts accept. Technosignature searches — waste heat, megastructures, atmospheric markers, null so far, with sensitivity improving. Great Filter reasoning — locates the improbable step, where it lies unconstrained by any measurement, which its proponents state. Rare Earth arguments — assemble contingencies for our own case, sample size one, which the field notes. Anthropic and selection-effect arguments — show what our existence does and does not license inferring, constraining inference and supplying no abundance.

**Narrowing recorded.**

**Progress.** The quantified searched fraction. The sixty-year null record with its sensitivity bounds. The technosignature limits.

**Own record.**

## 61 Poverty of the stimulus

**Conditions prior.** The statement holds across readings. The terms have determinate reference. The accounts hold their truth value while tested. A procedure repeated returns the same outcome independently of what precedes it. Nothing prior enters.

**Statement.** Children acquire grammar rapidly from limited and imperfect input. Is grammatical knowledge substantially innate?

**The field's account of its hardness.**

**Accounts.** Yes: the input underdetermines the grammar acquired, children converge on structure-dependent rules without evidence distinguishing them from structure-independent alternatives, and formal learnability results show unconstrained learners cannot converge from positive evidence alone.

No, or much less than claimed: corpus studies find the allegedly absent evidence present at low but non-zero frequency, statistical learners trained on realistic corpora acquire the structures in question, and the learnability results assume a class of grammars and a learning criterion that are choices.

**Specifications.** There is a determinate grammar acquired — local. The input is characterisable as evidence for or against it — local. Learning is convergence on that grammar — local.

**Specifications withdrawn.**

**Common requirement.** That the acquired grammar and the available input can each be specified well enough to compare.

**Conserving relation.** A separation of what the learner brings from what the input supplies, the separation standing on the corpora and the models.

**Conserving reach.** All the structures acquired.

**Reach.** The statement quantifies over what the learner brings, as against what the input supplies. Corpora, acquisition timelines, and trained models are what is measured. The two are not the same.

**Unreachability.** The corpus frequency and modelling results are read oppositely by the two accounts, which is the instruments disagreeing on the same evidence, and the learnability results hold relative to a class and a criterion, which the literature states. The separation is not reached over the structures by corpora, timelines, or trained models.

**Persistence.** A measurement separating what the learner brings from what the input supplies, or agreement on what frequency of evidence suffices.

**Addresses.** Keyed property by property.

*The report.*

**Approaches.** Corpus frequency studies — find relevant constructions at low frequency, whether low frequency suffices being the dispute restated quantitatively. Formal learnability — a negative result, and positive results under different criteria and priors, each relative to its class and criterion, which the literature states. Computational modelling — statistical learners acquiring several target structures from realistic input, whether the architecture encodes the constraint in question being the recurring objection. Large-scale language models — acquire structure-dependence from text at large scale, the data-volume objection met with a matched-scale programme whose targeted results show generalization on the canonical phenomena at child-scale input without direct positive evidence, while remaining less data-efficient than children, with weaker generalizations; each account reads its half. Cross-linguistic and atypical acquisition studies — constrain timelines, both accounts accommodating the findings.

**Narrowing recorded.** Formal learnability narrows the reach: the negative result stands for a stated class of grammars and a stated learning criterion.

**Progress.** The measured frequencies of the relevant constructions. The learnability results under stated criteria. The matched-scale modelling results and their measured data-efficiency gap.

**Own record.**

## 62 Morphogenesis

**Conditions prior.** The statement holds across readings. The terms have determinate reference. The accounts hold their truth value while tested. A procedure repeated returns the same outcome independently of what precedes it. Nothing prior enters.

**Statement.** A fertilised egg becomes a body with parts in the right places. What determines where each part goes?

**The field's account of its hardness.**

**Accounts.** Positional information: cells read their position from concentration gradients of signalling molecules and interpret it against thresholds, gradients being measured, thresholds identified, and manipulating a gradient relocating the structure.

Self-organisation: reaction-diffusion systems generate pattern with no pre-existing coordinates, produce the observed spacing and its scaling, and account for regeneration and for pattern in tissues where no gradient is found.

**Specifications.** Pattern is specified by information distinguishable from the tissue that carries it — local. Position is a variable cells can read — local.

**Specifications withdrawn.**

**Common requirement.** That there is a determinant of pattern locatable either in a signal or in the dynamics.

**Conserving relation.** A determinant of placement located in one of the two, the location standing across the tissues.

**Conserving reach.** All tissues the question is asked of.

**Reach.** The statement quantifies over what determines placement. Concentrations, expression domains, and the effects of perturbation are what is measured, all in tissue where both processes run together. The two are not the same.

**Unreachability.** Both frameworks fit most datasets, which is the recurring finding, and hybrid models fit better than either. The location is not preserved across the tissues by measurements taken where both processes run together.

**Persistence.** A measurement in tissue separating the two processes, or agreement that both operate and the question is their proportion.

**Addresses.** Keyed property by property.

*The report.*

**Approaches.** Gradient manipulation — relocates structures, establishing gradients are instructive where tested, without excluding self-organising dynamics operating on them. Reaction-diffusion identification — candidate Turing systems identified in digit spacing, hair follicle spacing, and palatal ridges, with predicted perturbation outcomes confirmed, establishing the mechanism in those cases. Precision measurement of gradients — readout precision near physical limits in the fly embryo, constraining how much information a gradient can carry. Synthetic reconstitution — organoids generating axes and structures without maternal gradients, establishing self-organisation is sufficient in those settings. Modelling — both frameworks fitting most datasets, hybrid models fitting better than either.

**Narrowing recorded.** Reaction-diffusion identification narrows the reach: the mechanism stands established in the specific tissues identified. Synthetic reconstitution narrows the reach: sufficiency stands in the organoid settings.

**Progress.** The measured gradients and their readout precision. The identified Turing systems with confirmed perturbation outcomes. The organoid axes formed without maternal gradients.

**Own record.**

## 63 Homochirality

**Conditions prior.** The statement holds across readings. The terms have determinate reference. The accounts hold their truth value while tested. A procedure repeated returns the same outcome independently of what precedes it. Nothing prior enters.

**Statement.** Biological amino acids are almost all left-handed and sugars right-handed. Ordinary chemistry produces both equally. Why one hand?

**The field's account of its hardness.**

**Accounts.** Amplification of a small initial bias: autocatalytic reactions amplify tiny enantiomeric excesses to near-purity, demonstrated in a known reaction, and small biases are available from circularly polarised light or crystal surfaces. The initial bias may be accidental.

A systematic cause: the weak interaction is parity-violating and makes one enantiomer very slightly more stable, and this is not accidental, a universal bias accounting for why the same hand appears everywhere rather than by chance.

**Specifications.** The asymmetry originated once and was inherited — local. A mechanism either selects a hand or amplifies whatever bias is present — local.

**Specifications withdrawn.**

**Common requirement.** That the observed excess traces to a determinable origin.

**Conserving relation.** The origin of the excess determined, the determination standing on measurable chemistry.

**Conserving reach.** The initial excess, and the amplification acting on it.

**Reach.** The statement quantifies over the initial excess, and whether a bias that small is amplifiable. Present chemistry, laboratory amplification, and meteoritic samples are what is measured. The two are not the same.

**Unreachability.** Amplification is demonstrated and does not supply the initial bias; the parity-violation energy difference is calculated at order 10⁻¹⁷ in relative terms, and whether amplification can act on a bias that small over available timescales is disputed and is the crux. The determination is not reached over the initial excess by present chemistry or laboratory amplification.

**Persistence.** A demonstration that amplification acts on a bias of the calculated magnitude over available timescales, or a measurement of the initial excess.

**Addresses.** Keyed property by property.

*The report.*

**Approaches.** Autocatalytic amplification — demonstrated conclusively, establishing amplification without supplying the initial bias. Parity-violation energy difference — calculated, of order 10⁻¹⁷ in relative terms, its sufficiency being the crux. Circularly polarised light — produces measurable excesses, astronomical sources identified in star-forming regions. Meteoritic amino acids — left-handed excesses in some samples, supporting an extraterrestrial bias and relocating rather than settling the origin. Crystallisation and surface effects — produce excesses readily, without a preferred direction.

**Narrowing recorded.**

**Progress.** The demonstrated amplification. The calculated energy difference. The measured excesses from polarised light and the identified astronomical sources. The meteoritic excesses.

**Own record.**

## 64 Matter and antimatter asymmetry

**Conditions prior.** The statement holds across readings. The terms have determinate reference. The accounts hold their truth value while tested. A procedure repeated returns the same outcome independently of what precedes it. Nothing prior enters.

**Statement.** The laws produce matter and antimatter nearly symmetrically. The universe is matter. Where did the antimatter go?

**The field's account of its hardness.**

**Accounts.** Known physics is insufficient and new physics is required: the three conditions are agreed, and the measured CP violation in the Standard Model is smaller than the observed asymmetry by roughly ten orders of magnitude.

**Specifications.** The initial state was symmetric — local. The asymmetry was generated dynamically — local. The three conditions are necessary and sufficient — universal under the framework.

**Specifications withdrawn.**

**Common requirement.** That the asymmetry was produced by a mechanism operating in the early universe.

**Conserving relation.** A mechanism identified as having produced the asymmetry, the identification standing on measurement.

**Conserving reach.** Conditions at the energies and epochs the mechanism operated at.

**Reach.** The statement quantifies over conditions at energies and epochs not accessible. Collider energies, precision CP measurements, and cosmological observations of the resulting abundance are what is measured. The two are not the same.

**Unreachability.** The measured Standard Model CP violation is insufficient by orders, and every proposed mechanism narrows without closing, several sitting at scales far above collider reach, which their proponents state. The identification is not reached over the epoch by measurements at accessible energies.

**Persistence.** A mechanism distinguished from the others by measurement, or a detection at a scale one of them predicts.

**Addresses.** Keyed property by property.

*The report.*

**Approaches.** Standard Model CP violation — measured precisely in kaon, B, and D systems, established and quantitatively insufficient by many orders. Electroweak baryogenesis — requires a first-order transition, excluded in the minimal model by the measured Higgs mass, extensions remaining viable and constrained by collider and electric dipole moment searches. Leptogenesis — consistent and attractive, its scale typically far above collider reach, which its proponents state, with neutrinoless double beta decay supporting the required nature and searches null so far. Electric dipole moment searches — improving by orders of magnitude and null, excluding parameter space for several mechanisms. Direct search for baryon number violation — proton decay searches null, with lifetime limits now extremely long.

**Narrowing recorded.** Electroweak baryogenesis narrows the reach: the mechanism stands for extended Higgs sectors, the minimal model excluded by the measured mass.

**Progress.** The precisely measured CP violation and its magnitude relative to the asymmetry. The exclusion of the minimal electroweak mechanism. The dipole moment and proton decay limits.

**Own record.**

**Conditions prior.** The statement holds across readings. The terms have determinate reference. The accounts hold their truth value while tested. A procedure repeated returns the same outcome independently of what precedes it. Nothing prior enters.

It may require no explanation: the parameter is a free constant and small values are permitted, the demand for explanation resting on a measure over parameter space that is stipulated, and decades of searches having returned nothing.

*The report.*

Massless up quark — would remove the problem, excluded by lattice calculation, closed. Spontaneously broken CP mechanisms — viable, with model-building costs, and no distinguishing observation. Anthropic reasoning — argues no explanation is needed, its critics noting the parameter has little anthropic relevance, which weakens the argument here specifically. Neutron dipole moment measurement — the bound that defines the problem, improving steadily and null.

## 66 The problem of time in quantum gravity

**Conditions prior.** The statement holds across readings. The terms have determinate reference. The accounts hold their truth value while tested. A procedure repeated returns the same outcome independently of what precedes it. Nothing prior enters.

**Statement.** Quantum mechanics treats time as an external parameter. General relativity treats it as part of the dynamical geometry. What is time in a theory of both?

**The field's account of its hardness.**

**Accounts.** Time is emergent and the fundamental equation is timeless: the constraint equation contains no time parameter, and apparent time arises from correlations between subsystems, one variable serving as a clock for the rest.

A timeless fundamental theory has not been shown to recover ordinary dynamics, and the conditional-probability route has known technical problems: some programmes reintroduce a preferred time and reproduce general relativity's predictions in the tested regime.

**Specifications.** A quantum theory requires a time parameter for evolution — universal under quantum mechanics. General covariance forbids a preferred one — universal under general relativity.

**Specifications withdrawn.**

**Common requirement.** That the two treatments must be reconciled in one theory covering both regimes.

**Conserving relation.** One treatment of time covering both regimes, the covering standing where each theory is confirmed.

**Conserving reach.** Both regimes, at the scales the reconciliation concerns.

**Reach.** The statement quantifies over the structure of a theory at scales not accessible. The two theories separately, each confirmed in its own regime, are what is measured. The two are not the same.

**Unreachability.** Semiclassical emergence recovers a time parameter in the limit where both theories already work, and no experiment probes the regime, which the field states. The covering is not reached over the regimes by measurements confirming each theory separately.

**Persistence.** An observation reaching the regime, or a derivation of ordinary dynamics from a timeless theory agreed to succeed.

**Addresses.** Keyed property by property.

*The report.*

**Approaches.** The constraint equation with an internal clock — faces operator ordering, the measure, and the question of which variable is the clock. Conditional probabilities — recovers evolution relative to a clock subsystem, early objections addressed and the interpretation remaining disputed. Shape dynamics and preferred-foliation approaches — reproduce general relativity where tested, at the cost of the symmetry the other account takes as fundamental. Semiclassical emergence — recovers a time parameter in the appropriate limit, with corrections, the limit being where both theories already work. Observation — no experiment probes the regime, which the field states.

**Narrowing recorded.** Shape dynamics narrows the reach: the reproduction stands in the tested regime, at the cost of the symmetry. Semiclassical emergence narrows the reach: the recovery stands in the limit where both theories already work.

**Progress.** The constraint equation and its structure. The conditional-probability recovery relative to a clock subsystem. The reproduction of general relativity in the tested regime by preferred-foliation approaches.

**Own record.**

## 67 Chronic pain

**Conditions prior.** The statement holds across readings. The terms have determinate reference. The accounts hold their truth value while tested. A procedure repeated returns the same outcome independently of what precedes it. Nothing prior enters.

**Statement.** Pain persists in many patients after tissue damage has healed, or with no damage found. What produces it?

**The field's account of its hardness.** That the central mechanism cannot be assumed in a patient: what can be assessed clinically are its features — spreading pain, hypersensitivity, sensitivity to touch, movement, pressure, or temperature — and not the mechanism itself. The field's third mechanistic descriptor is defined by absence of evidence for the other two, patients commonly present with overlapping features of more than one, and the descriptor is recorded as applied residually to those not fully meeting the other criteria. The field states that the aetiology of that category is not yet settled.

**Accounts.** Central sensitisation: changes in the nervous system's own processing amplify and sustain signalling independently of peripheral input, the changes being measurable, and the pain real and neurally produced.

Peripheral drivers persist and are missed: small-fibre pathology, occult inflammation, and nerve injury are found in substantial fractions of patients previously classed as centrally driven, and improved detection keeps moving cases from one category to the other.

**Specifications.** Pain has a locatable driver, peripheral or central — local. Finding it determines the treatment — local.

**Specifications withdrawn.**

**Common requirement.** That the pain is produced by an identifiable process at one location or the other.

**Conserving relation.** The driver located at one site or the other, the location standing on measurement in the individual patient.

**Conserving reach.** All patients the question is asked of.

**Reach.** The statement quantifies over which process drives the persistence in a given patient. Imaging, quantitative sensory testing, and biopsy are what is measured, each returning findings compatible with both accounts in most patients. The two are not the same.

**Unreachability.** Imaging establishes central change without separating cause from consequence, which the field states, and biopsy moves a measured fraction between categories without closing. The location is not reached over the patients by imaging, sensory testing, or biopsy.

**Persistence.** A measurement separating cause from consequence in the individual patient, or agreement that both drivers operate and the question is their proportion.

**Addresses.** Hardness, and the statement that the central mechanism cannot be assumed while its features can be assessed: the terminology editorial, Brazilian Journal of Physical Therapy, 2023, misconceptions table. The descriptor's definition by absence and its residual application: the same, and the non-neuronal review, PMC12886048, introduction. Overlapping presentation and unsettled aetiology: PMC12886048. The multifactorial model and the osteoarthritis case: the central sensitisation review, Lancet Rheumatology 2021. Prognostic association with outcomes after peripherally focused intervention: the critical reflection, Frontiers in Medicine 2026. Persistence after joint replacement: the arthroplasty study record, NCT05575310. Remaining properties: keyed at the next pass.

*The report.*

**Approaches.** Peripheral blockade — relieves some patients and not others, read by both accounts as supporting theirs for those patients. Skin biopsy for small-fibre density — moves a measured fraction of patients into the peripheral category. Functional imaging — identifies central changes reliably, whether cause or consequence not settled by the imaging, which the field states. Clinical assessment of sensitization features — quantitative sensory testing and symptom-based instruments, assessing the features rather than the mechanism, which the field distinguishes explicitly. The third mechanistic descriptor — introduced as a pain phenotype alongside the nociceptive and neuropathic, defined by absence of clear evidence for either, and applied residually where the other criteria are not fully met. The multifactorial model — peripheral drivers and central mechanisms held to combine in the same condition, with osteoarthritis recorded as the worked case, and the field stating that the presence of central features should not stop the search for peripheral dysfunction. Prognostic use — sensitization features associated with poorer outcomes and greater risk of persistent pain after peripherally focused interventions, used to direct referral rather than to identify the driver. Persistence after structural removal — pain continuing after joint replacement where the implicated structures are no longer present, recorded as evidence for a central contribution. Non-neuronal mechanisms — glial and immune contributions to sensitization at both sites, a distinct mechanistic route. Psychological and multidisciplinary treatment — improves outcomes substantially without identifying a driver. Channel-targeted and growth-factor drugs — efficacy in specific conditions, defining subgroups rather than settling the general question.

**Narrowing recorded.** Channel-targeted drugs narrow the reach: efficacy stands for specific conditions, defining subgroups. Biopsy narrows the reach: the peripheral location stands established for the measured fraction.

**Progress.** The measurable central changes. The measured fraction reclassified by biopsy. The outcome improvements from multidisciplinary treatment. The subgroup efficacy results.

**Own record.**

## 68 The axiom of choice

**Conditions prior.** The statement holds across readings. The terms have determinate reference. The accounts hold their truth value while tested. A procedure repeated returns the same outcome independently of what precedes it. Nothing prior enters.

**Statement.** Given infinitely many non-empty sets, can one always select an element from each?

**The field's account of its hardness.**

**Accounts.** Yes, and the axiom is indispensable: without it standard results fail — every vector space having a basis, every field an algebraic closure, arbitrary products of compact spaces compact, countable unions of countable sets countable — and mathematics as practised uses it constantly.

Its consequences include results widely regarded as unacceptable: a solid ball decomposes into finitely many pieces reassembling into two balls of the same size. The pieces are non-measurable, so no contradiction follows, and constructive mathematics proceeds without the axiom at known cost.

**Specifications.** A selection may be asserted without a rule producing it — local. Sets exist independently of any construction of them — local.

**Specifications withdrawn.**

**Common requirement.** That there is a fact about which axioms hold of the sets.

**Conserving relation.** A determination of whether the axiom holds of the sets, standing on the other axioms.

**Conserving reach.** All models of the other axioms.

**Reach.** The statement quantifies over whether a selection may be asserted where no rule produces it. Consequences of chosen axiom systems are what is measured. The two are not the same.

**Unreachability.** Independence is proved from both directions; and the dispute reduces to whether non-measurable sets are acceptable, which is the original dispute. The determination is not reached over the models by consequences of chosen systems.

**Persistence.** A criterion for the truth of the axiom beyond its consistency, or agreement on whether non-measurable sets are acceptable.

**Addresses.** Keyed property by property.

*The report.*

**Approaches.** Proving or refuting from the other axioms — consistency and independence both proved, closed by proof. Weaker choice principles — countable choice, dependent choice, the ultrafilter lemma, each sufficing for part of the practice and not all, the hierarchy mapped precisely.
