Determinacy axioms — contradict full choice, imply all sets of reals are measurable, and remove the decomposition, consistent relative to large cardinals, the conflict with choice being the trade. Constructive mathematics — proceeds without the axiom, the obtainable results precisely characterised, a known and exact cost. Locating the fault in measurability — the decomposition using non-measurable sets, whose existence follows from the axiom, the dispute becoming whether such sets are acceptable.

**Narrowing recorded.** Weaker choice principles narrow the reach: each stands for the part of the practice it suffices for, the hierarchy mapped precisely. Constructive mathematics narrows the reach: the obtainable results stand characterised without the axiom.

**Progress.** The two independence results. The mapped hierarchy of weaker principles. The characterised constructive results. The determinacy alternative and its stated trade.

**Own record.**

## 69 Skolem's paradox

**Conditions prior.** The statement holds across readings. The terms have determinate reference. The accounts hold their truth value while tested. A procedure repeated returns the same outcome independently of what precedes it. Nothing prior enters.

**Statement.** Set theory proves uncountable sets exist, and has a countable model. How can a countable model contain uncountable sets?

**The field's account of its hardness.**

**Accounts.** There is no paradox: uncountability is a statement about the absence of a bijection, and the required bijection exists outside the model but not inside it, countability being relative to a model.

The relativity is the difficulty, not its removal: if "uncountable" holds relative to a model, then either there is an absolute notion the models approximate, or set-theoretic language does not determine its own subject matter.

**Specifications.** First-order axioms characterise the structures they are about — local. Cardinality is a property of a set — local.

**Specifications withdrawn.**

**Common requirement.** That the axioms are about something, and the question is whether they determine it.

**Conserving relation.** A determination of the subject matter by the axioms, standing across the models.

**Conserving reach.** All models satisfying the axioms.

**Reach.** The statement quantifies over whether the axioms fix their subject matter. Models satisfying the axioms, of which there are many non-isomorphic ones, are what is measured. The two are not the same.

**Unreachability.** Relativisation removes the contradiction and both accounts accept it, the disagreement being about what follows; and second-order categoricity presupposes the set theory in question. The determination is not reached over the models by the models themselves.

**Persistence.** A characterisation of the subject matter not presupposing it, or agreement on what follows from the relativity.

**Addresses.** Keyed property by property.

*The report.*

**Approaches.** Relativisation — removes the contradiction completely, accepted by both accounts, the disagreement being about what follows. Second-order logic — categorical characterisations becoming available, its semantics presupposing the set theory in question, which is the standing objection. Categoricity results — full second-order set theory quasi-categorical, given a background notion of set, the same objection at one remove. Structuralism — holds that only structure is determined and nothing further is missing, whether it answers the determinacy question disputed. The relativity conclusion — that set-theoretic notions are relative, held by a minority and not refuted.

**Narrowing recorded.** Second-order categoricity narrows the reach: the characterisation stands given a background notion of set.

**Progress.** The relativisation. The categoricity results and their stated presupposition. The map of non-isomorphic models.

**Own record.**

## 70 Grue

**Conditions prior.** The statement holds across readings. The terms have determinate reference. The accounts hold their truth value while tested. A procedure repeated returns the same outcome independently of what precedes it. Nothing prior enters.

**Statement.** Evidence that all observed emeralds are green equally supports the prediction that they are grue — green if observed before a time, blue if after. Why does one projection succeed and not the other?

**The field's account of its hardness.**

**Accounts.** Predicates differ in projectibility, and entrenchment settles which: predicates that have figured in successful past inductions are entrenched, and entrenchment is a matter of record.

Entrenchment describes practice without justifying it: the grue predicate is definable from green and a time, and green is equally definable from grue and bleen, so the asymmetry is not in the logic, and every proposed non-circular criterion of naturalness has been contested.

**Specifications.** Evidence supports a hypothesis by a relation statable in logical terms — universal under confirmation theory. The vocabulary is given — local.

**Specifications withdrawn.**

**Common requirement.** That there is a fact about which predicates support projection.

**Conserving relation.** A criterion of projectibility, standing without presupposing the vocabulary it selects.

**Conserving reach.** All predicates the projection is asked of.

**Reach.** The statement quantifies over which vocabulary the world is described in. Observations, describable in either vocabulary, are what is measured. The two are not the same.

**Unreachability.** The interdefinability makes the asymmetry symmetric in the logic; entrenchment describes practice; and naturalness rankings and priors encode the asymmetry rather than deriving it, which their proponents accept. The criterion is not reached over the predicates by observations describable in either vocabulary.

**Persistence.** A criterion of projectibility not presupposing the vocabulary it selects.

**Addresses.** Keyed property by property.

*The report.*

**Approaches.** Entrenchment — describes practice, its status as justification being the dispute. Natural kinds and natural properties — a naturalness ranking supplying the asymmetry, at the cost of a primitive the account does not derive. Simplicity of definition — the grue predicate requiring a time reference, the interdefinability showing this is symmetric, which the construction is built to show. Causal or nomic accounts — project only predicates in laws, laws being stated in projectible predicates, which is the circle. Bayesian priors — assign lower prior to grue-like hypotheses, the prior encoding the asymmetry rather than deriving it, which its proponents accept.

**Narrowing recorded.** Naturalness rankings weaken the relation: the asymmetry stands supplied by a primitive rather than derived. Bayesian priors weaken the relation: the asymmetry stands encoded in a prior rather than derived.

**Progress.** The interdefinability result. The entrenchment record. The mapped costs of each proposed criterion.

**Own record.**

## 71 The ravens

**Conditions prior.** The statement holds across readings. The terms have determinate reference. The accounts hold their truth value while tested. A procedure repeated returns the same outcome independently of what precedes it. Nothing prior enters.

**Statement.** "All ravens are black" is logically equivalent to "all non-black things are non-ravens." Does observing a white shoe confirm that all ravens are black?

**The field's account of its hardness.**

**Accounts.** Yes, by a negligible amount: the confirmation is real and tiny, proportional to the relative sizes of the classes, and the appearance of absurdity is a failure of intuition about small quantities.

The calculation depends on assumptions about the sampling and the class sizes that are supplied rather than derived, and reverses under different but equally reasonable assumptions: the relation between a generalisation and its instances is not settled by the calculation.

**Specifications.** Confirmation is a relation between statements, invariant under logical equivalence — universal under confirmation theory. Instances confirm generalisations — universal under confirmation theory.

**Specifications withdrawn.**

**Common requirement.** That there is a confirmation relation with determinate values.

**Conserving relation.** A confirmation relation with determinate values, standing independently of assumptions supplied.

**Conserving reach.** The confirmation relation in general.

**Reach.** The statement quantifies over the confirmation relation in general. Particular calculations under particular assumptions are what is measured. The two are not the same.

**Unreachability.** The calculation reverses under equally reasonable assumptions, and whether the shoe was drawn from non-black things or from shoes changes the result, which establishes that the relation depends on how evidence is gathered, not stated in the original statement. The determinacy is not reached over the relation by calculations under supplied assumptions.

**Persistence.** A confirmation relation whose values do not depend on assumptions supplied from outside the statement.

**Addresses.** Keyed property by property.

*The report.*

**Approaches.** Bayesian quantification — yields tiny confirmation under standard assumptions, the assumptions being the disputed point, reversals exhibited in the literature. Denying equivalence-invariance — blocks the paradox and gives up a condition most accounts regard as basic. Sampling considerations — establish that the relation depends on how evidence is gathered, which is not in the original statement. Selective confirmation — restricts confirmation to instances of the subject class, ad hoc by its critics' account, and blocks the paradox. Rejecting the confirmation-relation framework — taken by some, the alternative accounts carrying their own open problems.

**Narrowing recorded.** Selective confirmation narrows the reach: confirmation stands for instances of the subject class. Denying equivalence-invariance weakens the relation: a condition most accounts regard as basic is given up.

**Progress.** The quantified confirmation under standard assumptions. The exhibited reversals. The demonstrated dependence on sampling.

**Own record.**

## 72 Personal identity over time

**Conditions prior.** The statement holds across readings. The terms have determinate reference. The accounts hold their truth value while tested. A procedure repeated returns the same outcome independently of what precedes it. Nothing prior enters.

**Statement.** A thing has all its parts replaced gradually. Is it the same thing? For a person, what makes someone the same person as the child they were?

**The field's account of its hardness.**

**Accounts.** Psychological continuity: overlapping chains of memory, intention, and character constitute identity, the account matching judgments about ordinary cases and about imagined transfers.

Biological continuity: a person is an organism, and the organism persists through psychological discontinuity — dreamless sleep, coma, early infancy — where the psychological account gives the wrong result or none.

**Specifications.** There is a determinate fact about whether a later thing is the earlier one — local. A criterion states it — local.

**Specifications withdrawn.**

**Common requirement.** That identity over time is determinate and criterion-governed.

**Conserving relation.** A criterion of identity over time, the criterion standing across the cases.

**Conserving reach.** All cases, the fission cases included.

**Reach.** The statement quantifies over a relation across time. States at times, and the relations between them, are what is measured, all compatible with both criteria. The two are not the same.

**Unreachability.** A person divided into two psychologically continuous successors defeats simple versions of both criteria, which both accounts accept as decisive. The criterion is not preserved across the cases by either account in its simple form.

**Persistence.** A criterion surviving the fission cases, or agreement that identity is not what the question asks about.

**Addresses.** Keyed property by property.

*The report.*

**Approaches.** Fission cases — both criteria giving unacceptable results, the case agreed decisive against simple versions. The what-matters response — identity not being what matters, continuity admitting of degree, widely influential and widely rejected as changing the question. Four-dimensionalism — persons extended in time and fission being two overlapping persons, consistent, at the cost of a metaphysics the other account rejects. Animalism — solves fission by the organism, facing transplant intuitions, which its proponents accept and bite. Denying determinacy — some cases having no answer, its critics holding that first-person survival does not admit of indeterminacy.

**Narrowing recorded.** The what-matters response weakens the relation: continuity admitting of degree stands in place of a determinate criterion of identity. Denying determinacy narrows the reach: the criterion stands for the cases admitting an answer.

**Progress.** The fission result against simple versions of both criteria. The mapped costs of each response.

**Own record.**

## 73 Path dependence and lock-in

**Conditions prior.** The statement holds across readings. The terms have determinate reference. The accounts hold their truth value while tested. A procedure repeated returns the same outcome independently of what precedes it. Nothing prior enters.

**Statement.** Some technologies and institutions persist though better alternatives exist. Is the persistence inefficiency, or evidence that the alternative is not better?

**The field's account of its hardness.**

**Accounts.** Lock-in is real and inefficient: increasing returns, network effects, and switching costs make an early advantage self-reinforcing, the outcome needing to bear no relation to merit, and formal models producing it reliably.

Claimed cases dissolve on inspection: re-examination of the standard examples found the alleged superior alternatives not superior on the relevant margins, and no case of remediable inefficiency demonstrated.

**Specifications.** The alternatives can be compared on a standard independent of what was adopted — local. Adoption reflects or fails to reflect that standard — local.

**Specifications withdrawn.**

**Common requirement.** That there is a fact about which alternative is better, statable independently of which was adopted.

**Conserving relation.** A comparison of adopted and unadopted alternatives, standing at the scale the adopted one reached.

**Conserving reach.** All cases the comparison is asked of.

**Reach.** The statement quantifies over a counterfactual: how the unadopted alternative would have performed at scale. The adopted path, and small-scale or laboratory comparisons, are what is measured. The two are not the same.

**Unreachability.** The counterfactual performance at scale is unobservable by construction, and the historical cases were re-examined and the re-examinations contested in turn, which is the field's instruments disagreeing on the same episodes. The comparison is not reached over the cases by the adopted path or by small-scale comparison.

**Persistence.** A case of remediable inefficiency demonstrated at scale, or agreement on a standard of comparison independent of adoption.

**Addresses.** Keyed property by property.

*The report.*

**Approaches.** Formal modelling — produces lock-in under stated conditions, whether the conditions obtain being the empirical question. Historical case studies — the standard cases re-examined and contested, the re-examinations contested in turn, no case agreed. Laboratory experiments — produce lock-in reliably under controlled network effects, transfer to markets with entry and side payments disputed. Looking for remediable inefficiency — requires the counterfactual performance at scale, unobservable by construction. Ex post comparison after a switch — comparison available where switches occur, selection into switching being the confounding, which the field notes.

**Narrowing recorded.** Laboratory experiments narrow the reach: lock-in stands established under controlled network effects. Ex post comparison narrows the reach: the comparison stands where switches occurred.

**Progress.** The formal models and their stated conditions. The laboratory results under controlled network effects. The contested re-examination record.

**Own record.**

## 74 The hermeneutic circle

**Conditions prior.** The statement holds across readings. The terms have determinate reference. The accounts hold their truth value while tested. A procedure repeated returns the same outcome independently of what precedes it. Nothing prior enters.

**Statement.** Understanding a part of a text requires understanding the whole; understanding the whole requires understanding the parts. How does interpretation begin?

**The field's account of its hardness.**

**Accounts.** The circle is productive rather than vicious: interpretation proceeds by provisional understanding of the whole, revised as parts are read, and revised again — a spiral rather than a circle, and it is how understanding works.

Naming it productive does not remove the problem of validation: if every reading is revisable in the light of the whole and the whole in the light of readings, no reading is ever adjudicated, and the criterion distinguishing better interpretation from worse is what the circle withholds.

**Specifications.** A text has a determinate meaning — local. Interpretation approaches it — local. Parts and whole are distinguishable — local.

**Specifications withdrawn.**

**Common requirement.** That there is something the interpretation is of, against which readings can be better or worse.

**Conserving relation.** A criterion for the correctness of a reading, standing outside the readings it adjudicates.

**Conserving reach.** All readings the criterion is asked of.

**Reach.** The statement quantifies over a criterion for the correctness of a reading. Readings, each already inside the circle, are what is measured. The two are not the same.

**Unreachability.** Intention is reconstructed by interpretation and context is a text requiring interpretation, so each proposed criterion is itself inside the circle; and community-of-interpreters accounts give up the independent criterion, which their proponents accept. The criterion is not reached over the readings by readings taken from inside.

**Persistence.** A criterion standing outside the readings it adjudicates, or agreement that no independent criterion is owed.

**Addresses.** Keyed property by property.

*The report.*

**Approaches.** Authorial intention as the criterion — faces the difficulty that intention is itself reconstructed by interpretation. Historical context as the criterion — context being a text requiring interpretation, at one remove. Community of interpreters — validity being what the community accepts, accounting for practice and giving up the independent criterion, which its proponents accept. Philological method — establishes what the words meant at the time, with real results, underdetermining the readings in dispute, which are usually about significance rather than lexis. Rejecting the whole-part framing — reframes, the dependence reappearing at whatever units are chosen.

**Narrowing recorded.** Philological method narrows the reach: the criterion stands for questions of lexis rather than for the readings in dispute. Community-of-interpreters accounts weaken the relation: validity stands as community acceptance rather than as an independent criterion.

**Progress.** The philological results. The mapped position of each proposed criterion relative to the circle.

**Own record.**

## 75 Hard cases in law

**Conditions prior.** The statement holds across readings. The terms have determinate reference. The accounts hold their truth value while tested. A procedure repeated returns the same outcome independently of what precedes it. Nothing prior enters.

**Statement.** Some legal cases are not settled by the applicable rules. Does the law contain an answer, or does the judge make one?

**The field's account of its hardness.**

**Accounts.** The law contains an answer: beyond rules there are principles with weight, and the correct answer is the one fitting and justifying the legal materials as a whole, judges seeking that fit rather than exercising discretion.

Where rules run out the judge exercises discretion and makes new law: legal materials are open-textured, they underdetermine outcomes in exactly the hard cases, and disagreement among competent judges over the same materials is the evidence.

**Specifications.** The legal materials either determine an outcome or do not — local. A judge's task is to apply the law — local.

**Specifications withdrawn.**

**Common requirement.** That there is a fact about whether the materials determine the case.

**Conserving relation.** A determination of whether the materials fix the outcome, standing separately from the outcome reached.

**Conserving reach.** All hard cases.

**Reach.** The statement quantifies over whether the materials determine an outcome. Decided cases, in which an outcome was reached either way, are what is measured. The two are not the same.

**Unreachability.** Every hard case gets an outcome, which both accounts predict and read oppositely, and judges write as though finding rather than making, which both accounts accept and read differently. The determination is not separated from the outcome by decided cases.

**Persistence.** A measurement separating whether the materials fixed the outcome from the fact that an outcome was reached.

**Addresses.** Keyed property by property.

*The report.*

**Approaches.** Pointing to the decided outcome — every hard case getting an outcome, which both accounts predict and read oppositely. Analysing judicial reasoning — judges writing as though finding rather than making, both accounts accepting the practice and reading it differently. Empirical study of judicial behaviour — ideology predicting outcomes in contested cases with measurable effect, constraining the finding account without settling whether an answer existed. Inclusive positivism — admits moral criteria into the rule of recognition where a legal system so provides, narrowing the dispute and leaving the determinacy question. Critical legal studies — holds indeterminacy pervasive, its critics noting most cases are not litigated because the answer is clear, which is measurable and true.

**Narrowing recorded.** Inclusive positivism narrows the reach: the account stands for systems whose rule of recognition admits moral criteria.

**Progress.** The measured effect of ideology on contested outcomes. The litigation rates bearing on how many cases are clear.

**Own record.**

## 76 Natural law and legal positivism

**Conditions prior.** The statement holds across readings. The terms have determinate reference. The accounts hold their truth value while tested. A procedure repeated returns the same outcome independently of what precedes it. Nothing prior enters.

**Statement.** Is an unjust rule, properly enacted, law?

**The field's account of its hardness.**

**Accounts.** Yes: law and morality are separate questions, what the law is being settled by social facts about enactment and acceptance, whether it is just being a further question, and keeping them separate being what makes criticism of law possible.

Not entirely: a rule of sufficient injustice fails as law, and legal systems have an internal morality without which they do not function as legal systems at all, the post-war tribunals and the treatment of enacted atrocity standing as the cases.

**Specifications.** There is a fact about what the law is, determined by criteria — local. The criteria are either exhausted by social facts or include moral ones — local.

**Specifications withdrawn.**

**Common requirement.** That "law" has a determinate extension.

**Conserving relation.** Criteria of legal validity sorting rules into law and not-law, the sorting standing across the cases.

**Conserving reach.** All rules the question is asked of, the enacted atrocities included.

**Reach.** The statement quantifies over the criteria of legal validity. Legal practice, in which officials apply criteria they may state or not, is what is measured. The two are not the same.

**Unreachability.** The grudge informer exchange produced two coherent treatments of one set of events reaching different characterisations, and comparative study documents both patterns extensively, supporting both readings. The sorting is not preserved across the cases by legal practice.

**Persistence.** A case sorting differently under the two accounts and agreed to have been sorted correctly by one.

**Addresses.** Keyed property by property.

*The report.*

**Approaches.** The grudge informer exchange — each account producing a coherent treatment and reaching a different characterisation of the same events, documented and unresolved. Rule of recognition — locates validity in official practice, whether the practice can incorporate moral criteria being the inclusive-positivism debate, ongoing. Internal morality of law — the eight desiderata, their necessity widely accepted, whether they constitute a moral rather than an efficacy constraint disputed. Constitutional practice — many systems incorporating moral criteria explicitly, which one account accommodates as a contingent fact and the other reads as recognition. Comparative and historical study — documents both patterns extensively, supporting both readings.

**Narrowing recorded.** Inclusive positivism narrows the reach: moral criteria stand incorporated where a system's rule of recognition so provides.

**Progress.** The documented exchange and its two treatments. The eight desiderata and their accepted necessity. The comparative record.

**Own record.**

## 77 Information as fundamental

**Conditions prior.** The statement holds across readings. The terms have determinate reference. The accounts hold their truth value while tested. A procedure repeated returns the same outcome independently of what precedes it. Nothing prior enters.

**Statement.** Is physical reality made of information, or is information a description we apply to physical things?

**The field's account of its hardness.**

**Accounts.** Information is fundamental: erasure carries a thermodynamic cost, measured experimentally at the predicted bound; black hole entropy scales with area in units of information; and quantum theory has been reconstructed from information-theoretic axioms.

These are results about physical systems described informationally, not evidence that information is the substrate: information requires a physical carrier and an encoding, and the reconstruction results show quantum theory follows from principles about what can be done with systems, which presupposes systems.

**Specifications.** There is a fact about what is fundamental — local. The relation between information and its carrier is one of dependence in one direction — local.

**Specifications withdrawn.**

**Common requirement.** That "fundamental" has a determinate sense in which one of these is it.

**Conserving relation.** A dependence relation established in one direction between two descriptions.

**Conserving reach.** All physical systems the question is asked of.

**Reach.** The statement quantifies over a dependence relation between two descriptions, both of which apply. Physical systems, measured, and describable both ways, are what is measured. The two are not the same.

**Unreachability.** The erasure bound and the area scaling are established and read both ways, and the reconstruction axioms quantify over systems, which is the standing objection. The dependence is not established over the systems by measurements describable in either description.

**Persistence.** A distinguishing prediction between the two readings, which no informational ontology has produced.

**Addresses.** Keyed property by property.

*The report.*

**Approaches.** The erasure principle — verified experimentally at the predicted bound, establishing a link and read both ways. Black hole thermodynamics — entropy scaling with area, a major result, its interpretation being the dispute at one more site. Information-theoretic reconstructions of quantum theory — derive the formalism from axioms about information processing, the axioms quantifying over systems, which is the standing objection. Informational ontology programmes — proposed, no distinguishing prediction produced. Digital physics and cellular automaton models — constrained by Lorentz invariance and by Bell correlations, specific proposals excluded.

**Narrowing recorded.** Information-theoretic reconstructions narrow the reach: the derivation stands from axioms about what can be done with systems rather than establishing the dependence.

**Progress.** The verified erasure bound. The area scaling of black hole entropy. The reconstruction results and their stated presupposition. The exclusion of specific digital proposals.

**Own record.**

## 78 Meno's paradox

**Conditions prior.** The statement holds across readings. The terms have determinate reference. The accounts hold their truth value while tested. A procedure repeated returns the same outcome independently of what precedes it. Nothing prior enters.

**Statement.** If you know what you are looking for, you need not look. If you do not, you would not recognise it. How is inquiry possible?

**The field's account of its hardness.**

**Accounts.** By partial knowledge: inquiry begins from knowing enough to specify the question and not enough to answer it, the two horns falsely assuming knowledge is all or nothing. A detective knows the crime and not the culprit, a chemist knows the reaction and not the mechanism, and in each case the object is specified under one description and sought under another, recognition being by the specifying description, which the inquirer has.

Specifying the question already presupposes the concepts the inquiry is meant to acquire, and the difficulty recurs at the level of concepts: acquiring a genuinely new concept cannot be described as recognising an answer to a question posed in prior terms.

**Specifications.** Inquiry is the search for something specifiable in advance — local. Recognising a result requires a prior criterion — local.

**Specifications withdrawn.**

**Common requirement.** That inquiry has an object identifiable by the inquirer.

**Conserving relation.** An account of acquisition, standing for what was not already available to the inquirer.

**Conserving reach.** All acquisitions, the acquisition of primitives included.

**Reach.** The statement quantifies over acquisition of what was not already available. Learners already equipped with concepts, studied acquiring further ones, are what is measured. The two are not the same.

**Unreachability.** Partial knowledge dissolves the classical version and does not reach the acquisition version; statistical learning accounts for acquisition from data with the architecture supplying the primitives, which is the objection; and the nativist argument stands unrefuted rather than accepted. The account is not reached over the acquisitions by learners already equipped.

**Persistence.** An account of primitive acquisition not presupposing the primitives, or a refutation of the argument that none is possible.

**Addresses.** Keyed property by property.

*The report.*

**Approaches.** Partial knowledge — dissolves the classical version, not reaching the concept-acquisition version. Recollection — not taken up as literal by later accounts. Statistical and associative learning — accounts for acquisition of many categories from data, whether the primitives are learned or presupposed being the dispute, the architecture supplying them, which is the objection. Radical concept nativism — concludes primitives are innate, widely resisted, with no generally accepted refutation of the argument itself. Developmental studies — document the sequence of acquisition, establishing when rather than how the recognition problem is solved.

**Narrowing recorded.** Partial knowledge narrows the reach: the account stands for acquisitions specifiable in prior terms. Statistical learning narrows the reach: acquisition stands accounted for where the architecture supplies the primitives.

**Progress.** The dissolution of the classical version. The categories acquired by statistical learners from data. The documented acquisition sequences.

**Own record.**

## 79 The equity premium puzzle

**Conditions prior.** The statement holds across readings. The terms have determinate reference. The accounts hold their truth value while tested. A procedure repeated returns the same outcome independently of what precedes it. Nothing prior enters.

**Statement.** Stocks have returned far more than safe bonds over long periods. Standard models say the gap should be small. Why is it large?

**The field's account of its hardness.** That no reasonable values of the risk-aversion coefficient or the time discount factor reproduce the observed premium when the model's parameters are fitted to the historical consumption-growth data, so the discrepancy is between a prediction and a measurement rather than between two measurements. The problem's originators, reviewing the literature two decades on, reviewed the main classes of proposed explanation and recorded that none had produced a fully satisfactory resolution; the field's own survey states that the proposed resolutions fail along crucial dimensions.

**Accounts.** The models are wrong about preferences: matching the observed premium with standard utility requires implausibly high risk aversion, and alternative preference specifications — habit formation, recursive utility, loss aversion — reproduce the premium with plausible parameters.

The premium may be smaller than measured: survivorship bias in the sample of surviving markets, a possible peso problem in which rare disasters did not occur in the sample, and the ex post rather than ex ante nature of the measurement all inflate the estimate, and rare disaster models reproduce the premium with standard preferences.

**Specifications.** Asset prices are set by a representative agent maximising expected utility over consumption — universal under asset pricing. Historical returns estimate expected returns — local.

**Specifications withdrawn.**

**Common requirement.** That there is a true expected premium the models should reproduce.

**Conserving relation.** An expected premium reproduced by a model, the reproduction standing on parameters independently measured.

**Conserving reach.** Expected returns, and preferences over states that did not occur.

**Reach.** The statement quantifies over expected returns, and preferences over states that did not occur. Realised returns in samples that did occur are what is measured. The two are not the same.

**Unreachability.** Alternative preference specifications fit by introducing parameters not independently measured, which the field states, and disaster calibration requires rates estimated from other histories and is not testable within the sample. The reproduction is not reached over the expected premium by realised returns.

**Persistence.** A model reproducing the premium on parameters independently measured, or a measurement of expected rather than realised returns.

**Addresses.** Hardness, and the failure of proposed resolutions along crucial dimensions: Mehra, "The Equity Premium: Why is it a Puzzle?", NBER Working Paper 9512, 2003, abstract. The twenty-year review of explanation classes and the originators' later leaning: Mehra and Prescott, "The equity premium in retrospect", Handbook of the Economics of Finance, 2003. The parametric-restriction counter-argument: Kocherlakota 1996, as reported at arXiv:1909.13019 §1. Disaster calibration to thirty-five countries: Barro 2006, as reported at the same address. Recent European decrease and the standard model's fit: the European-markets analysis reported at ResearchGate 228291610. Survivorship bias: Jorion and Goetzmann 1999, as cited at arXiv:cs/0512027 §D. Remaining properties: keyed at the next pass.

*The report.*

**Approaches.** Raising risk aversion — reproduces the premium and produces the risk-free rate puzzle, the same problem at the other price, with the recorded counter-argument that both puzzles follow from the parametric restrictions the original model imposed on the discount factor and the aversion coefficient. Habit formation — a fitted specification, recorded in later work as predicting a value much higher than observed in recent European data. Recursive preferences — separate risk aversion from intertemporal substitution, each introducing parameters not independently measured. Rare disasters — the historical consumption sample recorded as unrepresentative, the originating country having experienced no peacetime peak-to-trough decline of the relevant size while many others did, with calibration to an international dataset of thirty-five countries reproducing the premium under standard preferences and not testable within the sample. Heterogeneous agents with borrowing constraints — the representative-agent assumption relaxed and a fraction of the population constrained from holding the optimal portfolio, generating larger premia and requiring additional structural assumptions about who is constrained and why; recorded as the originators' own later leaning. Behavioural explanations — loss aversion and myopic evaluation, surveyed as a class. Survivorship bias — the surviving-market sample proposed as inflating the estimate. Broadening the sample — cross-country and long-horizon datasets reducing the estimate somewhat, with a substantial decrease in European markets recorded after the recent recession and a standard model then reproducing the premium without extreme aversion.

**Narrowing recorded.** Rare disaster models narrow the reach: the premium stands reproduced under disaster rates estimated from other histories. Heterogeneous-agent models weaken the relation: the required aversion falls at the cost of fitting parameters not independently measured.

**Progress.** The measured premium across samples. The reduction under broadened samples. The fits obtained under each specification, with their parameter costs stated.

**Own record.**

## 80 The Easterlin paradox

**Conditions prior.** The statement holds across readings. The terms have determinate reference. The accounts hold their truth value while tested. A procedure repeated returns the same outcome independently of what precedes it. Nothing prior enters.

**Statement.** Within a country, richer people report greater wellbeing. Over time, as a country grows richer, average reported wellbeing does not rise correspondingly. Which is the relation?

**The field's account of its hardness.**

**Accounts.** Relative income governs: wellbeing depends on position within a reference group, so raising everyone's income leaves positions unchanged and average reports flat, the cross-sectional and time-series findings then being consistent.

The time-series flatness is a measurement artefact: larger international datasets under a log specification find no satiation point and a consistent income-wellbeing relation within and across countries and over time, the paradox arising from short series and bounded scales.

**Specifications.** Reported wellbeing measures a quantity comparable across persons and times — local. A bounded scale can register unbounded change — local.

**Specifications withdrawn.**

**Common requirement.** That the reports measure the same thing in both comparisons.

**Conserving relation.** One quantity measured alike in both comparisons, the sameness standing across populations and times.

**Conserving reach.** Wellbeing, compared across times and populations.

**Reach.** The statement quantifies over wellbeing, compared across times and populations. Responses on bounded scales, whose interpretation may shift with the population, are what is measured. The two are not the same.

**Unreachability.** Both accounts read the extended series differently, which is the instruments disagreeing, and whether the log form is correct is not settled by data that fits both. The sameness is not preserved across populations and times by responses on bounded scales.

**Persistence.** A measure whose interpretation is demonstrably stable across populations and times.

**Addresses.** Keyed property by property.

*The report.*

**Approaches.** Extending the time series — both accounts doing so and reading the extended series differently, the dispute continuing through successive datasets. Changing the functional form — log income removing the apparent satiation, whether the log form is correct not settled by data that fits both. Distinguishing evaluative from experienced wellbeing — the two measures behaving differently, evaluative tracking income further, refining the question without settling the original comparison. Anchoring vignettes — correct for scale interpretation across cultures and change some cross-country rankings, a genuine advance applied to a subset of datasets. Objective correlates — health, longevity, and time-use measures rising with income, accepted by both accounts and disputed as to whether they are the subject.

**Narrowing recorded.** Anchoring vignettes narrow the reach: the correction stands for the subset of datasets to which it has been applied. Distinguishing evaluative from experienced wellbeing narrows the reach: each measure stands for its own construct rather than for the comparison as posed.

**Progress.** The extended series. The measured behaviour of the two wellbeing constructs. The corrected cross-country rankings. The objective correlates.

**Own record.**

## 81 Allais and Ellsberg

**Conditions prior.** The statement holds across readings. The terms have determinate reference. The accounts hold their truth value while tested. A procedure repeated returns the same outcome independently of what precedes it. Nothing prior enters.

**Statement.** People make choices that violate expected utility axioms, consistently and on reflection. Are the axioms wrong, or the choices?

**The field's account of its hardness.**

**Accounts.** The axioms describe rationality and the choices are errors: independence follows from a sure-thing principle most endorse when stated abstractly, a person violating it can be presented with a sequence of trades leaving them worse off by their own lights, and many shown the inconsistency revise. A theory of rational choice answers to what people would endorse on reflection, not to what they do.

Many do not revise on reflection, and the ambiguity pattern reflects a real distinction between risk with known probabilities and ambiguity without them, which the axioms cannot represent. A normative theory that competent reflective agents reject is not obviously the normative one.

**Specifications.** Preferences over outcomes are represented by a utility function and probabilities — universal under decision theory. The independence axiom holds — local.

**Specifications withdrawn.**

**Common requirement.** That there is a standard of correct choice against which the pattern is right or wrong.

**Conserving relation.** A standard of correct choice, standing against the pattern rather than fitted to it.

**Conserving reach.** All the choice patterns the axioms are asked of.

**Reach.** The statement quantifies over whether the axioms state the standard. Observed choices, and reports after being shown the inconsistency, are what is measured. The two are not the same.

**Unreachability.** Some revise and some do not, and the split is stable across studies; and the accommodating theories introduce functions fitted to the data they explain, which their authors state. The standard is not reached over the patterns by observed choices or by post-reflection reports.

**Persistence.** A standard the competent reflective agents who reject the axioms would endorse, or agreement on what settles a normative question empirically.

**Addresses.** Keyed property by property.

*The report.*

**Approaches.** Showing the inconsistency and re-asking — some revising, some not, the split stable across studies. Weakening independence — rank-dependent and prospect theories accommodating the first pattern, each introducing functions fitted to the data they explain, which their authors state. Non-additive beliefs and multiple priors — accommodate the ambiguity pattern by representing ambiguity, establishing representability and not which representation is correct. Raising the stakes — violations persisting at substantial stakes in several studies, the highest-stakes evidence limited. Neuroeconomic measurement — distinct activity for ambiguity and risk, establishing the distinction is registered without adjudicating the normative question.

**Narrowing recorded.** Weakening independence weakens the relation: the standard stands as a fitted representation of the pattern rather than a criterion against it. Multiple-prior representations weaken the relation: ambiguity stands represented rather than the correct representation identified.

**Progress.** The stable revision split. The measured persistence at substantial stakes. The distinct neural registration of ambiguity and risk. The accommodating representations and their stated fitting costs.

**Own record.**

## 82 Time preference and preference reversal

**Conditions prior.** The statement holds across readings. The terms have determinate reference. The accounts hold their truth value while tested. A procedure repeated returns the same outcome independently of what precedes it. Nothing prior enters.

**Statement.** People discount future rewards at rates that fall as the delay grows, and reverse earlier choices as a date approaches. Is discounting a stable rate?

**The field's account of its hardness.**

**Accounts.** Not a single rate: hyperbolic discounting fits the reversals directly and is documented across humans and animals, the reversals following from the shape of the curve rather than from any change in preference.

Measured hyperbolic patterns can arise from aggregation over heterogeneous exponential discounters, from uncertainty about receipt, and from procedural features of the elicitation: individual-level data are less clearly hyperbolic than aggregate data, and the reversals may reflect changing information rather than changing preference.

**Specifications.** A person's valuation of a delayed reward is a function of delay alone — local. Elicited choices measure that function — local.

**Specifications withdrawn.**

**Common requirement.** That there is a discount function to be measured.

**Conserving relation.** A discount function measured for an individual, the measurement standing across elicitation formats and domains.

**Conserving reach.** All individuals and domains the function is asked of.

**Reach.** The statement quantifies over an individual's valuation function. Elicited choices, aggregated, and sensitive to format, are what is measured. The two are not the same.

**Unreachability.** Estimated rates vary by elicitation format and by domain, and field estimates vary by orders of magnitude across studies, which is the instruments disagreeing on the same quantity. The measurement is not preserved across formats and domains by elicited choices.

**Persistence.** A measurement of the function stable across elicitation formats and domains.

**Addresses.** Keyed property by property.

*The report.*

**Approaches.** Eliciting at individual level — reduces the aggregation artefact, heterogeneity within individuals across domains remaining. Varying elicitation format — changes the estimated rates substantially, documented, and the standing methodological problem. Controlling for uncertainty of receipt — reduces apparent hyperbolicity in some designs, not eliminated. Field measurement from real decisions — estimates from borrowing and saving behaviour varying by orders of magnitude across studies and domains. Commitment device studies — show demand for commitment, supporting the reversal account behaviourally, the interpretation of demand contested.

**Narrowing recorded.** Individual-level elicitation narrows the reach: the measurement stands for an individual within a domain rather than across domains. Controlling for uncertainty narrows the reach: the reduction stands in the designs where it is applied.

**Progress.** The documented hyperbolic fit across humans and animals. The measured format sensitivity. The measured demand for commitment devices.

**Own record.**

## 83 The productivity paradox

**Conditions prior.** The statement holds across readings. The terms have determinate reference. The accounts hold their truth value while tested. A procedure repeated returns the same outcome independently of what precedes it. Nothing prior enters.

**Statement.** Large investments in information technology were followed by measured productivity growth that did not rise correspondingly. Where did the gains go?

**The field's account of its hardness.**

**Accounts.** Measurement misses them: output quality, variety, and free digital services are poorly captured in national accounts, the gains being real but unmeasured, with successive studies finding substantial unmeasured consumer surplus.

Mismeasurement is insufficient in magnitude: the slowdown is broad across countries and sectors, including sectors with little digital output, and plausible mismeasurement adjustments do not close the gap. The gains may be smaller than expected, or delayed by complementary reorganisation.

**Specifications.** Productivity is output per input, both measurable — universal under national accounting. Technology raises output for given inputs — local.

**Specifications withdrawn.**

**Common requirement.** That there is a true productivity change the statistics approximate.

**Conserving relation.** A true productivity change approximated by the statistics, the approximation standing for output the accounts do not price.

**Conserving reach.** Output including what the accounts do not price.

**Reach.** The statement quantifies over output including what the accounts do not price. Priced transactions are what is measured. The two are not the same.

**Unreachability.** Estimates of unmeasured surplus are large and, by the accounting against them, still short of the gap; and the slowdown appears where digital output shares differ widely, which constrains the mismeasurement account. The approximation is not reached over unpriced output by priced transactions.

**Persistence.** A measurement of unpriced output closing the gap, or the predicted acceleration observed.

**Addresses.** Keyed property by property.

*The report.*

**Approaches.** Adjusting for quality and free goods — estimates of unmeasured surplus large and, by the accounting against them, still short of the gap. Cross-country comparison — the slowdown appearing where digital output shares differ widely, constraining the mismeasurement account. Firm-level studies — large returns to IT combined with organisational change, establishing complementarity, the aggregate gap remaining. Lag hypotheses — general purpose technologies diffusing over decades, the record for electricity supporting a lag, predicting eventual acceleration, not observed and remaining available. Intangible capital measurement — raises measured output somewhat and shifts timing.

**Narrowing recorded.** Firm-level studies narrow the reach: the returns stand at firm level where organisational change accompanies the investment. Intangible capital measurement narrows the reach: the correction stands for the intangibles it prices.

**Progress.** The estimated unmeasured surplus and its magnitude against the gap. The cross-country and cross-sector distribution of the slowdown. The firm-level returns under complementary reorganisation.

**Own record.**

## 84 The transformation problem

**Conditions prior.** The statement holds across readings. The terms have determinate reference. The accounts hold their truth value while tested. A procedure repeated returns the same outcome independently of what precedes it. Nothing prior enters.

**Statement.** In a theory where value derives from labour time, prices are observed to deviate systematically from labour values. How do the two relate?

**The field's account of its hardness.**

**Accounts.** The transformation can be completed: successive formulations, including single-system interpretations that value inputs and outputs at prices rather than values, preserve the aggregate equalities and remove the inconsistency the original procedure carried.

The transformation is redundant: prices and the profit rate are determined by physical input-output data and the real wage alone, with no role for labour values in the determination, and the value magnitudes do no work.

**Specifications.** There is a magnitude, labour value, standing behind price — local. Aggregate equalities between the two systems should hold — local.

**Specifications withdrawn.**

**Common requirement.** That labour values are determinate magnitudes and that their relation to prices is a question with an answer.

**Conserving relation.** A determination of prices by value magnitudes, the determination standing alongside the physical determination.

**Conserving reach.** All prices the theory is asked of.

**Reach.** The statement quantifies over whether one determination underlies the other. Observed prices and physical input-output tables are what is measured. The two are not the same.

**Unreachability.** The aggregate equalities cannot all be preserved simultaneously in the standard framework, which is proved; and prices and profit are determined without value magnitudes, which is not disputed as mathematics. The determination by value magnitudes is not reached over the prices by observed prices and physical tables.

**Persistence.** A role for value magnitudes in the determination not already discharged by the physical data, or agreement that the equalities are definitional.

**Addresses.** Keyed property by property.

*The report.*

**Approaches.** The corrected procedure — the aggregate equalities proved not all simultaneously preservable in the standard framework. Single-system interpretations — preserve the equalities by redefining the value of inputs, their critics holding the redefinition makes the equalities definitional. The physical-determination critique — establishes that prices and profit are determined without value magnitudes, not disputed as mathematics. Empirical correlation studies — high correlation between labour values and prices across sectors, contested as an artefact of sector size, examined and not settled. Rejecting the framework — alternatives determining prices differently, the dispute then being between frameworks rather than within one.

**Narrowing recorded.** Single-system interpretations weaken the relation: the equalities stand under a redefinition of input value, which their critics hold makes them definitional.

**Progress.** The proof that the aggregate equalities cannot all hold. The physical determination result. The measured correlations and the sector-size objection to them.

**Own record.**

## 85 Money illusion

**Conditions prior.** The statement holds across readings. The terms have determinate reference. The accounts hold their truth value while tested. A procedure repeated returns the same outcome independently of what precedes it. Nothing prior enters.

**Statement.** Do people respond to nominal amounts rather than to real purchasing power?

**The field's account of its hardness.**

**Accounts.** Yes: experimental and survey evidence shows systematic responses to nominal framing, downward nominal wage rigidity is documented and hard to account for otherwise, and it is a load-bearing assumption in models that fit inflation-output dynamics.

Apparent illusion is often rational under uncertainty about relative prices, or an artefact of framing in surveys where real magnitudes are not salient: nominal rigidity is accountable by contracting and fairness considerations without any error about real values.

**Specifications.** Agents have preferences over real quantities — local. Nominal magnitudes are transformations of them — universal under the framework.

**Specifications withdrawn.**

**Common requirement.** That there is a fact about which magnitude the agent responds to.

**Conserving relation.** The magnitude the agent responds to, identified from choices.

**Conserving reach.** All decisions the question is asked of.

**Reach.** The statement quantifies over what the agent is responding to. Choices and reports under nominal presentation, with the real magnitude inferable but not presented, are what is measured. The two are not the same.

**Unreachability.** Wage rigidity is consistent with fairness norms about nominal terms as well as with error, nominal anchoring in transaction data admits explanation by liquidity constraints, and the diminishing of illusion under salient inflation is predicted by both accounts. The identification is not reached over the decisions by choices under nominal presentation.

**Persistence.** A decision setting in which the two accounts predict differently, or a measurement of the decision weight rather than the registration.

**Addresses.** Keyed property by property.

*The report.*

**Approaches.** Framing experiments — robust nominal effects found, whether laboratory framing transfers to consequential decisions being the standing question. Wage rigidity evidence — nominal cuts rare and real cuts via inflation accepted, consistent with illusion and with fairness norms about nominal terms. Housing and asset markets — nominal anchoring effects documented in transaction data, alternative explanations by liquidity constraints available. High-inflation environments — illusion diminishing where inflation is salient, which both accounts predict. Neuroimaging — distinct response to nominal gains at equal real value, establishing registration and not the decision weight.

**Narrowing recorded.** Framing experiments narrow the reach: the effects stand established in the laboratory, transfer to consequential decisions being the standing question.

**Progress.** The measured framing effects. The documented wage rigidity. The nominal anchoring in transaction data. The distinct neural registration.

**Own record.**

## 86 The two-fold cost of sex

**Conditions prior.** The statement holds across readings. The terms have determinate reference. The accounts hold their truth value while tested. A procedure repeated returns the same outcome independently of what precedes it. Nothing prior enters.

**Statement.** An asexual lineage produces twice as many reproducing offspring per parent. Sexual reproduction is nonetheless nearly universal in multicellular life. What pays the cost?

**The field's account of its hardness.**

**Accounts.** Recombination clears deleterious mutations and combines beneficial ones: a ratchet operates in asexual lineages, and the mutational-deterministic account requires only that mutations interact in a specified way.

Parasites adapt to common host genotypes and recombination generates rare combinations: field systems where sexual and asexual forms coexist show sexual forms favoured under high parasite pressure, which the mutational account does not predict.

**Specifications.** The cost is correctly stated at two-fold — local. An advantage must be located that repays it in each generation — local.

**Specifications withdrawn.**

**Common requirement.** That the maintenance of sex is accounted for by a benefit exceeding the cost.

**Conserving relation.** A benefit measured against the two-fold cost, the measurement standing across the taxa.

**Conserving reach.** All multicellular taxa where sex is maintained.

**Reach.** The statement quantifies over the advantage, measured against a stated two-fold cost. Particular systems where the comparison can be made are what is measured. The two are not the same.

**Unreachability.** Each mechanism is demonstrated in its own system, and pluralist accounts combine them with weights not independently measured; the magnitude of the recombination benefit relative to the two-fold cost is contested. The measurement is not preserved across the taxa by particular systems.

**Persistence.** A benefit measured against the two-fold cost across taxa, or independently measured weights for the combined mechanisms.

**Addresses.** Keyed property by property.

*The report.*

**Approaches.** Systems with mixed reproduction — support the parasite account in those systems, with measured parasite pressure, system-specific. Experimental evolution with and without recombination — recombination speeding adaptation under stress in several organisms, establishing a benefit, its magnitude relative to the cost contested. Mutation accumulation experiments — confirm the ratchet in asexual lines, establishing the mechanism, the rates required for the deterministic account being at the edge of measured values. Ancient asexual lineages — the standing counterexample weakened by subsequent findings of horizontal transfer and other genetic exchange, which complicates the categories. Theoretical pluralism — most current accounts combining mechanisms, fitting, the combination's weights not independently measured.

**Narrowing recorded.** The parasite account narrows the reach: the advantage stands measured in the systems where parasite pressure is measured. Experimental evolution narrows the reach: the benefit stands under stress in the organisms tested.

**Progress.** The measured parasite pressure and its association with sexual forms. The confirmed ratchet in asexual lines. The measured speed of adaptation under recombination.

**Own record.**

## 87 Peto's paradox

**Conditions prior.** The statement holds across readings. The terms have determinate reference. The accounts hold their truth value while tested. A procedure repeated returns the same outcome independently of what precedes it. Nothing prior enters.

**Statement.** Cancer arises from mutations in dividing cells. Large long-lived animals have vastly more cell divisions and do not have proportionally more cancer. Why?

**The field's account of its hardness.**

**Accounts.** Evolved additional suppression: larger species carry additional tumour-suppressor mechanisms — many copies of a key suppressor gene in one lineage, alterations in DNA repair pathways in another — and the suppression scales with the risk.

The framing may overstate the paradox: multistage models with species-specific parameters can produce the observed pattern without additional mechanisms, and cross-species incidence data are sparse, biased toward captive and veterinary populations, and not standardised.

**Specifications.** Cancer risk is a function of cell number and division rate — local. Species are comparable on a standardised incidence measure — local.

**Specifications withdrawn.**

**Common requirement.** That a cross-species incidence comparison is meaningful.

**Conserving relation.** Lifetime risk compared across species, the comparison standing on standardised incidence.

**Conserving reach.** All species the comparison is asked of.

**Reach.** The statement quantifies over lifetime risk across species. Necropsy and veterinary records, unevenly sampled across species and conditions, are what is measured. The two are not the same.

**Unreachability.** Comparative genomics establishes mechanisms without quantifying their sufficiency, and multistage models fit with parameters estimated from the same data; the sampling bias is acknowledged in the studies themselves. The comparison is not reached over the species by unevenly sampled records.

**Persistence.** A standardised cross-species incidence measure, or an independent quantification of the suppressor mechanisms' sufficiency.

**Addresses.** Keyed property by property.

*The report.*

**Approaches.** Comparative genomics — identifies suppressor duplications and repair alterations in large species, establishing mechanisms exist, their quantitative sufficiency unmeasured. Zoo and necropsy databases — assemble incidence across many species and confirm weak scaling with body size, sampling bias acknowledged in the studies themselves. Multistage modelling — reproduces patterns under fitted parameters, the parameters not independently measured. Cell-level experiments — cells from large species showing different sensitivity to damage, establishing differences, the link to whole-organism incidence unmeasured. Within-species scaling — larger individuals within a species showing higher risk, consistent with the framing and not resolving the cross-species case.

**Narrowing recorded.** Within-species scaling narrows the reach: the relation stands within a species rather than across species. Cell-level experiments narrow the reach: the differences stand at cell level, the link to organism incidence unmeasured.

**Progress.** The identified suppressor duplications and repair alterations. The confirmed weak scaling with body size. The measured within-species scaling. The cell-level sensitivity differences.

**Own record.**

## 88 The C-value paradox

**Conditions prior.** The statement holds across readings. The terms have determinate reference. The accounts hold their truth value while tested. A procedure repeated returns the same outcome independently of what precedes it. Nothing prior enters.

**Statement.** Genome size varies more than a thousandfold among organisms of similar complexity. What determines it?

**The field's account of its hardness.**

**Accounts.** Non-coding content is largely neutral: genome size tracks the accumulation of repetitive elements, and effective population size governs how efficiently selection removes them, no functional role being required.

Much non-coding DNA is functional: regulatory elements, structural roles, and biochemical activity across most of the genome indicate function, and the assumption of neutrality was a placeholder rather than a finding.

**Specifications.** Genome content is either functional or not — local. Function is identifiable by an operational criterion — local.

**Specifications withdrawn.**

**Common requirement.** That "functional" has a criterion both accounts accept.

**Conserving relation.** A criterion of function sorting sequence into functional and not, the sorting standing across the instruments.

**Conserving reach.** All sequence the question is asked of.

**Reach.** The statement quantifies over whether sequence has a fitness-relevant role. Biochemical activity, conservation, and effects of deletion are what is measured — three criteria giving different answers on the same sequence. The two are not the same.

**Unreachability.** Three criteria of function return different fractions on the same sequence, and the field debated exactly whether activity is function in print. The sorting is not preserved across the instruments by any of the three criteria.

**Persistence.** A criterion of function the three instruments agree on, applied to the same sequence.

**Addresses.** Keyed property by property.

*The report.*

**Approaches.** Biochemical activity assays — report activity over most of the genome, whether activity is function being the dispute, debated in print. Comparative conservation — constrains functional fraction to a much smaller figure, a different criterion giving a different number. Deletion experiments — large deletions of conserved non-coding regions producing viable animals in some cases, establishing dispensability under laboratory conditions. The onion comparison and relatives — species with similar biology and vastly different genome sizes, a standing challenge to functional accounts, not answered. Population-genetic modelling — reproduces size variation from effective population size, fitting, and depending on parameters estimated from the same data.

**Narrowing recorded.** Comparative conservation narrows the reach: the functional fraction stands under a conservation criterion. Deletion experiments narrow the reach: dispensability stands under laboratory conditions.

**Progress.** The measured activity fraction. The conserved fraction. The viable deletions. The measured genome-size variation across similar biology.

**Own record.**

## 89 The paradox of the plankton

**Conditions prior.** The statement holds across readings. The terms have determinate reference. The accounts hold their truth value while tested. A procedure repeated returns the same outcome independently of what precedes it. Nothing prior enters.

**Statement.** Competition theory says the number of coexisting species cannot exceed the number of limiting resources. Plankton communities show dozens of species on a handful of resources. How?

**The field's account of its hardness.** That the fluctuation-dependent mechanisms remove the theoretical limit rather than supplying a count: one of them can support a number of species growing quadratically with the number of discrete resources, the other an arbitrary number. The field states the consequence itself — the mechanisms resolved the paradox and imposed additional problems, since with no theoretical limit to diversity the question becomes why the observed number is the number observed, and answering it requires a framework capable of measuring the relative importance of the competing explanations. The field also records that the mechanism originally proposed fails as stated, temporal variation alone being insufficient, while continuing to be cited as the explanation.

**Accounts.** Equilibrium never arrives: environmental fluctuation, mixing, and predation reset competition before exclusion completes, and the exclusion principle applies to an equilibrium the system does not reach.

The niches are not being counted correctly: spatial structure, temporal partitioning, and species-specific predation supply more limiting factors than the resource count records, and coexistence is niche-based after all.

**Specifications.** Coexistence requires niche differences — local. Limiting factors are enumerable — local.

**Specifications withdrawn.**

**Common requirement.** That the number of limiting factors is countable in principle.

**Conserving relation.** A count of limiting factors sufficient for the observed coexistence, the count standing on measurement.

**Conserving reach.** All limiting factors operating.

**Reach.** The statement quantifies over all limiting factors operating. Measured resource concentrations and identified predators are what is measured. The two are not the same.

**Unreachability.** Each added measured factor raises the ceiling and whether the count converges is open; and the coexistence framework accommodates both accounts without measuring their weights in natural communities. The count is not reached over the operating factors by measured concentrations and identified predators.

**Persistence.** A count of limiting factors demonstrated sufficient, or measured weights for the fluctuation-dependent and fluctuation-independent mechanisms in natural communities.

**Addresses.** Hardness, the theoretical species counts, and the statement that the mechanisms resolved the paradox and imposed additional problems: the conceptual-issues paper on modern coexistence theory, arXiv:2201.07926, introduction. The failure of the originally proposed mechanism, and its continued citation: Li and Chesson, American Naturalist 188(2), 2016, and the storage-effect analysis at arXiv:1904.10845 §1. Storage effect and buffered growth: arXiv:2201.06691. Time-series selection estimates across 148 series: Fox, Ecology 2010, abstract. Spatial, turbulence, and toxin routes: the plankton-paradox review, ResearchGate 263267841. Trait-structured ocean models and slowed exclusion: ICES Journal of Marine Science 71(2), 2014. Remaining properties: keyed at the next pass.

*The report.*

**Approaches.** The originally proposed mechanism — coexistence by permanent failure to reach equilibrium as external factors change; the argument recorded as incomplete, with no model validating it, and the specific mechanism shown by later modelling to fail, while continuing to be cited. The coexistence framework — partitions mechanisms into stabilising and equalising, and into fluctuation-dependent and fluctuation-independent, each measurable in principle. The storage effect — species specialising on different aspects of a variable environment, with buffered growth when not actively taking up resources, supporting an arbitrary number of species in theory and tested in the field in specific systems. Relative nonlinearity — species specialising on resource variance rather than mean level, the variation generated externally by seasonal turnover or internally by consumer-resource dynamics, supporting a number growing quadratically with the resources. Chaotic dynamics — competition among few resources generating chaos permitting many species, established in models. Spatial and self-generated heterogeneity — segregation, mesoscale turbulence and coherent vortices, and toxin production, each proposed as a distinct route. Neutral and equalising accounts — diversity patterns reproduced with no niche differences, and trait-structured ocean models in which exclusion remains an intrinsic property but its rate is slowed. Time-series estimation of selection — net and fluctuating selection estimated across ninety-nine annual zooplankton series and forty-nine algal series, with within-year net selection found statistically significant and ecologically weak. Adding measured factors — each addition raising the ceiling, whether the count converges being the open part. Long-term microcosm experiments — sustained coexistence observed beyond the resource count.

**Narrowing recorded.** Chaotic dynamics narrows the reach: sufficiency stands established in models. Microcosm experiments narrow the reach: the coexistence stands observed under laboratory conditions.

**Progress.** The partitioned coexistence framework. The chaotic sufficiency result in models. The observed microcosm coexistence beyond the resource count.

**Own record.**

## 90 The binding problem

**Conditions prior.** The statement holds across readings. The terms have determinate reference. The accounts hold their truth value while tested. A procedure repeated returns the same outcome independently of what precedes it. Nothing prior enters.

**Statement.** Different features of an object are processed in separate brain regions. Experience presents one object. What binds them?

**The field's account of its hardness.**

**Accounts.** Temporal synchrony: neurons responding to features of one object fire in synchrony while those responding to different objects do not, supplying a tag available without dedicated conjunction cells and scaling combinatorially where convergence does not. Gamma-band coherence is the documented signature, appearing across species and tasks, and tracking perceptual grouping in the predicted direction.

Synchrony is a correlate, and no mechanism reading it has been identified: hierarchical convergence onto neurons responsive to conjunctions, plus attention, accounts for the phenomena without a separate binding process, and the problem may be posed by an assumption about how experience must be constructed.

**Specifications.** Features are represented separately — universal under systems neuroscience. Unified experience requires a process combining them — local.

**Specifications withdrawn.**

**Common requirement.** That the unity of the object in experience corresponds to a combining operation.

**Conserving relation.** A combining operation identified, the identification standing as cause rather than correlate.

**Conserving reach.** All the cases unity is asked of.

**Reach.** The statement quantifies over what makes the features one object in experience. Neural activity and behavioural report are what is measured. The two are not the same.

**Unreachability.** Synchrony is documented and whether it is causal or a consequence of shared drive is where the field's analyses disagree, with the disputes in print on both sides; and no mechanism reading the synchrony has been identified. The identification is not reached over the cases by neural activity and behavioural report.

**Persistence.** A mechanism reading the synchrony identified, or a manipulation separating cause from consequence across the cases.

**Addresses.** Keyed property by property.

*The report.*

**Approaches.** Gamma synchrony studies — documented across species and tasks, whether causal or a consequence of shared drive disputed, the disputes in print on both sides. Illusory conjunction studies — binding failures under attentional load, establishing attention's role, not a mechanism. Convergence and conjunction cells — found in higher visual areas, handling some conjunctions, combinatorial coverage being the objection. Optogenetic manipulation of synchrony — behavioural effects in specific paradigms, the strongest causal route, scope so far narrow. Dissolving the problem — unity argued to be a feature of report rather than a construction requiring a process, reframing, the phenomena remaining to be accounted.

**Narrowing recorded.** Optogenetic manipulation narrows the reach: the causal result stands for the specific paradigms manipulated. Conjunction cells narrow the reach: the account stands for the conjunctions convergence covers.

**Progress.** The documented gamma coherence across species and tasks. The measured binding failures under attentional load. The identified conjunction cells. The behavioural effects of synchrony manipulation.

**Own record.**

## 91 Sleep

**Conditions prior.** The statement holds across readings. The terms have determinate reference. The accounts hold their truth value while tested. A procedure repeated returns the same outcome independently of what precedes it. Nothing prior enters.

**Statement.** Sleep is universal among animals with nervous systems, costly, and dangerous. What is it for?

**The field's account of its hardness.**

**Accounts.** Restoration and clearance: metabolic waste clearance rises during sleep, specific molecular and cellular repair processes are sleep-dependent, and deprivation produces measurable physiological damage and, in some species, death.

Synaptic and informational function: sleep supports memory consolidation and synaptic downscaling, with replay of waking activity documented, and the informational account addresses why brain activity is patterned rather than merely reduced.

**Specifications.** Sleep has a function — local. Identifying its primary function is the question — local.

**Specifications withdrawn.**

**Common requirement.** That there is a primary function to be identified.

**Conserving relation.** A primary function identified, the identification standing separately from the state that carries it.

**Conserving reach.** All animals with nervous systems.
