**Reach.** The statement quantifies over the contribution of sleep, separable from the state that carries it. Correlates and effects of deprivation are what is measured, both of which the two accounts predict. The two are not the same.

**Unreachability.** Deprivation produces cognitive and physiological deficits together, which both accounts predict, and the clearance results have been contested in technique, which is the instruments in dispute. The identification is not reached over the animals by correlates and deprivation effects.

**Persistence.** A measurement separating the two functions, or agreement that both operate and the question is their proportion.

**Addresses.** Keyed property by property.

*The report.*

**Approaches.** Deprivation studies — produce cognitive and physiological deficits together, predicted by both accounts. Clearance measurement — clearance rising in sleep in rodents, the magnitude and the technique disputed in subsequent work. Replay and consolidation studies — documented in hippocampus and cortex, with targeted reactivation improving retention, establishing an informational role, whether primary not settled. Comparative studies — sleep duration and architecture varying widely with ecology and phylogeny, constraining single-function accounts. Unihemispheric and reduced sleep species — showing the requirement is modifiable, the residual requirement not zero.

**Narrowing recorded.** Replay studies narrow the reach: the informational role stands established in the structures where replay is documented. Clearance measurement narrows the reach: the result stands in the species and technique measured.

**Progress.** The measured deprivation effects. The documented replay and the retention gains from targeted reactivation. The comparative variation in duration and architecture. The modifiable requirement in reduced-sleep species.

**Own record.**

## 92 Other minds

**Conditions prior.** The statement holds across readings. The terms have determinate reference. The accounts hold their truth value while tested. A procedure repeated returns the same outcome independently of what precedes it. Nothing prior enters.

**Statement.** I know my own experience directly. I infer everyone else's from behaviour. What justifies the inference?

**The field's account of its hardness.**

**Accounts.** Inference to the best explanation: other bodies resemble mine, behave as mine does, and share my physiology, and experience in them is by far the best explanation of the behaviour.

The inference is from a single case, which is the weakest form of induction there is, and no independent check on it is available: alternatives — that experience is a way of describing behaviour, or that the demand for justification is malformed — have been developed precisely because the argument from analogy is regarded as insufficient.

**Specifications.** Experience is directly known in one's own case and not in others' — local. The asymmetry is a fact about access rather than about the concepts — local.

**Specifications withdrawn.**

**Common requirement.** That there is something in others whose presence the inference is about.

**Conserving relation.** A justification of the inference, standing on a check independent of the single case.

**Conserving reach.** All others the inference is made about.

**Reach.** The statement quantifies over experience in another. Behaviour and physiology are what is measured. The two are not the same.

**Unreachability.** The inference is from one case with no check, formally weak by a result both accounts accept; inference to the best explanation depends on the framework already admitting experience as an explanans, which is what is in question; and criterial accounts remove the inference and are contested as changing the question. The justification is not reached over the others by behaviour and physiology.

**Persistence.** A check on the inference independent of the single case, or agreement that no justification is owed.

**Addresses.** Keyed property by property.

*The report.*

**Approaches.** Argument from analogy — one case, no check, its weakness acknowledged by its defenders. Criterial accounts — behaviour in context as a criterion rather than evidence, removing the inference and contested as changing the question. Inference to the best explanation — depends on the explanatory framework already admitting experience as an explanans, which is what is in question. Neurophysiological similarity — establishes similarity, which is the analogy premise measured. Denying the asymmetry — self-knowledge held also to be inferential, levelling the two cases without supplying the missing check.

**Narrowing recorded.** Criterial accounts weaken the relation: behaviour in context stands as a criterion in place of an inference to be justified.

**Progress.** The measured neurophysiological similarity. The formal weakness of one-case induction, accepted by both accounts.

**Own record.**

## 93 Symbol grounding

**Conditions prior.** The statement holds across readings. The terms have determinate reference. The accounts hold their truth value while tested. A procedure repeated returns the same outcome independently of what precedes it. Nothing prior enters.

**Statement.** A system manipulates symbols by rules. How do its symbols come to be about anything?

**The field's account of its hardness.**

**Accounts.** By causal connection to the world: grounding is achieved through sensorimotor interaction, symbols acquired through perception and action being connected to what they denote.

Causal connection underdetermines reference: any causal chain admits multiple assignments of what the symbol is about, the indeterminacy arguments have not been answered, and adding sensors relocates the question to what the sensor states are about.

**Specifications.** Symbols have determinate content — local. Content is fixed by a relation between the system and the world — local.

**Specifications withdrawn.**

**Common requirement.** That there is a fact about what a symbol is about.

**Conserving relation.** Content fixed by a relation between system and world, the fixing standing against alternative assignments.

**Conserving reach.** All symbols the content relation is asked of.

**Reach.** The statement quantifies over the content relation. The system's behaviour and its causal history are what is measured. The two are not the same.

**Unreachability.** Indicator theories face the disjunction problem and misrepresentation cases, documented and unresolved; and content fixed by selection history inherits indeterminacy of the selected function. The fixing is not reached over the symbols by behaviour and causal history.

**Persistence.** A relation fixing content against the alternative assignments, or agreement that content is not fixed by such a relation.

**Addresses.** Keyed property by property.

*The report.*

**Approaches.** Robotic grounding — builds systems whose categories arise from interaction, establishing categories are acquirable, whether they are about anything being the original question. Causal and informational semantics — asymmetric dependence and indicator theories, each facing the disjunction problem and misrepresentation cases, documented and unresolved. Teleosemantics — fixes content by selection history, facing indeterminacy of the selected function, contested. The room argument — holds syntax insufficient for semantics, the systems reply and the robot reply standing as responses, the exchange not converged. Large language models — acquire behaviour previously thought to require grounding, what this shows about content actively disputed.

**Narrowing recorded.** Teleosemantics narrows the reach: content stands fixed by selection history where a function was selected. Robotic grounding narrows the reach: categories stand acquired through interaction in the systems built.

**Progress.** The acquirable categories in built systems. The documented disjunction and misrepresentation cases. The behaviour acquired by models without sensorimotor interaction.

**Own record.**

## 94 Interpretability of built systems

**Conditions prior.** The statement holds across readings. The terms have determinate reference. The accounts hold their truth value while tested. A procedure repeated returns the same outcome independently of what precedes it. Nothing prior enters.

**Statement.** A trained network produces outputs. Can its computation be explained in terms a person can check?

**The field's account of its hardness.**

**Accounts.** Yes, increasingly: circuits have been identified, features localised, and specific behaviours traced to specific components, sparse decomposition methods extract features more interpretable than raw units, and the methods improve steadily.

Explanations obtained so far cover a small fraction of computation, and the standard for success is not agreed: explanations that predict behaviour under intervention are rare, many are post hoc narratives fitting observed activity, and the field's own evaluations find explanations that appear plausible and fail causal tests.

**Specifications.** The computation decomposes into components with stateable roles — local. A person-checkable explanation exists — local.

**Specifications withdrawn.**

**Common requirement.** That there is a fact about what a component does, independent of the explanation offered.

**Conserving relation.** An explanation of the computation, standing under intervention rather than fitting observed activity.

**Conserving reach.** The whole of the system's computation.

**Reach.** The statement quantifies over what the system's computation is. Activations, ablations, and behaviour under intervention are what is measured. The two are not the same.

**Unreachability.** Probing was shown by the field to recover information the system does not use, a proved limitation on its own instrument; and causal results are component-specific and do not compose into whole-model accounts. The explanation is not reached over the whole computation by activations, ablations, and intervention.

**Persistence.** An explanation covering the computation and standing under intervention, or an agreed standard for what counts as one.

**Addresses.** Keyed property by property.

*The report.*

**Approaches.** Feature visualisation and probing — produces plausible descriptions, probes recovering information the system does not use, which the field established and now controls for. Causal ablation and patching — stronger, results component-specific and not composing into whole-model accounts. Sparse dictionary methods — extract more interpretable features than raw units, coverage of total computation partial and measured. Formal verification — available for small networks and specific properties, scaling poorly, which is the known limit. Behavioural evaluation — establishes what the system does, not how.

**Narrowing recorded.** Formal verification narrows the reach: the guarantee stands for small networks and specific properties. Causal ablation narrows the reach: the results stand component by component. Sparse dictionary methods narrow the reach: coverage stands measured and partial.

**Progress.** The identified circuits and localised features. The measured coverage of sparse decompositions. The established limitation on probing. The verified properties of small networks.

**Own record.**

## 95 Machine and animal sentience

**Conditions prior.** The statement holds across readings. The terms have determinate reference. The accounts hold their truth value while tested. A procedure repeated returns the same outcome independently of what precedes it. Nothing prior enters.

**Statement.** Which systems have experience, and how would one tell?

**The field's account of its hardness.**

**Accounts.** By identifying the mechanisms that produce it in the case we know: behavioural and neural markers — nociception with motivational trade-offs, flexible learning, integration — provide graded evidence, and precautionary frameworks are built on them for animals.

The markers are selected by resemblance to us and have no independent validation: a system engineered to display them displays them, an organism with different architecture may have experience without them, and no test distinguishes presence from absence.

**Specifications.** Experience is present or absent in a system — local. Markers correlate with it — local.

**Specifications withdrawn.**

**Common requirement.** That there is a fact about presence, and that evidence bears on it.

**Conserving relation.** Markers bearing on presence, the bearing standing on validation independent of resemblance.

**Conserving reach.** All systems the question is asked of.

**Reach.** The statement quantifies over presence, indexed by markers with no independent validation. Behaviour, architecture, and physiology are what is measured. The two are not the same.

**Unreachability.** The marker frameworks lack independent validation, stated by their own authors, and an engineered system can produce the same behavioural pattern; theory-driven tests apply a theory that is what is in dispute. The bearing is not reached over the systems by behaviour, architecture, and physiology.

**Persistence.** A marker validated independently of resemblance to the case we know.

**Addresses.** Keyed property by property.

*The report.*

**Approaches.** Marker frameworks — applied to invertebrates with legislative consequence in several jurisdictions, establishing a graded evidential practice, the validation problem stated within the frameworks themselves. Comparative neuroanatomy — finds candidate structures in distant lineages, establishing analogous organisation, the inference to experience being the original question. Behavioural trade-off tests — motivational trade-offs against noxious stimuli demonstrated in decapods and insects, the strongest behavioural evidence, engineered systems producing the same pattern. Theory-driven tests — apply a theory of consciousness and read off a verdict, the theory being what is in dispute. Denying determinacy — no fact in marginal cases, contested, and consequential for practice.

**Narrowing recorded.** Marker frameworks narrow the reach: graded evidence stands for the taxa the markers were developed against.

**Progress.** The demonstrated motivational trade-offs in decapods and insects. The candidate structures identified in distant lineages. The marker frameworks and their legislative uptake.

**Own record.**

## 96 Credit assignment

**Conditions prior.** The statement holds across readings. The terms have determinate reference. The accounts hold their truth value while tested. A procedure repeated returns the same outcome independently of what precedes it. Nothing prior enters.

**Statement.** An outcome follows a long sequence of actions. Which actions produced it?

**The field's account of its hardness.**

**Accounts.** Statistical assignment works: temporal difference methods propagate value backward without a model, eligibility traces distribute credit across a decaying window, and backpropagation through time assigns it exactly in differentiable settings. These are the basis of systems that learn tasks with horizons of thousands of steps from sparse reward, and the convergence properties of the methods are proved for stated conditions.

These assign credit relative to a model and a reward specification, both supplied: where the sequence is long, the signal sparse, and the environment non-stationary, the assignment is unidentifiable in principle — many action sequences being consistent with the same outcome — and the methods succeed by imposing structure rather than by recovering it.

**Specifications.** An outcome is produced by identifiable contributions — local. A reward signal is specified — local.

**Specifications withdrawn.**

**Common requirement.** That there is a fact about which actions contributed.

**Conserving relation.** Contribution of individual actions identified, the identification standing without a model or reward specification supplied.

**Conserving reach.** All sequences the assignment is asked of.

**Reach.** The statement quantifies over contribution of individual actions. Outcomes, and counterfactual rollouts within a model, are what is measured. The two are not the same.

**Unreachability.** Formal conditions for identifying effects from observational data are proved and are frequently unmet in sequential settings, and reward shaping changes what is credited. The identification is not reached over the sequences by outcomes and within-model rollouts.

**Persistence.** An identification of contribution not resting on a supplied model or reward specification.

**Addresses.** Keyed property by property.

*The report.*

**Approaches.** Temporal difference learning — works, with performance degrading as horizon and sparsity grow, in a measured way. Counterfactual estimation — requires a model or the ability to re-run, which real settings often deny. Causal identification — formal conditions for identifying effects from observational data known and frequently unmet in sequential settings. Reward shaping — improves learning and changes what is credited, which is the concern. Hierarchical decomposition — assigns credit at multiple timescales, the decomposition being supplied.

**Narrowing recorded.** Temporal difference methods narrow the reach: convergence stands proved for stated conditions. Counterfactual estimation narrows the reach: the identification stands where a model or re-running is available.

**Progress.** The proved convergence under stated conditions. The measured degradation with horizon and sparsity. The demonstrated learning at horizons of thousands of steps from sparse reward.

**Own record.**

## 97 Generalization in learning

**Conditions prior.** The statement holds across readings. The terms have determinate reference. The accounts hold their truth value while tested. A procedure repeated returns the same outcome independently of what precedes it. Nothing prior enters.

**Statement.** A model fits its training data and performs well on new data. Classical theory says a model with more parameters than data should not. Why does it?

**The field's account of its hardness.** That the classical bounds are vacuous at practical scale, and that the correction proposed for them — identifying the implicit bias of the optimiser and applying the bound to a correspondingly smaller class — was subsequently argued to be insufficient in principle: for the constructions given, the bound remains large even over a class restricted to what the algorithm actually reaches. The field states that a candidate measure must be non-vacuous, must track the observed width and depth dependence, must apply to the network as trained without modification, and must increase with label noise, and records that no proposed measure meets all four.

**Accounts.** Implicit regularisation: optimisation dynamics select solutions with properties that generalise, the double-descent curve shows test error falling again past the interpolation threshold, and the classical bound was about a different quantity than the one that governs.

No current theory predicts the observed generalisation in advance: uniform-convergence bounds have been shown insufficient for these regimes by explicit construction, the proposed measures fail to rank models correctly in large-scale empirical comparisons, and the explanations are fitted after the fact.

**Specifications.** Generalisation is governed by a complexity measure of the model class — local. A bound stated before training predicts performance — local.

**Specifications withdrawn.**

**Common requirement.** That there is a property of the trained model determining its generalisation.

**Conserving relation.** A property determining generalisation, stated before training rather than fitted after.

**Conserving reach.** Performance on data not drawn yet.

**Reach.** The statement quantifies over performance on data not drawn yet. Held-out samples from the same distribution are what is measured. The two are not the same.

**Unreachability.** Uniform convergence was proved insufficient for interpolating regimes by explicit construction, in the field's own literature, and the proposed complexity measures were measured across thousands of models not to rank them by generalisation. The property is not reached over undrawn data by held-out samples.

**Persistence.** A property stated before training that predicts generalisation across the settings in use.

**Addresses.** Hardness, the four stated requirements on a candidate measure, and the insufficiency argument: Nagarajan and Kolter, "Uniform convergence may be unable to explain generalization in deep learning", NeurIPS 2019, §1 and the authors' own exposition. Vacuity of classical bounds at practical scale: Zhang et al. 2017, as reported in the same. Norm-based guarantees growing with width: the same, §1. Candidate-measure comparison and the sharpness-plus-norms finding: Neyshabur, Bhojanapalli, McAllester, and Srebro, "Exploring Generalization in Deep Learning", NeurIPS 2017, conclusion. Stability's Lipschitz requirement and the framework inventory: the PAC-Bayes survey, arXiv:2601.08100, §I. Remaining properties: keyed at the next pass.

*The report.*

**Approaches.** Classical uniform convergence — VC dimension, covering numbers, Rademacher complexity, recorded as vacuous at practical scale. Norm-based and margin-based capacity control — weight norms and spectrally normalised margins, developed as the proposed correction, with the recorded finding that existing norm-based guarantees grow rather than shrink as networks are widened, against the observed behaviour. Algorithm-dependent refinement — bounds incorporating the implicit bias of stochastic gradient descent, applied to a smaller class, met by the argument that uniform convergence may be unable to account for generalization even so. Implicit bias analysis — the optimiser's dynamics shown to select margin-maximising solutions, proved for specific architectures and losses, coverage of practical settings partial. PAC-Bayes bounds — non-vacuous bounds achieved in specific settings, the framework recorded as the most versatile of the available tools for data-dependent and algorithm-aware guarantees, tightness in general settings open. Algorithmic stability — bounds sensitivity to single-point perturbation, requiring global Lipschitz constants recorded as unavailable for nonconvex losses. Compression and information-theoretic bounds — a further family, developed alongside the above. Empirical comparison of candidate measures — large-scale ranking across many models, with the recorded finding that no measure ranks models by generalization reliably and that sharpness combined with norms captures much but not all of the behaviour. Double descent and benign overfitting — test error falling again past the interpolation threshold, established empirically and reproduced in analysable linear settings. Empirical scaling laws — predict performance accurately as a function of scale, predictive without explanatory content, which the field states.

**Narrowing recorded.** Implicit bias analysis narrows the reach: the result stands proved for specific architectures and losses. PAC-Bayes bounds narrow the reach: non-vacuous bounds stand in specific settings. Scaling laws weaken the relation: prediction stands without the determining property identified.

**Progress.** The double-descent curve. The explicit construction against uniform convergence. The measured failure of complexity measures to rank models. The non-vacuous bounds in specific settings. The predictive scaling laws.

**Own record.**

## 98 Anesthesia

**Conditions prior.** The statement holds across readings. The terms have determinate reference. The accounts hold their truth value while tested. A procedure repeated returns the same outcome independently of what precedes it. Nothing prior enters.

**Statement.** Chemically diverse molecules all abolish consciousness. What do they have in common?

**The field's account of its hardness.** That a unitary mechanism is stated as a falsehood in the field's own review: anesthetic agents produce different altered states of consciousness as a function of agent type and dose, which cannot occur through a common pathway, with the mechanisms stated as incompletely understood since 1846 and an overly narrow circuit-based reading cautioned against in the current reviews.

**Accounts.** Convergent network effects: the agents act on different receptors and converge on one outcome — disruption of long-range cortical communication and of thalamocortical integration — measured directly across chemically unrelated agents, appearing at the concentrations where responsiveness is lost, and reversing in the same order as responsiveness returns.

Distinct agents produce distinct signatures: the agents differ in their spectral and connectivity effects, treating them as one phenomenon obscures the differences, and there may be several routes to unresponsiveness rather than one mechanism.

**Specifications.** Loss of consciousness is a single state — local. Unresponsiveness indexes it — local.

**Specifications withdrawn.**

**Common requirement.** That there is one state the agents produce.

**Conserving relation.** One state identified as what the agents produce, the identification standing across the agents.

**Conserving reach.** All the agents that abolish responsiveness.

**Reach.** The statement quantifies over consciousness, indexed by unresponsiveness. Responsiveness, and neural correlates, are what is measured. The two are not the same.

**Unreachability.** No common molecular target exists, established; and convergent disruption and agent-specific signatures are both measured, which is the disagreement. Reports of experience without responsiveness after emergence show the index is not responsiveness. The identification is not preserved across the agents by responsiveness and neural correlates.

**Persistence.** A measure of the state independent of responsiveness, applied across the agents.

**Addresses.** Hardness, the unitary-mechanism statement and the unresolved questions: Bonhomme et al., "General Anesthesia: A Probe to Explore Consciousness", Frontiers in Systems Neuroscience 13:36, 2019. The since-1846 standing: Mashour, "Top-down mechanisms of anesthetic-induced unconsciousness", Frontiers in Systems Neuroscience 8:115, 2014, introduction. The declaring-understood caution: Mashour, "Anesthesia and the neurobiology of consciousness", Neuron, 2024. Remaining properties: keyed at the next pass.

*The report.*

**Approaches.** Common molecular target search — no single target found, agents acting on different receptors, established. Network-level measurement — finds convergent disruption of integration, and also agent-specific signatures, which is the dispute. Perturbational complexity index — distinguishes conscious from unconscious states across conditions with high accuracy, calibrated on the cases in question. Disconnected consciousness — patients reporting experience without responsiveness after emergence, showing the index is not responsiveness. Reversal agents and arousal circuits — specific circuits restoring responsiveness, establishing control of arousal, distinguished from consciousness in the same literature.

**Narrowing recorded.** The perturbational index narrows the reach: the discrimination stands across the conditions it is calibrated on. Arousal circuit work narrows the reach: control stands established for arousal, distinguished from consciousness.

**Progress.** The established absence of a common molecular target. The measured convergent disruption and the measured agent-specific signatures. The index's discrimination accuracy. The documented disconnected consciousness.

**Own record.**

## 99 Phantom limb

**Conditions prior.** The statement holds across readings. The terms have determinate reference. The accounts hold their truth value while tested. A procedure repeated returns the same outcome independently of what precedes it. Nothing prior enters.

**Statement.** People feel a limb that is not there, often painfully. What produces the sensation?

**The field's account of its hardness.**

**Accounts.** Cortical reorganisation: after amputation adjacent representations invade the deafferented region, the degree of reorganisation correlates with pain in imaging studies, and therapies aimed at reversing it have effects.

The representation is preserved rather than reorganised: imaging with individuated finger stimulation in amputees found the missing limb's map largely intact and correlated with vivid phantom sensation.

**Specifications.** The sensation is produced by an identifiable neural state — local. Imaging measures the relevant organisation — local.

**Specifications withdrawn.**

**Common requirement.** That there is a fact about whether the representation is reorganised or preserved.

**Conserving relation.** The map's state determined, the determination standing across imaging paradigms.

**Conserving reach.** All amputees the question is asked of.

**Reach.** The statement quantifies over the map's state and its relation to the sensation. Imaging at a resolution and paradigm that has changed between the two literatures is what is measured. The two are not the same.

**Unreachability.** Two imaging paradigms on the same phenomenon returned opposite directions, and the field has not reconciled them. The determination is not preserved across the paradigms by imaging.

**Persistence.** A reconciliation of the two imaging paradigms, or a measurement of the map's state independent of paradigm.

**Addresses.** Keyed property by property.

*The report.*

**Approaches.** Early mapping studies — found shifted representations correlating with pain, establishing the original account. Individuated-digit imaging in amputees — found preserved maps, directly contesting the earlier finding, the field not having fully reconciled them. Mirror therapy — produces relief in some patients, controlled trials giving mixed results and the mechanism disputed. Peripheral interventions — neuroma treatment and targeted reinnervation helping some patients, supporting a peripheral contribution. Congenital absence cases — phantoms reported without any prior limb, constraining accounts requiring a lost representation.

**Narrowing recorded.** Peripheral interventions narrow the reach: the contribution stands established for the patients they help.

**Progress.** The measured correlations in both directions under the two paradigms. The mixed controlled-trial results for mirror therapy. The peripheral intervention effects. The congenital absence cases.

**Own record.**

## 100 The problem of the criterion

**Conditions prior.** The statement holds across readings. The terms have determinate reference. The accounts hold their truth value while tested. A procedure repeated returns the same outcome independently of what precedes it. Nothing prior enters.

**Statement.** To know which beliefs are knowledge, one needs a criterion. To know a criterion is correct, one needs to know which beliefs are knowledge. Where does one start?

**The field's account of its hardness.**

**Accounts.** With particular cases: starting from clear instances of knowledge and deriving the criterion from them, which is how inquiry proceeds.

With the criterion: starting from a standard and sorting cases by it, on the ground that calling instances clear already applies an unstated standard.

**Specifications.** Knowledge has an extension and a criterion — local. One determines the other — local. The two starting points are exhaustive — local.

**Specifications withdrawn.**

**Common requirement.** That there is a correct criterion and a correct extension, matching.

**Conserving relation.** A starting point fixing one of criterion and extension, the fixing standing without the other assumed.

**Conserving reach.** The relation between criterion and extension.

**Reach.** The statement quantifies over the relation between criterion and extension. Judgments about cases, each already reflecting a standard, are what is measured. The two are not the same.

**Unreachability.** Selecting the clear cases is where the criterion enters unannounced, and securing the criterion's warrant is the standing objection to the other start; reflective equilibrium adjusts both to each other, which its critics hold is the circle restated. The fixing is not reached over the relation by judgments already reflecting a standard.

**Persistence.** A starting point not assuming what the other requires, or agreement that adjusting both to each other is warrant.

**Addresses.** Keyed property by property.

*The report.*

**Approaches.** Particularism — starting from clear instances, its critics noting that selecting the clear cases is where the criterion enters unannounced. Methodism — starting from a standard, the criterion's warrant being the standing objection, attempts to secure it widely held to fail. Reflective equilibrium — adjusts both until they cohere, accounting for practice, whether coherence between two things each adjusted to the other is warrant being the same circle, addressed by its proponents and not accepted as answered by critics. Externalist reliabilism — makes the criterion a matter of fact about reliability rather than something one must know, shifting the demand, the generality problem being its own open problem. The skeptical conclusion — neither can start, held by few and not refuted.

**Narrowing recorded.** Reliabilism weakens the relation: the criterion stands as a fact about reliability rather than something the knower must fix. Reflective equilibrium weakens the relation: coherence between mutually adjusted terms stands in place of a starting point.

**Progress.** The exhaustive statement of the two starting points. The standing objection against each. The generality problem, stated.

**Own record.**

## 101 Skepticism about the external world

**Conditions prior.** The statement holds across readings. The terms have determinate reference. The accounts hold their truth value while tested. A procedure repeated returns the same outcome independently of what precedes it. Nothing prior enters.

**Statement.** Experience would be the same whether it is caused by an external world or not. What justifies belief in the world?

**The field's account of its hardness.**

**Accounts.** Inference to the best explanation: an external world accounts for the coherence, involuntariness, and predictability of experience far better than any rival, and skeptical scenarios are constructed to have no explanatory content at all.

The rivals are constructed to be empirically equivalent, so no evidence favours one, and best-explanation reasoning applied here presupposes standards of explanation the skeptic is asking about.

**Specifications.** Experience is one thing and its cause another — local. Justification runs from the first to the second — local.

**Specifications withdrawn.**

**Common requirement.** That belief in the world stands in need of justification of some kind.

**Conserving relation.** A justification running from experience to its cause, standing without presupposing standards the statement questions.

**Conserving reach.** All rivals accounting for the experience.

**Reach.** The statement quantifies over the cause of experience. Experience is what is measured. The two are not the same.

**Unreachability.** The skeptical scenarios are empirically equivalent by construction, and transcendental arguments establish at most how one must think, by the standing objection. The justification is not reached over the rivals by experience.

**Persistence.** A justification not presupposing the standards in question, or agreement that no justification is owed.

**Addresses.** Keyed property by property.

*The report.*

**Approaches.** The rationalist proof — requires a guarantor whose establishment is generally held to fail. The proof by exhibition — exhibits a hand, its critics holding it begs the question, its defenders holding the demand is the mistake, unresolved and long-standing. Transcendental arguments — the skeptical hypothesis unstatable without presupposing what it doubts, the objection that they establish at most how we must think not generally answered. Semantic externalism — the argument that a system in the skeptical scenario cannot truly say it is in one, widely discussed and widely held not to reach the epistemic question. Contextualism — skeptical standards applying only in skeptical contexts, accounting for the pattern of judgments, whether it answers the argument contested. Rejecting the demand — each form facing the charge of changing the subject.

**Narrowing recorded.** Contextualism narrows the reach: the justification stands within ordinary contexts rather than against the skeptical rivals. Transcendental arguments weaken the relation: what is established is how one must think rather than what is the case.

**Progress.** The construction of the empirically equivalent rivals. The mapped objection against each response.

**Own record.**

## 102 Substance and bundle

**Conditions prior.** The statement holds across readings. The terms have determinate reference. The accounts hold their truth value while tested. A procedure repeated returns the same outcome independently of what precedes it. Nothing prior enters.

**Statement.** An object has properties. Is there something that has them, or is the object just the properties together?

**The field's account of its hardness.**

**Accounts.** Something has them: properties are had by something, and without a bearer there is no account of what unifies a bundle, nor of how an object persists through property change.

Nothing further is needed: the bearer is characterised only as that which has properties, so it has no nature of its own and does no work, and bundles unified by compresence do the same job without positing a featureless item.

**Specifications.** An object decomposes into a bearer and properties, or into properties alone — local. One of these is correct — local.

**Specifications withdrawn.**

**Common requirement.** That there is a fact about the constitution of an object.

**Conserving relation.** A determination of whether a bearer does work the properties do not.

**Conserving reach.** All objects the question is asked of.

**Reach.** The statement quantifies over whether a bearer does work the properties do not. Properties, measured, are what is measured. The two are not the same.

**Unreachability.** Trope theory solves the indiscernibles case and inherits the question of what bundles the tropes, which is the original question; and the appeal to physics is read as supporting bundles by some and as presupposing a bearer by others. The determination is not reached over the objects by measured properties.

**Persistence.** A case where the two accounts give different verdicts and one verdict is agreed correct.

**Addresses.** Keyed property by property.

*The report.*

**Approaches.** Bare substratum — posits a bearer with no properties of its own, its critics holding an item with no nature is unintelligible, its defenders accepting the characterisation as the point. Bundle theory — faces the identity of indiscernibles, two exactly similar objects being one, responses invoking tropes or spatiotemporal relations, each with costs. Trope theory — properties as particulars, bundled, solving the indiscernibles case and facing questions about what bundles them, which is the original question. Appeal to physics — field values at points, or structures without relata, read as supporting bundles by some and as presupposing a bearer by others. Deflating the question — held terminological, its critics noting the two accounts give different verdicts in indiscernibles cases, so something turns on it.

**Narrowing recorded.** Trope theory weakens the relation: properties stand as particulars, which removes the indiscernibles case and leaves the bundling question. Spatiotemporal responses narrow the reach: the account stands for objects distinguishable by location.

**Progress.** The indiscernibles case and the responses to it with their costs. The mapped verdicts of the two accounts where they differ.

**Own record.**

## 103 Why there is something

**Conditions prior.** The statement holds across readings. The terms have determinate reference. The accounts hold their truth value while tested. A procedure repeated returns the same outcome independently of what precedes it. Nothing prior enters.

**Statement.** Why is there something rather than nothing?

**The field's account of its hardness.**

**Accounts.** The statement is malformed: explanation relates states of affairs, and there is no prior state from which the existence of anything could be accounted for. Nothing is not a possible alternative that failed to obtain.

The statement is intelligible and unanswered: physical accounts of a universe arising from a quantum vacuum account for the arising from something — a vacuum with laws is not nothing — and relocate the question rather than closing it.

**Specifications.** Existence admits of explanation — local. Nothingness is a coherent alternative — local.

**Specifications withdrawn.**

**Common requirement.** That the statement either has an answer or is demonstrably malformed.

**Conserving relation.** An account of the obtaining of anything, standing without a prior state assumed.

**Conserving reach.** The obtaining of anything at all.

**Reach.** The statement quantifies over the obtaining of anything at all. What obtains is what is measured. The two are not the same.

**Unreachability.** Quantum origination accounts for arising from a structured state, which both accounts accept, so it relocates rather than closes; and brute fact absorbs the question at an acknowledged cost. The account is not reached over the obtaining of anything by what obtains.

**Persistence.** An account not itself starting from something, or a demonstration that the statement is malformed accepted by both accounts.

**Addresses.** Keyed property by property.

*The report.*

**Approaches.** Necessary being — a being that could not fail to exist, the modal status being what is in question, the arguments for it widely held to fail. Quantum vacuum origination — accounts for a universe from a vacuum state and laws, which are something, both accounts agreeing on the physics and disagreeing on what it answers. Brute fact — existence unexplained, consistent, its critics holding it concedes the question. The principle of sufficient reason — if accepted, requiring an answer, its acceptance being the dispute, unrestricted versions having known costs. Deflation — nothingness held incoherent, its critics finding the notion coherent enough to state.

**Narrowing recorded.** Quantum origination narrows the reach: the account stands for a universe arising from a structured state rather than for the obtaining of anything.

**Progress.** The physics of vacuum origination, agreed by both accounts. The mapped costs of the unrestricted principle. The stated positions on the coherence of nothingness.

**Own record.**

## 104 The simulation hypothesis

**Conditions prior.** The statement holds across readings. The terms have determinate reference. The accounts hold their truth value while tested. A procedure repeated returns the same outcome independently of what precedes it. Nothing prior enters.

**Statement.** Could what we observe be a computation run at another level of reality, and could we tell?

**The field's account of its hardness.**

**Accounts.** The statistical argument stands: if civilisations run many high-fidelity ancestor simulations, simulated observers vastly outnumber unsimulated ones, so one of three disjuncts holds and the hypothesis is live.

The argument requires an indifference principle over observers and an assumption that consciousness is substrate-independent, both assumed rather than established, and the hypothesis makes no differential prediction.

**Specifications.** Observers can be counted across levels — local. Experience is substrate-independent — local. A simulating level is possible — local.

**Specifications withdrawn.**

**Common requirement.** That there is a fact about the level one is at.

**Conserving relation.** A determination of the level, standing on observations available within it.

**Conserving reach.** A level not accessible from within.

**Reach.** The statement quantifies over a level not accessible from within. Observations within the level are what is measured. The two are not the same.

**Unreachability.** The statistical argument yields a disjunction rather than a conclusion, which its author states; and proposed signature tests test one implementation among many and are null. The determination is not reached over the level by observations within it.

**Persistence.** A differential prediction distinguishing the levels, or agreement on the indifference principle over observers.

**Addresses.** Keyed property by property.

*The report.*

**Approaches.** The statistical argument — yields a disjunction, not a conclusion, which its author states explicitly. Search for computational artefacts — proposed tests for lattice signatures in cosmic ray spectra, null so far, testing one implementation among many. Computational cost arguments — argue full simulation is infeasible, assuming the simulating level's physics resembles ours, which the hypothesis does not require. Anthropic and indifference reasoning — constrains inference, the indifference principle over observers itself contested with known paradoxes. The unfalsifiability objection — places it outside empirical inquiry, its proponents accepting the disjunctive form is not a prediction.

**Narrowing recorded.** Signature tests narrow the reach: the null result stands for the one implementation tested.

**Progress.** The disjunctive form of the statistical argument. The null lattice-signature results and their stated scope. The known paradoxes of the indifference principle.

**Own record.**

## 105 Laws of nature

**Conditions prior.** The statement holds across readings. The terms have determinate reference. The accounts hold their truth value while tested. A procedure repeated returns the same outcome independently of what precedes it. Nothing prior enters.

**Statement.** Are laws prescriptive — governing what happens — or descriptive summaries of what happens?

**The field's account of its hardness.**

**Accounts.** Descriptive: laws are the axioms of the simplest, strongest true systematisation of the facts. Nothing governs, regularity is all there is, and this avoids positing a modal relation nobody has accounted for.

Prescriptive: regularities alone cannot distinguish laws from accidental generalisations, cannot support counterfactuals, and cannot account for why the regularity holds. A relation of necessitation between universals, or a primitive modal fact, is required.

**Specifications.** There is a fact distinguishing laws from accidents — local. Laws either govern or summarise — local.

**Specifications withdrawn.**

**Common requirement.** That the distinction between law and accident is real.

**Conserving relation.** The modal status of a regularity determined, the determination standing on the regularities.

**Conserving reach.** All regularities the distinction is asked of.

**Reach.** The statement quantifies over the modal status of a regularity. The regularities themselves are what is measured. The two are not the same.

**Unreachability.** Best-system simplicity is language-relative, addressed by appeal to natural properties, which is a primitive; and the identification problem — what makes the necessitation relation necessitate — is unanswered to general satisfaction. The determination is not reached over the regularities by the regularities.

**Persistence.** A criterion distinguishing law from accident not resting on a primitive, or agreement that a primitive is acceptable.

**Addresses.** Keyed property by property.

*The report.*

**Approaches.** Best-system analysis — yields the distinction from simplicity and strength, the measures being language-relative, which is the standing objection, addressed by appeal to natural properties, which is a primitive. Necessitation between universals — the identification problem, what makes the relation necessitate, unanswered to general satisfaction. Primitivism about laws — takes governing as basic, consistent, and accounting for nothing further, which its proponents accept. Anti-realism about laws — no laws, only models that fit, accommodating practice and contested on the counterfactual role. Looking to physics — symmetry principles treated as more fundamental than laws in practice, accommodated by both accounts.

**Narrowing recorded.** Anti-realism weakens the relation: models that fit stand in place of a modal status determined. Primitivism weakens the relation: governing stands basic rather than accounted for.

**Progress.** The best-system construction and its language-relativity. The identification problem, stated. The treatment of symmetry principles in practice.

**Own record.**

## 106 Presentism and eternalism

**Conditions prior.** The statement holds across readings. The terms have determinate reference. The accounts hold their truth value while tested. A procedure repeated returns the same outcome independently of what precedes it. Nothing prior enters.

**Statement.** Do past and future events exist, or only present ones?

**The field's account of its hardness.**

**Accounts.** Only the present: what is past no longer exists and what is future does not yet, and the tensed character of thought and language reflects a real feature of the world.

All times exist: relativity gives no privileged present, and the tensed series is incoherent as an objective feature — an event must be past, present, and future, and the attempt to relativise these generates a regress.

**Specifications.** Existence is a matter of fact not relative to a time — local. The tensed and tenseless descriptions are rivals — local.

**Specifications withdrawn.**

**Common requirement.** That one of the two descriptions is correct.

**Conserving relation.** A determination of what exists unqualified, standing on observation.

**Conserving reach.** All times.

**Reach.** The statement quantifies over what exists, unqualified. Observations, all made at a time, are what is measured. The two are not the same.

**Unreachability.** A preferred foliation is consistent and undetectable in principle, and both accounts accept the linguistic semantics of tense and dispute the metaphysics. The determination is not reached over the times by observations made at a time.

**Persistence.** A detectable privileged present, which the foliation response makes undetectable in principle, or agreement on what the tensed semantics establishes.

**Addresses.** Keyed property by property.

*The report.*

**Approaches.** The regress argument against objective tense — widely regarded as showing a difficulty, met by taking tense as primitive, the exchange old and unresolved. Relativity — excludes a frame-independent present, met with a preferred foliation undetectable in principle, consistent and unfalsifiable. The truthmaker objection — asks what makes past-tensed statements true if the past does not exist, met with present traces or primitive tensed facts, each response contested. Growing block — past and present existing, future not, facing the objection that its inhabitants could not know they are at the edge. Linguistic analysis of tense — establishes how tense functions, both accounts accepting the semantics and disputing the metaphysics.

**Narrowing recorded.** The preferred foliation narrows the reach: a present stands by stipulation rather than by detection. Growing block narrows the reach: existence stands for past and present rather than all times.

**Progress.** The exclusion of a frame-independent present. The tensed semantics, agreed by both accounts. The mapped responses to the truthmaker objection.

**Own record.**

## 107 The effectiveness of mathematics

**Conditions prior.** The statement holds across readings. The terms have determinate reference. The accounts hold their truth value while tested. A procedure repeated returns the same outcome independently of what precedes it. Nothing prior enters.

**Statement.** Mathematics developed for its own reasons turns out to describe physical phenomena precisely. Why?

**The field's account of its hardness.**

**Accounts.** Selection and construction: mathematics is developed in continual contact with physical problems, the branches that apply were often built for application, and the successes are salient while the vast unapplied remainder is not counted.

The selection account does not cover the striking cases: non-Euclidean geometry, group theory, and complex analysis were developed with no physical application in view and later proved indispensable, and predictive success far beyond the data used to construct the theory is not accounted for by contact or selection.

**Specifications.** Mathematics and the physical world are two domains — local. Applicability is a relation between them calling for explanation — local.

**Specifications withdrawn.**

**Common requirement.** That the applicability is a phenomenon with an explanation.

**Conserving relation.** An account of why the relation obtains, standing across the cases including the striking ones.

**Conserving reach.** All instances of application.

**Reach.** The statement quantifies over why the relation obtains. Instances of application, and the historical record of their development, are what is measured. The two are not the same.

**Unreachability.** Both accounts read the same historical record differently, and platonism absorbs the applicability and inherits the access problem. The account is not preserved across the cases by instances and the historical record.

**Persistence.** An account covering the striking cases that both accounts accept, or agreement on what the historical record shows.

**Addresses.** Keyed property by property.

*The report.*

**Approaches.** Selection effect accounting — accounts for many cases, both accounts disputing whether it covers the striking ones, the dispute being over a historical record that is available and read differently. Structural realism — the world having mathematical structure and theories capturing it, accounting for applicability by making it constitutive, what a structure without relata is remaining contested. Mathematical naturalism — mathematics abstracted from physical structure, facing the branches with no physical origin. Platonism — mathematics describing an independent realm the world instantiates, the access problem being its standing difficulty. Deflation — the question held rhetorical, its critics holding the striking cases are specific and documented.

**Narrowing recorded.** Structural realism weakens the relation: applicability stands constitutive rather than accounted for. Mathematical naturalism narrows the reach: the account stands for branches with physical origin.

**Progress.** The documented cases of application without prior physical motivation. The selection accounting for the cases it covers. The access problem, stated.

**Own record.**

## 108 The Gibbs paradox

**Conditions prior.** The statement holds across readings. The terms have determinate reference. The accounts hold their truth value while tested. A procedure repeated returns the same outcome independently of what precedes it. Nothing prior enters.

**Statement.** Mixing two different gases raises entropy. Mixing a gas with itself does not. The change is discontinuous as the gases are made more alike. Why?

**The field's account of its hardness.**

**Accounts.** Indistinguishability is fundamental: identical particles are not merely similar but the same kind, the correct counting divides by the permutations, quantum mechanics supplies this, and the discontinuity is real because identity is not a matter of degree.

The entropy of mixing depends on what can be separated by available operations, so it is relative to the capacity to discriminate: the paradox arises from treating entropy as a property of the system alone rather than of the description, and the discontinuity is a discontinuity in the description.

**Specifications.** Entropy is a property of a system — local. Particle identity is determinate — universal under quantum mechanics.

**Specifications withdrawn.**

**Common requirement.** That there is a fact about the entropy change on mixing.

**Conserving relation.** Entropy determined as a property of the system, the determination standing independently of the operations available.

**Conserving reach.** All mixings, including those interpolating between the two cases.

**Reach.** The statement quantifies over whether entropy is a property of the system or of the description. Measurable work extractable given available operations is what is measured. The two are not the same.

**Unreachability.** The information-theoretic treatment reproduces every measurement and relocates entropy to the description; the resource-theoretic formulation states the relativity rather than removing it; and particles continuously interpolating between two species are not available, so the discontinuity is not directly probed. The determination is not reached over the mixings by extractable work.

**Persistence.** A measurement of entropy independent of the operations available, or a direct probe of the interpolating case.

**Addresses.** Keyed property by property.

*The report.*

**Approaches.** Quantum indistinguishability — supplies the correct counting and the discontinuity, its critics noting classical statistical mechanics needed the division before quantum theory supplied it. The information-theoretic treatment — makes the entropy relative to available operations, reproducing every measurement, the disagreement being over whether entropy is thereby subjective. Isotope experiments — separable isotopes giving mixing entropy, the separation capacity being what changed historically, supporting the operational reading. Continuous interpolation — constructing particles continuously interpolating between two species not available for real particles, so the discontinuity is not directly probed. Resource-theoretic thermodynamics — makes the operation set explicit and derives entropy relative to it, a formal advance stating the relativity rather than removing it.

**Narrowing recorded.** Resource-theoretic thermodynamics narrows the reach: entropy stands derived relative to a stated operation set. The information-theoretic treatment weakens the relation: entropy stands as a property of the description rather than of the system.

**Progress.** The correct counting and the measured discontinuity. The isotope results and the historical change in separation capacity. The explicit operation-set formulation.

**Own record.**

## 109 The surprise examination

**Conditions prior.** The statement holds across readings. The terms have determinate reference. The accounts hold their truth value while tested. A procedure repeated returns the same outcome independently of what precedes it. Nothing prior enters.

**Statement.** An examination will be held next week and its day will be a surprise. The students argue it cannot happen. It happens, and is a surprise. Where is the error?

**The field's account of its hardness.**

**Accounts.** The backward induction is invalid: the announcement's truth conditions involve the students' knowledge, and after the argument they no longer know the exam will occur, so the eliminating step fails at the first move.

No consensus account exists: epistemic, temporal-retention, and self-referential analyses each block the argument at a different step, each has counterexamples in the literature, and the paradox has been reformulated repeatedly to evade each solution.

**Specifications.** The announcement is known to be true — local. Knowledge persists through the reasoning — local. Surprise is definable in terms of what is known — local.

**Specifications withdrawn.**

**Common requirement.** That there is a determinate error in the argument.

**Conserving relation.** An error located in the argument, the location standing against reformulations.

**Conserving reach.** All formulations of the argument.

**Reach.** The statement quantifies over the status of a self-referential knowledge claim. Formal reconstructions, each with its own assumptions, are what is measured. The two are not the same.

**Unreachability.** Each proposed solution has been met by a reformulation restoring the puzzle, documented over seventy years. The location is not preserved across the formulations by any reconstruction.

**Persistence.** A location of the error standing against the reformulations, or agreement on which assumption the reformulations must drop.

**Addresses.** Keyed property by property.

*The report.*

**Approaches.** Epistemic blocking — the announcement not knowable in the relevant sense, formalised, the reformulations restoring the puzzle with weaker assumptions. Temporal retention analysis — knowledge not retained through the eliminations, blocking the induction, whether the failure is principled disputed. Self-reference analysis — treats the announcement as a Moore-type sentence, illuminating and not settling which step fails. Game-theoretic treatment — models it as a strategic interaction with mixed strategies, changing the problem, which its authors note. Denying the phenomenon — the surprise held not to be the announced one, contested.

**Narrowing recorded.** Game-theoretic treatment narrows the reach: the account stands for a strategic formulation its authors distinguish from the original. Epistemic blocking narrows the reach: the solution stands for formulations with the stronger knowledge assumption.

**Progress.** The seventy-year record of solutions and the reformulations meeting each. The formal reconstructions and their stated assumptions.

**Own record.**

## 110 Determinism and randomness

**Conditions prior.** The statement holds across readings. The terms have determinate reference. The accounts hold their truth value while tested. A procedure repeated returns the same outcome independently of what precedes it. Nothing prior enters.

**Statement.** Are outcomes fixed by prior conditions, or genuinely chancy?

**The field's account of its hardness.**

**Accounts.** Chancy: quantum measurement outcomes are unpredictable in principle, not merely in practice, and no local account restoring determinism reproduces the correlations.

Determinism remains available: deterministic interpretations reproduce every prediction, apparent randomness is compatible with underlying determinism, and no measurement distinguishes them.

**Specifications.** An outcome is either fixed by prior conditions or not — local. Unpredictability indicates chance — local.

**Specifications withdrawn.**

**Common requirement.** That there is a fact about whether the outcome was fixed.

**Conserving relation.** A determination of whether prior conditions fix outcomes, standing on observed frequencies.

**Conserving reach.** All outcomes the question is asked of.

**Reach.** The statement quantifies over whether prior conditions fix outcomes. Observed frequencies, which both accounts reproduce, are what is measured. The two are not the same.

**Unreachability.** Statistical tests provably cannot distinguish deterministic chaos or hidden determination from chance, and the deterministic interpretations are empirically equivalent by construction. The determination is not reached over the outcomes by observed frequencies.

**Persistence.** A measurement distinguishing fixed from chancy outcomes, which the empirical equivalence excludes.

**Addresses.** Keyed property by property.

*The report.*

**Approaches.** Statistical tests for randomness — confirm the distributions and cannot distinguish deterministic chaos or hidden determination from chance, a general result about such tests. Bell tests — exclude local determination, nonlocal determination surviving. Algorithmic randomness — defines randomness precisely for sequences, applying to sequences and not to what produced them. Deterministic interpretations — empirically equivalent by construction. Chaos in classical systems — establishes that unpredictability does not imply indeterminism, accepted by both accounts.

**Narrowing recorded.** Bell tests narrow the reach: local determination stands excluded, nonlocal determination surviving. Algorithmic randomness narrows the reach: randomness stands defined for sequences rather than for what produced them.

**Progress.** The exclusion of local determination. The general result on statistical tests. The precise definition of randomness for sequences. The classical result that unpredictability does not imply indeterminism.

**Own record.**

## 111 Qualia and the zombie argument

**Conditions prior.** The statement holds across readings. The terms have determinate reference. The accounts hold their truth value while tested. A procedure repeated returns the same outcome independently of what precedes it. Nothing prior enters.

**Statement.** Could there be a being physically identical to a person with no experience at all? If it is conceivable, what follows?

**The field's account of its hardness.** The field's founding statement: what makes the hard problem hard and almost unique is that it goes beyond problems about the performance of functions — even when the cognitive and behavioural functions in the vicinity of experience are explained, a further question remains, why their performance is accompanied by experience, a simple explanation of the functions leaving it open, and no analogous further question standing in the explanation of genes, of life, or of learning.

**Accounts.** Conceivability supports possibility here, and possibility refutes physicalism: the argument is deductive, its premises are examinable, and no defect has been generally agreed on despite three decades of attention.

Conceivability does not entail possibility: water without H₂O is conceivable and impossible, the a posteriori necessity cases show the inference fails in exactly the structurally similar cases, and the conceivability may reflect a limitation in how the physical is represented rather than a metaphysical fact.

**Specifications.** Conceivability bears on possibility — local. Physical duplication fixes all physical facts — local.

**Specifications withdrawn.**

**Common requirement.** That the physical facts either fix the experiential ones or do not.

**Conserving relation.** A determination of whether the physical facts fix the experiential ones, standing on what is examinable.

**Conserving reach.** The modal relation between the two sets of facts.

**Reach.** The statement quantifies over a modal relation between two sets of facts. Actual systems are what is measured. The two are not the same.

**Unreachability.** The two-dimensional framework and the phenomenal concept strategy are technical and unresolved, the strategy facing a dilemma stated precisely by both accounts: either it fails to account for the concepts physically or it concedes the point. The determination is not reached over the modal relation by actual systems.

**Persistence.** A resolution of the conceivability-possibility inference for this case accepted by both accounts.

**Addresses.** Hardness: Chalmers, "Facing Up to the Problem of Consciousness", Journal of Consciousness Studies 2(3):200-219, 1995, §3. The explanatory-gap term: Levine 1983, as cited there. Remaining properties: keyed at the next pass.

*The report.*

**Approaches.** Two-dimensional semantics — a framework separating the senses in which the water case differs, contested in detail, the contest technical and unresolved. Phenomenal concept strategy — accounts for the conceivability by the special character of phenomenal concepts, preserving physicalism, its critics arguing the strategy either fails to account for the concepts physically or concedes the point. Denying the conceivability — zombies held not coherently conceivable on reflection, a minority position, defended. Denying the entailment — the main physicalist route, resting on the a posteriori necessity cases, which the two-dimensional framework addresses. Empirical work — bears on which functions are physical, the argument being about what follows if all of them are.

**Narrowing recorded.** The phenomenal concept strategy weakens the relation: the conceivability stands accounted for by the character of the concepts rather than the modal relation determined.

**Progress.** The three decades of examination without an agreed defect. The a posteriori necessity cases. The stated dilemma against the phenomenal concept strategy.

**Own record.**

## 112 The quantum-classical boundary

**Conditions prior.** The statement holds across readings. The terms have determinate reference. The accounts hold their truth value while tested. A procedure repeated returns the same outcome independently of what precedes it. Nothing prior enters.

**Statement.** Small systems show superposition; large ones do not. Where is the boundary and what sets it?

**The field's account of its hardness.**

**Accounts.** There is no boundary, only decoherence rates: superposition persists at all scales and becomes unobservable as environmental coupling grows, and interference has been demonstrated for progressively larger molecules with no scale-dependent break found.

A boundary may exist: collapse models predict a scale at which superposition genuinely fails, the experiments so far have excluded parameter ranges without excluding the class, and absence of a break at reached masses is not absence of a break.

**Specifications.** The boundary is either a matter of degree or a real transition — local. Interference experiments probe it — local.

**Specifications withdrawn.**

**Common requirement.** That the question of a break has a determinate answer.

**Conserving relation.** A determination of whether a break occurs, standing at the masses the question concerns.

**Conserving reach.** All masses, those not yet reached included.

**Reach.** The statement quantifies over behaviour at masses not yet reached. Masses reached, currently large molecules and mechanical oscillators, are what is measured. The two are not the same.

**Unreachability.** Interference at larger masses returns more of the reached domain, and collapse constraints narrow parameter ranges without excluding the class; decoherence accounts quantitatively for every case measured, which both accounts accept while disputing whether anything remains. The determination is not reached over the masses by measurements at masses reached.

**Persistence.** An interference measurement at a mass a collapse model predicts a break at, or a detection of collapse.

**Addresses.** Keyed property by property.

*The report.*

**Approaches.** Interference at larger masses — demonstrated to tens of thousands of atomic mass units, the frontier advancing, returning more of the reached domain. Optomechanical superposition — prepares mechanical oscillators in non-classical states, the masses remaining far below the proposed collapse scales. Collapse model constraints — bounded by interferometry, X-ray emission, and gravitational-wave detector noise, narrowing without closing. Decoherence calculation — accounts quantitatively for the disappearance of interference in every case measured, accepted by both accounts and disputed as to whether anything remains. Gravitationally induced collapse — proposals giving a scale, experiments to reach it proposed and not yet performed.

**Narrowing recorded.** Collapse model constraints narrow the reach: the exclusion stands over the parameter ranges bounded rather than over the class.

**Progress.** The interference demonstrated to tens of thousands of atomic mass units. The non-classical states prepared in mechanical oscillators. The bounded collapse parameter ranges. The quantitative decoherence accounting.

**Own record.**

## 113 Explore and exploit

**Conditions prior.** The statement holds across readings. The terms have determinate reference. The accounts hold their truth value while tested. A procedure repeated returns the same outcome independently of what precedes it. Nothing prior enters.

**Statement.** Acting on what is known yields returns now; trying alternatives yields information. How much of each?

**The field's account of its hardness.**

**Accounts.** The trade-off is solved for well-posed cases: an index policy is optimal for a class of bandit problems, and regret bounds for algorithms are proved and matched by lower bounds.

**Specifications.** The options are enumerated — local. The environment's statistics are stationary or their variation is specified — local. A horizon is given — local.

**Specifications withdrawn.**

**Common requirement.** That there is a well-defined optimum to approach.

**Conserving relation.** An optimum defined, standing where the option set and environment are not specified in advance.

**Conserving reach.** All settings the trade-off is asked of.

**Reach.** The statement quantifies over action where the option set and environment are not specified in advance. Specified problems with stated priors and horizons are what is measured. The two are not the same.

**Unreachability.** Index optimality holds under stated restrictive conditions, and regret is measured against the best fixed option in hindsight, which presupposes the option set; non-stationary bounds hold under a supplied variation budget. The optimum is not defined over the unspecified settings by results on specified problems.

**Persistence.** An optimum defined where the option set is not enumerated in advance.

**Addresses.** Keyed property by property.

*The report.*

**Approaches.** The index policy — optimal for discounted independent-arm problems, the conditions restrictive and stated. Regret minimisation — bounds proved and matched, regret measured against the best fixed option in hindsight, which presupposes the option set. Non-stationary and adversarial extensions — bounds available under stated variation budgets, the budget being supplied. Bayesian optimal control — defines the optimum given a prior, the prior over unknown option sets being the difficulty. Human and animal studies — find behaviour close to certain heuristics, establishing what is done rather than what is optimal in unspecified environments.

**Narrowing recorded.** The index policy narrows the reach: optimality stands for discounted independent-arm problems under stated conditions. Non-stationary extensions narrow the reach: bounds stand under a supplied variation budget. Regret minimisation narrows the reach: the measure stands against a presupposed option set.

**Progress.** The proved index optimality under its conditions. The matched regret bounds. The bounds under stated variation budgets. The measured proximity of behaviour to specific heuristics.

**Own record.**

## 114 Self and non-self in immunity

**Conditions prior.** The statement holds across readings. The terms have determinate reference. The accounts hold their truth value while tested. A procedure repeated returns the same outcome independently of what precedes it. Nothing prior enters.

**Statement.** The immune system attacks foreign things and not the body. How does it tell which is which?

**The field's account of its hardness.**

**Accounts.** By tolerance to self: lymphocytes reactive to self are deleted or suppressed during development, and the mechanisms — central and peripheral tolerance, regulatory cells — are identified and manipulable.

Self-non-self does not describe what is observed: the gut microbiota is foreign and tolerated, the fetus is foreign and tolerated, tumours are self and attacked, and responses are triggered by tissue damage signals rather than by foreignness.

**Specifications.** There is a distinction the system implements — local. The distinction is between self and foreign — local.

**Specifications withdrawn.**

**Common requirement.** That the system's discrimination has a describable basis.

**Conserving relation.** A criterion the system discriminates on, the criterion standing across the anomalous cases.

**Conserving reach.** All responses, the microbiota, fetal, and tumour cases included.

**Reach.** The statement quantifies over what the system discriminates on. Responses to defined antigens under defined conditions are what is measured, each consistent with more than one criterion. The two are not the same.

**Unreachability.** Tolerance and damage accounts each account for their own anomalies, and the damage model's trigger is defined partly by what triggers responses. The criterion is not preserved across the cases by responses consistent with more than one.

**Persistence.** A criterion accounting for the anomalous cases without being defined by what it accounts for.

**Addresses.** Keyed property by property.

*The report.*

**Approaches.** Tolerance mechanisms — central and peripheral tolerance identified and demonstrated, establishing mechanisms, the microbiota and fetal cases remaining. The danger model — accounts for the anomalies by damage signals, its critics noting damage signals are themselves defined partly by what triggers responses. Pattern recognition receptors — identify conserved microbial patterns, establishing a foreignness-detecting mechanism, alongside self-derived ligands for the same receptors. Tumour immunology — responses to self-derived neoantigens, supporting a criterion other than foreignness, the neoantigens also being mutated and so partly foreign. Microbiome tolerance studies — establish active tolerance of foreign organisms with mechanisms identified, extending the account rather than settling the criterion.

**Narrowing recorded.** Microbiome tolerance studies narrow the reach: active tolerance stands established for the foreign organisms studied. Pattern recognition receptors narrow the reach: foreignness detection stands for the conserved patterns identified.

**Progress.** The identified tolerance mechanisms. The identified pattern receptors and their self-derived ligands. The documented microbiota and fetal tolerance. The neoantigen responses.

**Own record.**

## 115 Cancer, the somatic mutation account and its rival

**Conditions prior.** The statement holds across readings. The terms have determinate reference. The accounts hold their truth value while tested. A procedure repeated returns the same outcome independently of what precedes it. Nothing prior enters.

**Statement.** Cancer arises when cells proliferate uncontrollably. Does the fault lie in the cell's own mutations, or in the tissue that organises it?

**The field's account of its hardness.**

**Accounts.** Somatic mutation: cancer is driven by accumulated mutations in oncogenes and tumour suppressors, driver mutations are catalogued across tumour types, targeted therapies against specific drivers work, and heritable mutation in the same genes raises risk.
