Tissue organisation: carcinogenesis is a tissue-level failure of the constraints normally imposed on proliferation, tissue recombination experiments show carcinogen-exposed stroma with normal epithelium produces tumours and the converse does not, and normal tissue can normalise malignant cells.

**Specifications.** The causal locus is either the cell or its tissue context — local. Identifying it determines the intervention — local.

**Specifications withdrawn.**

**Common requirement.** That there is a level at which the initiating fault sits.

**Conserving relation.** The initiating level identified, the identification standing on measurement in established tumours.

**Conserving reach.** All tumours the question is asked of.

**Reach.** The statement quantifies over which level initiates. Tumours already established, in which both cellular and tissue changes are present, are what is measured. The two are not the same.

**Unreachability.** Driver catalogues establish drivers without establishing initiation, tissue recombination supports the tissue account in the systems tested, and sequencing of normal tissue finds driver mutations at high density without cancer, which constrains the dominant account from inside and is accepted in the field. The identification is not reached over the tumours by measurements in established ones.

**Persistence.** A measurement at initiation separating the two levels, or agreement that both operate and the question is their order.

**Addresses.** Keyed property by property.

*The report.*

**Approaches.** Sequencing driver mutations — catalogued comprehensively, with targeted therapy successes and near-universal resistance, establishing drivers without establishing initiation. Tissue recombination experiments — support the tissue account in the systems tested, system-specific and not generally replicated across tissues. Normalisation experiments — malignant cells behaving normally in normal microenvironments in specific models, establishing context dependence. Mutations in normal tissue — sequencing of normal skin and oesophagus finding driver mutations at high density without cancer, constraining the mutation account substantially, accepted in the field. Field cancerisation and immune surveillance — accommodated by both accounts.

**Narrowing recorded.** Tissue recombination narrows the reach: the result stands for the systems tested. Normalisation experiments narrow the reach: context dependence stands in the specific models.

**Progress.** The driver catalogues and the targeted therapy responses with their resistance record. The tissue recombination and normalisation results. The measured driver burden in normal tissue.

**Own record.**

## 116 Addiction

**Conditions prior.** The statement holds across readings. The terms have determinate reference. The accounts hold their truth value while tested. A procedure repeated returns the same outcome independently of what precedes it. Nothing prior enters.

**Statement.** People continue a behaviour that harms them and say they want to stop. Is this a disease of the brain or a choice under bad conditions?

**The field's account of its hardness.**

**Accounts.** A brain disease: repeated exposure produces durable changes in reward and control circuits, the changes are imaged and dose-dependent, heritability is substantial, and treating it as a disease has reduced stigma and improved access to care.

The disease framing does not fit the data on recovery: most people who meet criteria recover without treatment, behaviour responds strongly to incentives including in randomised contingency-management trials, and rates track economic and social conditions in ways a brain disease does not predict.

**Specifications.** The behaviour has a locus — neural pathology or choice under constraint — local. Identifying it determines the response — local.

**Specifications withdrawn.**

**Common requirement.** That the behaviour is produced by an identifiable mechanism at one level.

**Conserving relation.** The governing level identified, the identification standing on measures altered in affected people.

**Conserving reach.** All affected people the question is asked of.

**Reach.** The statement quantifies over which account governs the behaviour. Brain measures and behavioural measures, both altered in affected people, are what is measured. The two are not the same.

**Unreachability.** Imaging does not separate cause from consequence, which the field states, and remission rates and incentive responsiveness are read oppositely by the two accounts. The identification is not reached over the affected by brain and behavioural measures.

**Persistence.** A measurement separating cause from consequence, or agreement that both operate and the question is their proportion.

**Addresses.** Keyed property by property.

*The report.*

**Approaches.** Neuroimaging — documents circuit changes reliably, cause and consequence not separated by the imaging, which the field states. Contingency management trials — large abstinence effects with modest incentives, establishing responsiveness to consequences, accommodated by both accounts with difficulty on one side. Epidemiology of recovery — high rates of remission without treatment, measured, constraining chronic-disease framings. Animal models — establish neuroadaptation, environmental enrichment substantially reducing self-administration, supporting the conditions account. Genetic studies — substantial heritability, with small individual effects and no identified pathology.

**Narrowing recorded.** Animal models narrow the reach: neuroadaptation stands established in the models, with enrichment effects measured there. Contingency management narrows the reach: the responsiveness stands demonstrated under the incentive schedules trialled.

**Progress.** The imaged dose-dependent circuit changes. The measured abstinence effects under incentives. The measured remission rates without treatment. The enrichment effects in animal models. The heritability estimates without identified pathology.

**Own record.**

## 117 Niche and neutral theory

**Conditions prior.** The statement holds across readings. The terms have determinate reference. The accounts hold their truth value while tested. A procedure repeated returns the same outcome independently of what precedes it. Nothing prior enters.

**Statement.** Do species coexist because they differ in what they do, or because they are approximately equivalent and abundance drifts?

**The field's account of its hardness.**

**Accounts.** Niche differences: coexistence requires stabilising differences, demonstrated experimentally by invasion tests, and species do differ measurably in resource use, timing, and enemies.

Neutrality fits the patterns: neutral theory reproduces species-abundance distributions and species-area relationships with no niche differences at all, and fits many datasets as well as or better than niche models.

**Specifications.** Community structure is determined either by differences or by drift — local. Abundance patterns discriminate between them — local.

**Specifications withdrawn.**

**Common requirement.** That the observed patterns bear on the mechanism.

**Conserving relation.** The mechanism producing community structure identified, the identification standing on abundance and distribution data.

**Conserving reach.** All communities the question is asked of.

**Reach.** The statement quantifies over the mechanism producing community structure. Abundance and distribution data, which both models fit, are what is measured. The two are not the same.

**Unreachability.** Both models fit abundance distributions, established early and not resolved by more data of the same kind, and the continuum framing accommodates both without measuring weights. The identification is not reached over the communities by abundance and distribution data.

**Persistence.** Measured weights for the stabilising and equalising mechanisms in natural communities, or a prediction on which the two models differ and a test discriminating them.

**Addresses.** Keyed property by property.

*The report.*

**Approaches.** Fitting abundance distributions — both fit, established early and not resolved by more data of the same kind. Invasion experiments — demonstrate stabilising differences where performed, species-specific and laborious. The partition of stabilising from equalising mechanisms — makes both measurable in principle, measurement in diverse natural communities partial. Dynamic predictions — neutral and niche models differing in predicted temporal turnover, tests giving mixed results and sensitive to timescale. Continuum framing — most current work treating both as operating together, fitting, the weights not independently measured.

**Narrowing recorded.** Invasion experiments narrow the reach: stabilising differences stand demonstrated for the species pairs tested.

**Progress.** The fits of both models to abundance distributions. The demonstrated stabilising differences where invasion tests were performed. The partition making both mechanism classes measurable in principle.

**Own record.**

## 118 Ecological stability and diversity

**Conditions prior.** The statement holds across readings. The terms have determinate reference. The accounts hold their truth value while tested. A procedure repeated returns the same outcome independently of what precedes it. Nothing prior enters.

**Statement.** Do more diverse communities resist disturbance better?

**The field's account of its hardness.**

**Accounts.** Yes: long-running grassland experiments that manipulate diversity directly find more diverse plots more stable in aggregate biomass across years, more resistant to drought, and faster to recover from it, replicating across continents and supported by separately measured mechanisms — asynchrony in species responses and complementarity in resource use.

Not in general: randomly assembled complex systems are provably less stable, not more, and the empirical result is for aggregate properties — individual species populations are often less stable in diverse communities, so what is stable depends on which variable is measured.

**Specifications.** Stability is a property of a community — local. Diversity is a variable that can be manipulated independently — local.

**Specifications withdrawn.**

**Common requirement.** That stability has a definition both accounts accept.

**Conserving relation.** A stability that rises with diversity, the rise standing across the variables measured.

**Conserving reach.** All the stability variables the question is asked of.

**Reach.** The statement quantifies over which stability the question is about, the concepts diverging. Specific variables — aggregate biomass, population variance, recovery time, resistance — are what is measured, and they move in different directions. The two are not the same.

**Unreachability.** Aggregate stability rises with diversity and population stability does not, in the same experiments, on the same plots; and multiple stability concepts formalised show the answer differs by concept. The rise is not preserved across the variables by the measurements.

**Persistence.** A stability concept both accounts accept, applied across the variables.

**Addresses.** Keyed property by property.

*The report.*

**Approaches.** Grassland manipulation experiments — aggregate stability rising with diversity, robustly, population-level stability not, measured in the same experiments. Random-matrix analysis — proves instability for random complex systems, real communities not being randomly assembled, which is the standing response. Structured network models — find stability depends on interaction structure, establishing that structure matters and supplying no general answer. Portfolio effects — statistical averaging accounting for part of the aggregate result without any ecological mechanism, measured and partial. Definitional work — multiple stability concepts formalised, clarifying the dispute and showing the answer differs by concept.

**Narrowing recorded.** Definitional work narrows the reach: an answer stands per stability concept rather than across them. Portfolio effects narrow the reach: part of the aggregate result stands accounted for by statistical averaging.

**Progress.** The measured aggregate stability rise across continents. The measured population-level result in the same experiments. The proved instability of random complex systems. The quantified portfolio contribution.

**Own record.**

## 119 The Red Queen

**Conditions prior.** The statement holds across readings. The terms have determinate reference. The accounts hold their truth value while tested. A procedure repeated returns the same outcome independently of what precedes it. Nothing prior enters.

**Statement.** Species keep adapting and their relative position does not improve. Is adaptation getting them anywhere?

**The field's account of its hardness.**

**Accounts.** Coevolution keeps them in place: antagonistic coevolution means each improvement is met, so extinction risk stays roughly constant with age, as the founding observation from fossil survivorship curves recorded.

The pattern has other explanations: age-independent extinction arises from environmental change and from taxonomic artefacts as well, and the coevolutionary interpretation is one among several fitting the same survivorship curves.

**Specifications.** The survivorship pattern indicates a mechanism — local. Taxa are comparable units — local.

**Specifications withdrawn.** The founding age-independence stands rejected as the norm by the corrected survivorship analyses, the taxonomic level of the original curves accounting for the discrepancy, and the coevolutionary account continues partly detached from the pattern it was built to account for.

**Common requirement.** That the fossil pattern bears on the mechanism.

**Conserving relation.** A mechanism identified as producing the survivorship pattern, the identification standing on fossil ranges.

**Conserving reach.** All taxa the pattern is measured over.

**Reach.** The statement quantifies over what produces constant extinction risk. Fossil ranges, with sampling and taxonomic definition affecting the curves, are what is measured. The two are not the same.

**Unreachability.** Several mechanisms fit the same survivorship curves, genus definitions affect the curves, and comparative analyses find environmental drivers dominating some intervals with weights unmeasured. The identification is not reached over the taxa by fossil ranges.

**Persistence.** Measured weights for coevolutionary and environmental drivers, or a survivorship pattern only one mechanism fits.

**Addresses.** Keyed property by property.

*The report.*

**Approaches.** Survivorship analysis — the founding pattern of apparent age-independence at higher taxonomic levels reversed by improved analyses, with age-dependent extinction widespread at species level, typically most intense among young taxa, and age-independence now the exception; the analysis continues on the corrected curves. Host-parasite coevolution experiments — demonstrate arms-race dynamics directly in microbial systems, establishing the mechanism at short timescales. Molecular evolution of interacting genes — rapid evolution at host-parasite interfaces, measured, supporting coevolution locally. Comparative extinction analyses — environmental drivers dominating some intervals, both mechanisms operating, weights unmeasured. Taxonomic artefact analysis — genus definitions affecting the curves, constraining the original inference.

**Narrowing recorded.** Coevolution experiments narrow the reach: the mechanism stands demonstrated at short timescales in microbial systems.

**Progress.** The corrected survivorship curves and the reversal of the founding pattern. The demonstrated arms-race dynamics. The measured rapid evolution at host-parasite interfaces. The quantified effect of taxonomic definition on the curves.

**Own record.**

## 120 The hygiene hypothesis

**Conditions prior.** The statement holds across readings. The terms have determinate reference. The accounts hold their truth value while tested. A procedure repeated returns the same outcome independently of what precedes it. Nothing prior enters.

**Statement.** Allergic and autoimmune conditions rose sharply in industrialised populations over decades. Why?

**The field's account of its hardness.**

**Accounts.** Reduced early microbial exposure: farm-childhood studies show large protective effects, the mechanisms are traceable to immune regulation, and the timing and geography of the rise match changes in exposure.

The original framing does not fit: infections in early life often raise rather than lower allergy risk, the effect is better attributed to microbiota composition and to specific exposures than to hygiene, and antibiotic use, diet, vitamin D, pollution, and diagnostic change all track the same period.

**Specifications.** The rise has a dominant cause — local. Population-level correlations identify it — local.

**Specifications withdrawn.**

**Common requirement.** That the rise is attributable.

**Conserving relation.** A dominant cause identified, the identification standing among co-varying exposures.

**Conserving reach.** The rise across the industrialised populations.

**Reach.** The statement quantifies over a dominant cause, identified from correlations among co-varying exposures. Observational cohorts with many co-varying exposures, and a small number of trials, are what is measured. The two are not the same.

**Unreachability.** Confounding by lifestyle is acknowledged in the strongest comparisons, microbiota associations narrow without establishing causality in humans, and several exposures track the same period. The identification is not reached over the rise by cohorts with co-varying exposures.

**Persistence.** A trial establishing causality for an exposure sufficient to account for the rise, or measured weights across the co-varying exposures.

**Addresses.** Keyed property by property.

*The report.*

**Approaches.** Farm and community comparisons — large, consistent protective associations with identified mechanistic differences, the strongest evidence, confounding by lifestyle acknowledged. Microbiota studies — associate composition with outcomes and identify plausible mechanisms, causality in humans largely unestablished. Helminth and probiotic trials — mixed results, with some positive findings in specific conditions. The early peanut introduction trial — randomised, large effect, reversing a prior guideline, establishing causality for one exposure and one outcome. Diagnostic-change analyses — account for part of the measured rise, quantified.

**Narrowing recorded.** The peanut trial narrows the reach: causality stands established for one exposure and one outcome. Diagnostic-change analyses narrow the reach: part of the measured rise stands accounted for.

**Progress.** The measured protective associations and their mechanistic correlates. The established causality for one exposure and outcome, with the adolescent follow-up standing: tolerance persisting to age twelve irrespective of subsequent consumption. The quantified diagnostic contribution to the measured rise.

**Own record.**

## 121 Hormesis

**Conditions prior.** The statement holds across readings. The terms have determinate reference. The accounts hold their truth value while tested. A procedure repeated returns the same outcome independently of what precedes it. Nothing prior enters.

**Statement.** Some agents harmful at high dose appear beneficial at low dose. Is the dose-response curve monotonic or not?

**The field's account of its hardness.**

**Accounts.** Non-monotonic responses are real and common: documented across thousands of studies in toxicology and pharmacology, with mechanisms — adaptive stress responses, receptor dynamics — identified in specific cases.

For regulation the default should remain linear at low dose: low-dose effects are near the limit of detection, publication favours positive findings, and adopting a non-monotonic default would relax exposure limits on evidence heterogeneous in quality.

**Specifications.** A dose-response relation exists for an agent and an endpoint — universal under toxicology. Low-dose behaviour is estimable — local.

**Specifications withdrawn.**

**Common requirement.** That there is a true low-dose response.

**Conserving relation.** A dose-response form determined at low dose, the determination standing on measurement.

**Conserving reach.** Doses near or below detection.

**Reach.** The statement quantifies over response at doses near or below detection. Doses with detectable effects, extrapolated downward by model, are what is measured. The two are not the same.

**Unreachability.** Rival dose-response models fit comparably in the observable range and diverge below it, which is where the question lies; and confounding dominates epidemiology at the relevant magnitudes, which is measured. The determination is not reached over the low doses by measurements at detectable ones.

**Persistence.** A measurement at the doses of regulatory interest, or agreement on a criterion for extrapolating below detection.

**Addresses.** Keyed property by property.

*The report.*

**Approaches.** Database assembly — large collections of non-monotonic curves, heterogeneous in endpoint and quality, which both accounts accept. Mechanistic studies — establish adaptive responses in specific systems, generality to other agents and endpoints being the open question. Large low-dose animal studies — increase power at some cost, the doses of regulatory interest remaining below the reachable range for many agents. Epidemiology at low exposure — confounding dominating at the relevant magnitudes, which is measured. Model selection — linear-no-threshold, threshold, and non-monotonic models fitting comparably in the observable range and diverging below it.

**Narrowing recorded.** Mechanistic studies narrow the reach: adaptive responses stand established in specific systems. Large animal studies narrow the reach: the reachable dose range stands extended without reaching regulatory magnitudes.

**Progress.** The assembled curve collections with their stated heterogeneity. The mechanisms established in specific systems. The measured confounding at low exposure. The comparable fits in the observable range.

**Own record.**

## 122 The dodo bird verdict

**Conditions prior.** The statement holds across readings. The terms have determinate reference. The accounts hold their truth value while tested. A procedure repeated returns the same outcome independently of what precedes it. Nothing prior enters.

**Statement.** Different psychotherapies produce similar outcomes. Do the specific techniques do anything?

**The field's account of its hardness.**

**Accounts.** Common factors dominate: meta-analyses across decades find small differences between bona fide therapies, and alliance, expectation, and therapist effects account for more variance than technique.

Equivalence is partly an artefact: some specific treatments outperform others for specific conditions — exposure for phobias, and specific protocols for obsessive-compulsive disorder — and averaging across conditions conceals this, allegiance effects also inflating apparent equivalence in comparative trials.

**Specifications.** Outcome is attributable to components of the treatment — local. Trials isolate components — local.

**Specifications withdrawn.**

**Common requirement.** That the contribution of technique is separable from context.

**Conserving relation.** The contribution of technique separated from context, the separation standing across conditions.

**Conserving reach.** All conditions the comparison is made over.

**Reach.** The statement quantifies over the contribution of technique. Whole treatments delivered in contexts, compared, are what is measured. The two are not the same.

**Unreachability.** Averaging across conditions conceals specific superiority, which is the disputed step, allegiance effects are substantial and measured, and controls establish that therapies exceed no treatment without separating technique from context. The separation is not reached over the conditions by comparisons of whole treatments.

**Persistence.** A design separating technique from context, or agreement on the level at which the comparison should be made.

**Addresses.** Keyed property by property.

*The report.*

**Approaches.** Comparative meta-analysis — small average differences, averaging across conditions being the disputed step. Dismantling studies — remove components and measure change, some showing specific components matter and many underpowered, which is documented. Condition-specific comparisons — show specific superiority for several disorders, accepted by both accounts. Allegiance analyses — quantify researcher allegiance effects, substantial and measured. Placebo and waitlist controls — establish therapies exceed no treatment, without separating technique from context.

**Narrowing recorded.** Condition-specific comparisons narrow the reach: superiority stands established for the disorders compared. Dismantling studies narrow the reach: component contributions stand where the studies were powered.

**Progress.** The measured average differences. The established condition-specific superiorities. The quantified allegiance effects. The established advantage over no treatment.

**Own record.**

## 123 Dreaming

**Conditions prior.** The statement holds across readings. The terms have determinate reference. The accounts hold their truth value while tested. A procedure repeated returns the same outcome independently of what precedes it. Nothing prior enters.

**Statement.** Dreaming is universal and vivid and occurs nightly. What is it for?

**The field's account of its hardness.**

**Accounts.** It has a function: candidates include emotional processing, memory consolidation, and threat simulation, each with supporting evidence, and a phenomenon this conserved and this costly in brain activity is unlikely to be incidental.

It may be a by-product: dreaming may be an epiphenomenon of the physiological state that has the function, the evidence for content-specific benefit is weak, and damage abolishing dream report leaves sleep function largely intact in reported cases.

**Specifications.** Dreaming is separable from the sleep state carrying it — local. A function is identifiable by manipulation — local.

**Specifications withdrawn.**

**Common requirement.** That dreaming can be manipulated separately from sleep.

**Conserving relation.** The contribution of dreaming identified, the identification standing separately from the sleep state.

**Conserving reach.** All the sleep in which dreaming occurs.

**Reach.** The statement quantifies over the contribution of dreaming as distinct from sleep. Sleep stages and reports on waking are what is measured, with dreaming and its physiological substrate co-occurring. The two are not the same.

**Unreachability.** Selective deprivation is confounded by arousal and rebound, documented extensively, and memory reactivation establishes a sleep effect not linked to dream content. The identification is not reached over the sleep by stages and waking reports.

**Persistence.** A manipulation of dreaming separate from the sleep state, or a content-specific effect established.

**Addresses.** Keyed property by property.

*The report.*

**Approaches.** Selective deprivation — confounded by arousal and rebound, which the field has documented extensively. Lesion cases abolishing dream report — rare, the reported cases constraining function claims. Targeted memory reactivation in sleep — improves retention, establishing a sleep effect, the link to dream content unestablished. Dream content analysis — finds thematic regularities consistent with several accounts. Lucid dreaming with communication — two-way communication established in recent work, opening a route, early, and its reach for function questions not yet clear.

**Narrowing recorded.** Targeted memory reactivation narrows the reach: the effect stands established for sleep rather than for dreaming. Lucid dreaming narrows the reach: the route stands open for the states in which communication is established.

**Progress.** The documented confounding of selective deprivation. The reported lesion cases and their constraint on function claims. The measured retention gains from reactivation. The established two-way communication in lucid states.

**Own record.**

## 124 The Cambrian explosion

**Conditions prior.** The statement holds across readings. The terms have determinate reference. The accounts hold their truth value while tested. A procedure repeated returns the same outcome independently of what precedes it. Nothing prior enters.

**Statement.** Most animal body plans appear in the fossil record over a geologically short interval. What produced the concentration?

**The field's account of its hardness.**

**Accounts.** A real evolutionary event: ecological triggers — the rise of predation, oxygen thresholds, the evolution of eyes — produced rapid diversification, and the fossil concentration reflects a genuine burst.

The concentration is partly artefact: molecular clocks place divergences substantially earlier, the appearance of hard parts changes preservation probability sharply at that boundary, and taxonomic practice assigns higher ranks to early forms, all of which concentrate apparent origination.

**Specifications.** First appearance in the record approximates origination — local. Body plans are comparable units — local.

**Specifications withdrawn.**

**Common requirement.** That the record bears on the timing of divergence.

**Conserving relation.** Origination times determined, the determination standing across the two records.

**Conserving reach.** The interval the concentration is measured over.

**Reach.** The statement quantifies over origination times, approximated by first appearance. First appearances, conditioned on preservation, and molecular clocks conditioned on calibration, are what is measured. The two are not the same.

**Unreachability.** The two records returned different timings by hundreds of millions of years; under the recalibrated analyses the divergence has narrowed to the Ediacaran and the records stand in much closer accord, with a literal reading of the fossil record still rejected and preservation modelling quantifying part of the remaining difference. The determination is not reached over the interval by first appearances and clocks alone.

**Persistence.** Agreement between the two records on origination times, or a preservation model accounting for the remaining difference.

**Addresses.** Keyed property by property.

*The report.*

**Approaches.** Molecular clocks — place divergences earlier, estimates depending on calibration and rate models and having varied widely between studies. Preservation modelling — quantifies the hard-part bias substantially, establishing part of the artefact. Ediacaran and trace fossil work — extends the record of animal activity earlier, narrowing the gap in one direction. Oxygen proxies — improved records showing a complex rather than a single threshold, weakening simple trigger accounts. Developmental genetics — establishes the regulatory toolkit predates the concentration, supporting the earlier-divergence account and not dating the morphological events.

**Narrowing recorded.** Preservation modelling narrows the reach: part of the concentration stands accounted for as bias. Trace fossil work narrows the reach: the record of activity stands extended earlier for the traces recovered.

**Progress.** The recalibrated clock estimates and their narrowed divergence from the fossil record. The quantified hard-part bias. The extended trace record. The regulatory toolkit predating the concentration.

**Own record.**

## 125 The Sapient paradox

**Conditions prior.** The statement holds across readings. The terms have determinate reference. The accounts hold their truth value while tested. A procedure repeated returns the same outcome independently of what precedes it. Nothing prior enters.

**Statement.** Anatomically modern humans appear long before the changes in behaviour and settlement usually taken as their signature. Why the gap?

**The field's account of its hardness.**

**Accounts.** A cognitive change occurred later: a neural or genetic change enabled symbolic behaviour tens of thousands of years after anatomy stabilised, and the archaeological transition marks it.

The gap is in the record and in demography: African finds push symbolic behaviour progressively earlier, population density thresholds affect cultural accumulation and loss independently of cognition, and the apparent transition is a sampling and demography effect.

**Specifications.** Behavioural modernity is a definable package — local. Its archaeological signature indexes cognition — local.

**Specifications withdrawn.**

**Common requirement.** That the archaeological record bears on cognitive capacity.

**Conserving relation.** Cognitive capacity indexed by material culture, the indexing standing across the sampled record.

**Conserving reach.** The interval between anatomical and behavioural appearance.

**Reach.** The statement quantifies over cognitive capacity, indexed by preserved material culture. Preserved material culture, unevenly sampled by region and period, is what is measured. The two are not the same.

**Unreachability.** Demographic modelling reproduces the pattern without cognitive change, candidate genetic changes are identified and none tied to behaviour, and the record has been extended repeatedly by new finds. The indexing is not reached over the interval by unevenly sampled material culture.

**Persistence.** A genetic or neural change tied to behaviour, or a record dense enough that further finds do not move the earliest evidence.

**Addresses.** Keyed property by property.

*The report.*

**Approaches.** Pushing the record earlier — African pigment, bead, and engraving finds repeatedly moving the earliest evidence back, a productive route that continues. Demographic modelling — accumulation and loss depending on population size and connectedness, reproducing the pattern without cognitive change; a published dispute stands against the models' assumptions and archaeological support, with robustness defended in reply, the standing contested rather than settled. Ancient genomics — identifies candidate changes, none tied to behaviour. Redefining modernity as a mosaic — now widely adopted, weakening the original framing. Comparison with other hominins — symbolic behaviour attributed in several finds, contested in dating and attribution.

**Narrowing recorded.** The mosaic reframing weakens the relation: modernity stands as a set of separately appearing traits rather than a package indexing capacity. Demographic modelling narrows the reach: the pattern stands accounted for by population size and connectedness where those are estimated.

**Progress.** The repeatedly extended record of symbolic behaviour. The demographic models reproducing the pattern. The identified candidate genetic changes.

**Own record.**

## 126 The Flynn effect and its reversal

**Conditions prior.** The statement holds across readings. The terms have determinate reference. The accounts hold their truth value while tested. A procedure repeated returns the same outcome independently of what precedes it. Nothing prior enters.

**Statement.** Test scores rose substantially for decades across countries, then flattened or fell in several. What was rising, and what changed?

**The field's account of its hardness.**

**Accounts.** Environmental improvement, then its exhaustion: nutrition, schooling, family size, and test familiarity raised measured performance, and gains flattened as those improved conditions saturated in wealthy countries.

What was measured may not be what the tests are taken to measure: gains are concentrated in subtests least loaded on the general factor, which is a measured pattern and the opposite of what a rise in general ability predicts, and measurement invariance across cohorts has been tested and often fails.

**Specifications.** The tests measure the same construct across cohorts — local. Score change indexes construct change — local.

**Specifications withdrawn.**

**Common requirement.** That cross-cohort comparison of scores is meaningful.

**Conserving relation.** One construct measured alike across cohorts, the sameness standing under invariance testing.

**Conserving reach.** All cohorts compared.

**Reach.** The statement quantifies over a construct compared across cohorts. Test scores, whose invariance across cohorts is itself in question, are what is measured. The two are not the same.

**Unreachability.** Measurement invariance across cohorts frequently fails, which is established and is a limitation on the comparison the statement requires; and gains are concentrated on the least g-loaded subtests, measured consistently. The sameness is not preserved across the cohorts by test scores.

**Persistence.** A measure demonstrated invariant across the cohorts compared.

**Addresses.** Keyed property by property.

*The report.*

**Approaches.** Measurement invariance testing — frequently fails across cohorts, established, the strongest constraint on the interpretation. Subtest decomposition — gains concentrated on the least g-loaded subtests, measured consistently. Within-family analyses — conscript data showing the reversal within families, excluding compositional explanations such as migration for that population. Environmental proxies — nutrition and schooling correlating with gains historically, saturation inferred rather than measured directly. Test-content analyses — changes in what items require, consistent with familiarity accounts.

**Narrowing recorded.** Within-family analyses narrow the reach: the reversal stands established within families for the population analysed. Subtest decomposition narrows the reach: the gains stand located by subtest rather than in the construct.

**Progress.** The established failures of measurement invariance. The measured subtest concentration of gains. The within-family reversal. The historical correlations with nutrition and schooling.

**Own record.**

## 127 Eukaryogenesis

**Conditions prior.** The statement holds across readings. The terms have determinate reference. The accounts hold their truth value while tested. A procedure repeated returns the same outcome independently of what precedes it. Nothing prior enters.

**Statement.** Complex cells arose once, from an association of simpler cells. What was the sequence?

**The field's account of its hardness.**

**Accounts.** Mitochondria first: the host was an archaeon, and acquisition of the bacterial endosymbiont preceded and enabled the rest of eukaryotic complexity, the energy supply making a large genome affordable.

Host complexity first: Asgard archaea carry many eukaryotic signature proteins and cytoskeletal machinery, and cultured representatives form protrusions consistent with engulfment, indicating substantial complexity before the symbiosis.

**Specifications.** There was a sequence of events — local. Present organisms and phylogenies constrain it — local.

**Specifications withdrawn.**

**Common requirement.** That the order of acquisition is recoverable.

**Conserving relation.** The order of acquisition recovered, the recovery standing on extant material.

**Conserving reach.** The sequence of events, which occurred once.

**Reach.** The statement quantifies over the order of acquisition, recovered from extant genomes. Extant genomes, phylogenetic reconstruction, and a small number of cultured relatives are what is measured. The two are not the same.

**Unreachability.** Phylogenomics establishes the host lineage robustly and not the order of events; ancestral genome reconstruction depends on model and taxon sampling with estimates differing; and the cell biology of intermediate forms is now observable while the event sequence remains uninferred from it. The recovery is not reached over the sequence by extant material.

**Persistence.** An order of acquisition inferable from extant material, or a fossil assignment at the relevant age.

**Addresses.** Keyed property by property.

*The report.*

**Approaches.** Phylogenomics — places eukaryotes within Asgard archaea, robustly, establishing the host lineage and not the order of events. Asgard culturing — from one organism cultured after long effort to a small set of cultured and highly enriched representatives across branches, with slow growth, protrusion morphology, and an actin-based cytoskeleton observed directly, a major and widening advance, the event sequence remaining uninferred from it. Energetics arguments — energy-per-gene arguments favouring the endosymbiont-first order, contested on assumptions about scaling. Ancestral genome reconstruction — infers gene content of the last common ancestor, depending on model and taxon sampling, estimates differing. Fossil record — sparse and difficult to assign at the relevant age.

**Narrowing recorded.** Phylogenomics narrows the reach: the host lineage stands established without the order. Culturing narrows the reach: the cell biology stands observable for the representatives cultured.

**Progress.** The placement of eukaryotes within Asgard archaea. The cultured and enriched representatives with directly observed cytoskeleton and morphology. The energetics calculations with their stated assumptions.

**Own record.**

## 128 Homeostasis and allostasis

**Conditions prior.** The statement holds across readings. The terms have determinate reference. The accounts hold their truth value while tested. A procedure repeated returns the same outcome independently of what precedes it. Nothing prior enters.

**Statement.** Physiological variables are held within ranges. Is regulation toward a setpoint, or anticipatory adjustment of the target?

**The field's account of its hardness.**

**Accounts.** Setpoint regulation: negative feedback around a reference is well characterised for temperature, osmolality, and glucose, with identified sensors, comparators, and effectors.

Anticipatory regulation: targets shift in advance of demand, driven by prediction, measured variables show sustained shifts with no error signal, and the setpoint account handles these only by positing a moving setpoint.

**Specifications.** Regulation is toward a value — local. The value is fixed or predicted — local.

**Specifications withdrawn.**

**Common requirement.** That there is a regulated variable with a target.

**Conserving relation.** A determination of whether the target is fixed or set in advance, standing on measured trajectories.

**Conserving reach.** All regulated variables the question is asked of.

**Reach.** The statement quantifies over whether the target is fixed or set in advance. Measured variables and their trajectories, compatible with both descriptions, are what is measured. The two are not the same.

**Unreachability.** Both descriptions are representable and model comparison on the same data does not separate them reliably; and the moving-setpoint model absorbs anticipation at a cost to testability its critics state. The determination is not reached over the variables by measured trajectories.

**Persistence.** A trajectory the two descriptions predict differently, or agreement on what a moving setpoint concedes.

**Addresses.** Keyed property by property.

*The report.*

**Approaches.** Identifying comparator circuits — found for temperature and osmolality, establishing setpoint machinery in those systems. Anticipatory response measurement — documented before meals, exercise, and stress, robustly, establishing anticipation. Moving setpoint models — accommodate anticipation within the feedback framework, their critics holding this makes the framework unfalsifiable, which is stated in the literature. Control-theoretic modelling — both descriptions representable, model comparison on the same data not separating them reliably. Weight defence studies — vigorous defence of body weight, claimed by both accounts.

**Narrowing recorded.** Comparator identification narrows the reach: setpoint machinery stands established for temperature and osmolality. Moving setpoint models weaken the relation: the target stands variable within the feedback framework.

**Progress.** The identified sensors, comparators, and effectors in specific systems. The documented anticipatory responses. The measured defence of body weight.

**Own record.**

## 129 Antibiotic resistance

**Conditions prior.** The statement holds across readings. The terms have determinate reference. The accounts hold their truth value while tested. A procedure repeated returns the same outcome independently of what precedes it. Nothing prior enters.

**Statement.** Every antibiotic class has been followed by resistance. Is this avoidable by better use, or intrinsic to the approach?

**The field's account of its hardness.**

**Accounts.** Manageable by stewardship and pipeline: resistance rates respond to prescribing practice, cycling and combination strategies slow it, and new classes and mechanisms continue to be found.

Intrinsic: resistance genes predate clinical use, are widespread in environmental reservoirs, and are mobile between organisms, so any sustained selection pressure selects, and stewardship changes the rate rather than the outcome.

**Specifications.** Resistance is a response to use — local. The response is modifiable by managing use — local.

**Specifications withdrawn.**

**Common requirement.** That the trajectory is determined by selection pressure that management can alter.

**Conserving relation.** The outcome under sustained selection pressure determined, the determination standing for regimes not yet run.

**Conserving reach.** All regimes, those not yet run included.

**Reach.** The statement quantifies over the outcome under regimes not yet run, with selection pressure sustained. Resistance rates under regimes that have been tried, over years to decades, are what is measured. The two are not the same.

**Unreachability.** Stewardship reduces rates measurably and the effects reverse when relaxed; resistance has followed each new class within years of deployment, without exception so far; and the resistome surveys establish the reservoir predates use. The determination is not reached over the untried regimes by rates under regimes tried.

**Persistence.** A regime under which sustained pressure does not select, or agreement that the outcome is fixed and management sets the rate.

**Addresses.** Keyed property by property.

*The report.*

**Approaches.** Stewardship programmes — reduce resistance rates measurably in specific settings, effects real and partial and reversing when relaxed. Cycling and mixing strategies — modelling and trials giving mixed results, mixing outperforming cycling in several models. New classes — discovered at a slow rate, resistance following each within years of deployment, without exception so far. Resistome surveys — resistance genes found in ancient and pristine environments, establishing the reservoir predates use. Alternative modalities — phage, antivirulence, and microbiome approaches, early, resistance to phage arising readily, which is documented.

**Narrowing recorded.** Stewardship narrows the reach: the reduction stands in the settings where the programme runs, and reverses when relaxed. Mixing strategies narrow the reach: the advantage stands in the models where it is measured.

**Progress.** The measured stewardship reductions and their reversal on relaxation. The record of resistance following each class. The ancient and pristine resistome. The documented phage resistance.

**Own record.**

## 130 Alignment of built systems

**Conditions prior.** The statement holds across readings. The terms have determinate reference. The accounts hold their truth value while tested. A procedure repeated returns the same outcome independently of what precedes it. Nothing prior enters.

**Statement.** A system is trained to optimise a specified objective. How is it made to do what was intended rather than what was specified?

**The field's account of its hardness.**

**Accounts.** By improving specification and oversight: objectives learned from human feedback capture what a specification could not state, tasks are decomposed so that evaluation is easier than performance, systems assist in evaluating systems, and interpretability supplies a check independent of behaviour. Each component has measurable effect, and the combination has produced large and documented improvements in the behaviour of deployed systems.

The gap may not close by these means: feedback trains on evaluations, and where a system's competence exceeds an evaluator's, the training signal measures what evaluators approve rather than what is intended; specification gaming is documented in a large catalogue of cases, and the failure recurs at each level of the oversight scheme.

**Specifications.** The intended behaviour is specifiable, or a specification error is detectable by evaluation — local. Evaluation is available at the level of the behaviour — local.

**Specifications withdrawn.**

**Common requirement.** That there is an intended behaviour distinguishable from the specified one.

**Conserving relation.** Behaviour matching what was intended, the match standing in situations not evaluated.

**Conserving reach.** All situations, those not evaluated included.

**Reach.** The statement quantifies over behaviour in situations not evaluated. Behaviour on evaluated distributions, and on tests constructed in advance, are what is measured. The two are not the same.

**Unreachability.** Reward models are documented to be gameable and to be optimised against, and scalable oversight is untested where the gap is largest, by construction. The match is not reached over unevaluated situations by behaviour on evaluated ones.

**Persistence.** An oversight scheme tested where a system's competence exceeds the evaluator's, or a check on intent independent of behaviour.

**Addresses.** Keyed property by property.

*The report.*

**Approaches.** Reinforcement learning from human feedback — produces large behavioural improvements, reward models documented to be gameable and to be optimised against. Scalable oversight — debate, recursive decomposition, and assisted evaluation, results promising in constructed settings, whether they hold where the gap is largest untested by construction. Interpretability — aims to read intent from internals, its own coverage partial. Red-teaming and evaluations — find failures reliably, establishing presence rather than absence. Specification gaming catalogues — document the phenomenon across many systems, a record rather than a remedy.

**Narrowing recorded.** Scalable oversight narrows the reach: the results stand in constructed settings where evaluation remains available. Red-teaming narrows the reach: presence of failure stands established for the cases constructed.

**Progress.** The measured behavioural improvements from feedback training. The documented gameability of reward models. The results of oversight schemes in constructed settings, extended at the instrument itself: reasoning-trace monitoring detects gaming more effectively than action-only monitoring, weaker models sometimes monitoring stronger ones, and where optimisation incorporates the monitor, obfuscated gaming follows. The specification gaming catalogue.

**Own record.**

## 131 Benchmarks

**Conditions prior.** The statement holds across readings. The terms have determinate reference. The accounts hold their truth value while tested. A procedure repeated returns the same outcome independently of what precedes it. Nothing prior enters.

**Statement.** Systems are compared on benchmark scores. Do scores measure the capability the benchmark names?

**The field's account of its hardness.**

**Accounts.** Approximately, and usefully: benchmark scores correlate with downstream task performance where both are measured, benchmark competition has driven progress that transferred to applications, and suites are retired and replaced when they saturate. Every science measures with imperfect instruments and improves them.

Scores measure benchmark performance: contamination of training data by test items is documented and hard to exclude at scale, benchmarks are optimised against once published, and score gains have repeatedly failed to transfer to tasks the benchmark was taken to represent.

**Specifications.** The benchmark samples a capability — local. The sample is independent of what is trained on — local.

**Specifications withdrawn.**

**Common requirement.** That there is a capability the benchmark samples.

**Conserving relation.** A score measuring capability rather than benchmark performance, the measurement standing after the benchmark is published.

**Conserving reach.** Capability on tasks not in the benchmark.

**Reach.** The statement quantifies over capability on tasks not in the benchmark. Benchmark items, and constructed held-out sets that become public in turn, are what is measured. The two are not the same.

**Unreachability.** Each held-out set is optimised against in turn, and contamination detection gives partial coverage, which the field states. The measurement is not reached over untested tasks by benchmark items and held-out sets.

**Persistence.** A measure not optimised against after publication, or downstream measurement at a scale that settles transfer.

**Addresses.** Keyed property by property.

*The report.*

**Approaches.** Held-out and private test sets — effective until released or inferred, the ones kept private not independently verifiable, which is the trade. Contamination detection — methods existing and giving partial coverage, which the field states. Adversarial and dynamic benchmarks — constructed against current systems, saturating as systems adapt. Task diversity and aggregate suites — reduce single-benchmark artefacts, the aggregate optimised against in turn. Downstream deployment measurement — the strongest route, expensive, slow, and confounded by deployment context.

**Narrowing recorded.** Private test sets narrow the reach: the measurement stands where the set is unreleased, and is not independently verifiable there. Aggregate suites narrow the reach: single-benchmark artefacts stand reduced across the suite.

**Progress.** The measured correlations with downstream performance where both are available. The documented contamination and its partial detection. The saturation record of successive suites.

**Own record.**

## 132 Standards and interoperability

**Conditions prior.** The statement holds across readings. The terms have determinate reference. The accounts hold their truth value while tested. A procedure repeated returns the same outcome independently of what precedes it. Nothing prior enters.

**Statement.** Common standards let systems work together. Do they raise or lower the total value produced?

**The field's account of its hardness.**

**Accounts.** They raise it: network effects mean shared standards produce compatibility gains exceeding the constraint costs, demonstrated across shipping containers, telecommunications, and internet protocols.

It depends on timing and on what is frozen: standards set early lock in inferior designs and foreclose alternatives, and the cases where a standard blocked improvement are documented alongside the successes, so there is no general answer, only cases.

**Specifications.** The counterfactual — the value under an unadopted alternative or no standard — is estimable — local.

**Specifications withdrawn.**

**Common requirement.** That the value under the unadopted arrangement is comparable.

**Conserving relation.** A comparison of adopted and unadopted arrangements, standing at the scale the adopted one reached.

**Conserving reach.** All cases the comparison is asked of.

**Reach.** The statement quantifies over a counterfactual arrangement at scale. The adopted arrangement, and pre-standard periods that differ in many other respects, are what is measured. The two are not the same.

**Unreachability.** Case studies on both sides are contested, formal models produce both outcomes under stated conditions with the conditions being the empirical question, and competing-standard episodes are confounded by firm strategy and timing. The comparison is not reached over the cases by the adopted arrangement and pre-standard periods.

**Persistence.** A comparison at scale between adopted and unadopted arrangements, or agreement on the conditions under which each outcome follows.

**Addresses.** Keyed property by property.

*The report.*

**Approaches.** Case studies of successful standards — establish large gains where adopted, selection on adoption being the objection. Case studies of lock-in — establish costs in specific cases, contested in the same way. Competing-standard episodes — comparison available for a period where two standards competed, outcomes confounded by firm strategy and timing. Formal modelling — produces both outcomes under stated conditions, the conditions being the empirical question. Standards body process studies — document how choices are made, accounting for adoption rather than value.

**Narrowing recorded.** Competing-standard episodes narrow the reach: the comparison stands for the period and cases in which two standards ran together.

**Progress.** The measured gains where standards were adopted. The documented lock-in costs in specific cases. The formal conditions under which each outcome follows.

**Own record.**

## 133 Brooks's law

**Conditions prior.** The statement holds across readings. The terms have determinate reference. The accounts hold their truth value while tested. A procedure repeated returns the same outcome independently of what precedes it. Nothing prior enters.

**Statement.** Adding people to a late software project makes it later. Is this general?

**The field's account of its hardness.**

**Accounts.** Yes, by a countable mechanism: communication paths grow with the square of team size while labour grows linearly, and ramp-up consumes existing members' time, so the arithmetic holds wherever the work is not partitionable.

It is conditional and often violated: modular architecture, asynchronous coordination, and mature onboarding reduce the coupling, large distributed projects add contributors continuously without the predicted collapse, and the empirical record does not show a general law.

**Specifications.** Coordination cost scales with the number of pairs — local. The work's partitionability is fixed — local.

**Specifications withdrawn.**

**Common requirement.** That coordination cost and output are measurable against team size.

**Conserving relation.** The effect of adding people isolated, the isolation standing with all else held equal.

**Conserving reach.** All projects the effect is asked of.

**Reach.** The statement quantifies over the effect of adding people, holding all else equal. Projects where adding people co-occurs with schedule pressure, scope change, and architecture choices are what is measured. The two are not the same.

**Unreachability.** Case histories select on troubled projects, estimation models incorporate diseconomies as parameters fitted to the same data, and controlled experiments run at scales far below the cases in question. The isolation is not reached over the projects by observations where the additions co-occur with other changes.

**Persistence.** A measurement of the effect with architecture and scope held fixed, at the scale of the cases in question.

**Addresses.** Keyed property by property.

*The report.*

**Approaches.** Case histories — support the law in the original setting, selection on troubled projects being the standing objection. Open-source contributor studies — find sublinear but positive returns to contributors in modular projects, establishing the conditionality. Software estimation models — incorporate diseconomies of scale as fitted parameters, fitting, the parameters estimated from the same data. Controlled experiments — small-team laboratory studies finding coordination overhead, at scales far below the cases in question. Architecture studies — modularity reducing coupling measurably, establishing the mechanism's dependence on structure.

**Narrowing recorded.** Open-source studies narrow the reach: positive returns stand in modular projects. Controlled experiments narrow the reach: the overhead stands measured at small-team scale.

**Progress.** The measured returns to contributors in modular projects. The measured coordination overhead at small scale. The measured effect of modularity on coupling.

**Own record.**

## 134 Risk compensation

**Conditions prior.** The statement holds across readings. The terms have determinate reference. The accounts hold their truth value while tested. A procedure repeated returns the same outcome independently of what precedes it. Nothing prior enters.

**Statement.** Safety measures reduce the harm per event. Do they reduce total harm, or do people take more risk?

**The field's account of its hardness.**

**Accounts.** Total harm falls: seat belt, helmet, and airbag introductions are followed by large measured reductions in death and serious injury, established across many jurisdictions with staggered adoption and confirmed in occupant-level data where exposure is controlled, and compensation, where measured, offsets a fraction of the mechanical gain and never the whole of it.

Compensation is real and sometimes complete: behavioural adaptation is documented — following distances, overtaking margins, cycling behaviour around helmeted riders — and in some settings the aggregate effect on total harm is null or adverse, particularly where risk is transferred to others.

**Specifications.** A safety measure's effect decomposes into a mechanical effect and a behavioural response — local. Both are estimable — local.

**Specifications withdrawn.**

**Common requirement.** That the counterfactual harm without the measure is estimable.

**Conserving relation.** Total harm compared against the counterfactual regime, the comparison standing with concurrent changes accounted for.

**Conserving reach.** All settings the measure is adopted in.

**Reach.** The statement quantifies over total harm under a counterfactual regime. Outcomes after adoption, with concurrent changes in traffic, vehicles, medicine, and enforcement, are what is measured. The two are not the same.

**Unreachability.** Before-after series are confounded by concurrent trends, which the literature addresses with varying success, and risk transfer to other users is measured in some settings and concealed by aggregate series. The comparison is not reached over the settings by outcomes after adoption.

**Persistence.** A comparison against the counterfactual regime with concurrent changes accounted for, or measured magnitudes for the mechanical and behavioural components across settings.

**Addresses.** Keyed property by property.

*The report.*

**Approaches.** Before-after mortality series — show large reductions, confounded by concurrent trends, which the literature addresses with varying success. Behavioural measurement — documents adaptation directly in specific behaviours, establishing the mechanism, magnitude relative to the mechanical effect varying by study. Natural experiments from staggered adoption — the strongest route, results supporting net reductions for major measures with partial offsets. Risk transfer studies — find effects on other road users in some settings, which the aggregate series can conceal. Full-compensation theory — predicts complete offset, its critics holding the mortality series refute it, its defenders disputing the counterfactual.

**Narrowing recorded.** Staggered-adoption designs narrow the reach: the net reduction stands for the jurisdictions and measures analysed. Risk transfer studies narrow the reach: the transfer stands measured in the settings studied.

**Progress.** The measured reductions in death and serious injury. The documented behavioural adaptation. The staggered-adoption results with partial offsets. The measured risk transfer in specific settings.

**Own record.**

## 135 Technical debt

**Conditions prior.** The statement holds across readings. The terms have determinate reference. The accounts hold their truth value while tested. A procedure repeated returns the same outcome independently of what precedes it. Nothing prior enters.

**Statement.** Choices that speed delivery now raise the cost of later change. Is the trade-off measurable?

**The field's account of its hardness.**

**Accounts.** Yes, and increasingly: code quality metrics correlate with defect rates and change cost, static analysis identifies specific classes, and firms that measure it report actionable results.

The measurements are of proxies: metrics correlate with each other more strongly than with change cost, the counterfactual code that was not written is unavailable, and what is called debt is often a design decision whose cost depends on changes not yet requested.

**Specifications.** There is a present cost incurred by a past choice — local. The cost is attributable to identifiable code properties — local.

**Specifications withdrawn.**

**Common requirement.** That the later cost is attributable to the earlier choice.

**Conserving relation.** The later cost attributed to the earlier choice, the attribution standing for changes not yet requested.

**Conserving reach.** All changes, those not yet requested included.

**Reach.** The statement quantifies over cost of changes not yet requested. Code properties now, and change history so far, are what is measured. The two are not the same.

**Unreachability.** The counterfactual code carrying the disputed cost is unavailable, and change-cost measurement covers changes made while the unmade changes carry the disputed cost. The attribution is not reached over unrequested changes by code properties and change history.

**Persistence.** A measurement of the cost of changes not yet requested, or agreement on a proxy that tracks it.

**Addresses.** Keyed property by property.

*The report.*

**Approaches.** Metric-defect correlation — established for several metrics, attribution to the earlier decision being the gap. Change-cost measurement — measures cost of changes made, the unmade changes carrying the disputed cost. Refactoring experiments — show improvements in specific measures, cost-benefit over the project life unmeasured. Developer survey and interview studies — establish that practitioners identify debt consistently, establishing a shared concept rather than a quantity. Architecture erosion studies — track drift from intended structure, establishing drift, consequences depending on future requirements.

**Narrowing recorded.** Refactoring experiments narrow the reach: the improvement stands in the measures taken. Change-cost measurement narrows the reach: the cost stands measured for changes made.

**Progress.** The established metric-defect correlations. The measured cost of changes made. The measured improvements from refactoring. The consistency of practitioner identification.

**Own record.**

## 136 Signal and noise

**Conditions prior.** The statement holds across readings. The terms have determinate reference. The accounts hold their truth value while tested. A procedure repeated returns the same outcome independently of what precedes it. Nothing prior enters.

**Statement.** A channel carries a signal with noise. Where does one end and the other begin?

**The field's account of its hardness.**

**Accounts.** The distinction is definable relative to a model of the source: given the source statistics the decomposition is exact, and information theory makes the whole treatment quantitative.

The decomposition is relative to what is wanted: nothing in the channel marks which component is signal, the same physical variation is signal for one purpose and noise for another, and added noise improving detection is treated as anomalous by the framing.

**Specifications.** The channel's content decomposes into wanted and unwanted components — local. The source model is available — local.

**Specifications withdrawn.**

**Common requirement.** That the decomposition is well defined.

**Conserving relation.** A decomposition into signal and noise, standing independently of purpose.

**Conserving reach.** All channels the decomposition is asked of.

**Reach.** The statement quantifies over which component is which, independent of purpose. The total variation, and a model supplied by the analyst, are what is measured. The two are not the same.

**Unreachability.** Every decomposition is exact given its model, and the model is supplied; and added noise improving detection in nonlinear systems, measured across physical and biological settings, establishes the labels are purpose-relative. The decomposition is not reached over the channels independently of a supplied model.

**Persistence.** A decomposition not resting on a supplied model or purpose, or agreement that the labels are purpose-relative.

**Addresses.** Keyed property by property.

*The report.*

**Approaches.** Modelling the source — makes the decomposition exact given the model, the model being supplied. Blind source separation — recovers components from mixtures under stated assumptions, independence and sparsity, which are supplied. Stochastic resonance — demonstrates noise improving detection in nonlinear systems, measured across physical and biological settings, establishing the labels are purpose-relative. Matched filtering — optimal given the expected signal shape, which is supplied. Information-theoretic bounds — exact given the channel and source distributions.

**Narrowing recorded.** Blind source separation narrows the reach: the recovery stands under stated independence or sparsity assumptions. Matched filtering narrows the reach: optimality stands for a supplied signal shape.

**Progress.** The exact decompositions under stated models. The measured stochastic resonance across settings. The information-theoretic bounds under given distributions.

**Own record.**

## 137 Work and waste heat

**Conditions prior.** The statement holds across readings. The terms have determinate reference. The accounts hold their truth value while tested. A procedure repeated returns the same outcome independently of what precedes it. Nothing prior enters.

**Statement.** An engine produces work and rejects heat. Is the split a property of the engine?

**The field's account of its hardness.**

**Accounts.** The split is exact and physical: the first and second laws determine the maximum work extractable, efficiency is measured to high precision, and nothing about the accounting is conventional.

Which output counts as work is set by what is wanted from the device: in a heat pump the rejected heat is the product, in cogeneration both are, the thermodynamics is invariant and the labels are not, and nothing in the engine marks the split.

**Specifications.** A device has an intended output — local. Efficiency is output over input — universal under thermodynamics.

**Specifications withdrawn.**

**Common requirement.** That efficiency is well defined for the device.

**Conserving relation.** A determination of which output is the product, standing on the energy flows.

**Conserving reach.** All devices the question is asked of.

**Reach.** The statement quantifies over which output is the product. Energy flows, all measurable, none labelled, are what is measured. The two are not the same.

**Unreachability.** Thermodynamic accounting is exact and invariant under relabelling, which both accounts accept entirely; exergy requires a specified reference environment; and cogeneration standards allocate between outputs by conventions that differ between jurisdictions, which is documented. The determination is not reached over the devices by energy flows.

**Persistence.** A measure of the product not resting on a specified reference or convention, or agreement that the labels are set by purpose.

**Addresses.** Keyed property by property.

*The report.*

**Approaches.** Thermodynamic accounting — exact, and invariant under relabelling, accepted entirely by both accounts. Exergy analysis — measures available work regardless of purpose, coming closest to a purpose-free measure and still requiring a reference environment, which is specified. Second-law efficiency — ratio to the reversible ideal, requiring specification of which output the ideal produces. Cogeneration accounting — standards allocating between outputs by convention, the conventions differing between jurisdictions, which is documented. Life-cycle accounting — extends the boundary, the boundary being drawn.

**Narrowing recorded.** Exergy analysis narrows the reach: the measure stands relative to a specified reference environment. Cogeneration standards narrow the reach: the allocation stands by convention within a jurisdiction.

**Progress.** The exact thermodynamic accounting and its invariance under relabelling. The exergy measure with its stated reference. The documented divergence of allocation conventions.

**Own record.**

## 138 Mutation, fidelity and variation

**Conditions prior.** The statement holds across readings. The terms have determinate reference. The accounts hold their truth value while tested. A procedure repeated returns the same outcome independently of what precedes it. Nothing prior enters.

**Statement.** Replication makes errors. Are errors a failure of fidelity or the source of variation?

**The field's account of its hardness.**

**Accounts.** Both descriptions apply and the biology is settled: fidelity is under selection, mutation rates are themselves evolved, and rates sit near an optimum balancing the cost of errors against the cost of proofreading.

The optimum framing assumes selection acts on the rate as a trait, which requires linkage conditions that often fail: rates are set by the limits of selection efficacy rather than by an optimum, and rates track effective population size across taxa as that predicts.

**Specifications.** Mutation rate is a trait — local. Its value is accounted for either by optimisation or by the limits of selection — local.

**Specifications withdrawn.**

**Common requirement.** That the observed rate has an account in these terms.

**Conserving relation.** An account of what sets the rate, standing on parameters estimated independently of the rate.

**Conserving reach.** All taxa the rate is measured across.

**Reach.** The statement quantifies over what sets the rate. Measured rates across taxa, and population-genetic parameters estimated from the same genomes, are what is measured. The two are not the same.

**Unreachability.** Effective population size is estimated from the same genomes the rates are measured in, so the strongest correlation closes on itself; and rates vary along genomes with sequence context and repair activity, which complicates a single rate as the trait. The account is not reached over the taxa by rates and parameters estimated together.

**Persistence.** An estimate of effective population size independent of the genomes the rates are measured in.

**Addresses.** Keyed property by property.

*The report.*

**Approaches.** Mutation accumulation experiments — measure rates directly across many species, establishing the values. Drift-barrier tests — rates correlating inversely with effective population size across a wide range, as predicted, the strongest evidence, effective population size estimated with model assumptions. Mutator strain studies — elevated rates favoured under strong selection in the laboratory, establishing conditions where rate evolves adaptively. Proofreading cost measurement — costs small in most systems, weakening simple optimisation accounts. Local rate variation studies — rates varying along genomes with sequence context and repair activity, complicating a single rate as the trait.

**Narrowing recorded.** Mutator strain studies narrow the reach: adaptive rate evolution stands established under laboratory selection. Local rate variation narrows the reach: a rate stands defined per genomic context rather than per organism.

**Progress.** The directly measured rates across species. The measured inverse correlation with effective population size. The measured proofreading costs. The measured local rate variation.

**Own record.**

## 139 Intellectual property

**Conditions prior.** The statement holds across readings. The terms have determinate reference. The accounts hold their truth value while tested. A procedure repeated returns the same outcome independently of what precedes it. Nothing prior enters.

**Statement.** Ideas can be copied at no cost. Does granting exclusive rights over them increase or decrease what is created?

**The field's account of its hardness.**

**Accounts.** It increases it: without exclusivity, investment in costly creation is not recoverable, and the record in pharmaceuticals — where development costs are high and imitation cheap — supports protection producing innovation that would not otherwise occur.

The evidence does not support it generally: cross-country and historical studies of regime changes find little effect on innovation rates and some negative effects through blocking and litigation, sectors with weak protection show high innovation, and cumulative innovation is impeded where the inputs are owned.

**Specifications.** Creation responds to expected returns — local. Innovation is measurable and attributable to a regime — local.

**Specifications withdrawn.**

**Common requirement.** That the counterfactual innovation under a different regime is estimable.

**Conserving relation.** Creation compared against a different regime, the comparison standing at the scale the adopted regime reached.

**Conserving reach.** All sectors and regimes the question is asked of.

**Reach.** The statement quantifies over creation that would have occurred under a different regime. Patents filed, products launched, and innovation proxies under regimes that were adopted are what is measured. The two are not the same.

**Unreachability.** Cross-country comparison is confounded by development level and institutions, historical episodes return mixed results, and sector comparison is read by both accounts as supporting their own. The comparison is not reached over the sectors and regimes by proxies under regimes adopted.

**Persistence.** A comparison against an unadopted regime at scale, or agreement on the conditions under which each effect follows.

**Addresses.** Keyed property by property.

*The report.*

**Approaches.** Cross-country regime comparison — finds weak or no association with innovation rates, confounded by development level and institutions. Historical natural experiments — extension and abolition episodes giving mixed results, several finding no innovation effect.
