Sector comparison — pharmaceuticals differing from software and finance in imitation cost, accepted by both accounts and read as supporting each. Litigation and blocking studies — document costs of the regime, measured. Prize and open-source alternatives — demonstrate creation without exclusivity in specific domains, scope of transfer disputed.

**Narrowing recorded.** Alternatives narrow the reach: creation without exclusivity stands demonstrated in the domains where prizes and open collaboration operate. Sector comparison narrows the reach: the account stands per sector by imitation cost rather than generally.

**Progress.** The measured cross-country associations. The historical episode results. The measured litigation and blocking costs. The demonstrated creation under alternative arrangements.

**Own record.**

## 140 Money as a store of value

**Conditions prior.** The statement holds across readings. The terms have determinate reference. The accounts hold their truth value while tested. A procedure repeated returns the same outcome independently of what precedes it. Nothing prior enters.

**Statement.** Money holds value over time. What makes it hold?

**The field's account of its hardness.**

**Accounts.** Backing and scarcity: value derives from what stands behind the money — a commodity redeemable on demand, a state's obligation to accept it for taxes, or a credible limit on issuance — and the historical record is consistent, currencies losing their backing or whose issuance became unlimited losing value, and those retaining one or the other not.

Acceptance is self-referential: fiat money holds value because it is accepted, and it is accepted because it is expected to hold value; the equilibrium is multiple, and models admit both valued and worthless outcomes for the same fundamentals, which is a theorem rather than an anomaly.

**Specifications.** Value has a determinant — local. The determinant is either external backing or expectations — local.

**Specifications withdrawn.**

**Common requirement.** That there is a determinant of the value.

**Conserving relation.** A determinant of the value identified, the identification standing for regimes with the same fundamentals.

**Conserving reach.** All regimes the question is asked of, fiat regimes included.

**Reach.** The statement quantifies over why acceptance persists. Prices and holdings under regimes that persisted, and episodes where they did not, are what is measured. The two are not the same.

**Unreachability.** Search-theoretic models prove equilibrium multiplicity — valued and non-valued outcomes coexisting for the same fundamentals — which does not select; and hyperinflation onset timing is not predicted by fundamentals in the episodes studied. The identification is not reached over the regimes by prices and holdings.

**Persistence.** A selection among the multiple equilibria, or a determinant predicting onset in the episodes where acceptance failed.

**Addresses.** Keyed property by property.

*The report.*

**Approaches.** Backing theories — account for commodity and convertible regimes, fiat regimes persisting without convertibility, which is the case in question. Search-theoretic models — prove multiplicity, valued and non-valued equilibria coexisting for the same fundamentals, a proved result that does not select. Legal tender and tax-driven demand — accounts for acceptance by obligation, its critics noting money circulates beyond tax obligations and across borders. Hyperinflation episodes — establish that acceptance can fail, onset timing not predicted by fundamentals in the episodes studied. Cryptocurrency — demonstrates acceptance without backing or legal tender for a period, read as supporting expectations by one account and as unresolved by the other.

**Narrowing recorded.** Backing theories narrow the reach: the account stands for commodity and convertible regimes. Tax-driven demand narrows the reach: acceptance stands accounted for within the jurisdiction imposing the obligation.

**Progress.** The historical record of backing and issuance against loss of value. The proved equilibrium multiplicity. The measured hyperinflation episodes and their unpredicted onsets.

**Own record.**

## 141 Principal and agent

**Conditions prior.** The statement holds across readings. The terms have determinate reference. The accounts hold their truth value while tested. A procedure repeated returns the same outcome independently of what precedes it. Nothing prior enters.

**Statement.** One party acts for another whose interests differ and whose monitoring is imperfect. Can contracts align them?

**The field's account of its hardness.**

**Accounts.** Yes, approximately: optimal contracts under stated informational conditions are characterised, incentive pay responds to the theory's predictions, and the design problem is solved where the conditions hold.

The conditions rarely hold: with multiple tasks, incentives distort toward the measured one; with team production, individual contribution is unidentifiable; and empirical returns to incentive pay are mixed with documented adverse effects. Alignment is partial by construction.

**Specifications.** The agent's action affects an observable outcome — local. A contract on observables can approximate one on the action — local.

**Specifications withdrawn.**

**Common requirement.** That the divergence is representable as a contracting problem.

**Conserving relation.** A contract aligning the parties, the alignment standing on observables where the action is unobserved.

**Conserving reach.** All settings the contracting problem is posed in.

**Reach.** The statement quantifies over the agent's unobserved action. Outcomes and observables, from which action is inferred under assumptions, are what is measured. The two are not the same.

**Unreachability.** Incentives are proved to distort toward measured dimensions where tasks are multiple, and individual contribution is proved unidentifiable in team production without further structure. The alignment is not reached over the settings by contracts on observables.

**Persistence.** A contract aligning the parties where tasks are multiple or production is joint, or agreement that alignment is partial by construction.

**Addresses.** Keyed property by property.

*The report.*

**Approaches.** Optimal contracting theory — solves the stated problem, the conditions restrictive and stated. Multitask analysis — proves distortion toward measured dimensions, a proved limitation. Team production — individual contribution unidentifiable without further structure, proved. Empirical incentive-pay studies — mixed effects with documented adverse cases, establishing the phenomenon is not uniform. Relational contracts and reputation — achieve alignment where repeated interaction is available, the conditions being the empirical question.

**Narrowing recorded.** Optimal contracting narrows the reach: the solution stands under the stated informational conditions. Relational contracts narrow the reach: alignment stands where repeated interaction is available.

**Progress.** The characterised optimal contracts under stated conditions. The two proved limitations. The measured mixed returns to incentive pay.

**Own record.**

## 142 The paradox of thrift

**Conditions prior.** The statement holds across readings. The terms have determinate reference. The accounts hold their truth value while tested. A procedure repeated returns the same outcome independently of what precedes it. Nothing prior enters.

**Statement.** Saving benefits an individual. If everyone saves more, income falls and saving may not rise. Which holds?

**The field's account of its hardness.**

**Accounts.** The aggregate effect is real: in demand-constrained conditions, higher desired saving lowers output and can leave realised saving unchanged, the mechanism being standard and the policy implication drawn from it.

It holds only under conditions: with flexible prices, accommodating monetary policy, or an open economy, higher saving finances investment and raises capacity, and the result is a special case of a demand-constrained regime rather than a general one.

**Specifications.** Individual and aggregate relations can differ — universal under macroeconomics. The operative regime is identifiable — local.

**Specifications withdrawn.**

**Common requirement.** That the regime determining which relation holds is identifiable in advance.

**Conserving relation.** The operative regime identified in advance, the identification standing on aggregate series.

**Conserving reach.** All periods the question is asked of.

**Reach.** The statement quantifies over which regime obtains. Aggregate series in which regime and outcome are jointly determined are what is measured. The two are not the same.

**Unreachability.** Both results are derivable in their own models, which both accounts accept, and multiplier estimates vary widely by method and by regime, which the literature documents. The identification is not reached over the periods by series in which regime and outcome are jointly determined.

**Persistence.** A regime identified independently of the outcome, or agreement on an estimation method for the multiplier.

**Addresses.** Keyed property by property.

*The report.*

**Approaches.** Model comparison — both results derivable in their own models, accepted by both accounts. Zero lower bound episodes — providing conditions where the demand-constrained mechanism is most likely, empirical work supporting it there and contested on identification. Cross-country saving-growth correlations — positive on average, negative in some periods, both readings available. Fiscal multiplier estimation — central to the dispute, estimates varying widely by method and by regime, which the literature documents. Microfoundation — reproduces the result in models with nominal rigidity, which is the disputed assumption.

**Narrowing recorded.** Zero lower bound analysis narrows the reach: the mechanism stands supported in the episodes where the constraint binds.

**Progress.** The derivations in both model classes. The empirical work at the zero lower bound. The measured spread of multiplier estimates by method and regime.

**Own record.**

## 143 The evolution of cooperation

**Conditions prior.** The statement holds across readings. The terms have determinate reference. The accounts hold their truth value while tested. A procedure repeated returns the same outcome independently of what precedes it. Nothing prior enters.

**Statement.** Natural selection favours what raises an individual's reproduction. Cooperation is widespread. How?

**The field's account of its hardness.**

**Accounts.** By identified mechanisms: kin selection, direct and indirect reciprocity, and population structure each produce cooperation under stated conditions, and the threshold is quantified in terms measurable in the field. The mechanisms make differential predictions tested across taxa — relatedness in eusocial insects, reputation in humans, spatial structure in microbes — each confirmed in its own domain.

The accounting is disputed at its foundation: inclusive fitness and multilevel selection give the same predictions under general conditions, which is proved, so they are two accountings of one process rather than rival hypotheses, and the long dispute is about the accounting rather than the biology.

**Specifications.** Fitness effects are attributable to individuals — local. Cooperation is a departure requiring explanation — local.

**Specifications withdrawn.**

**Common requirement.** That the fitness accounting is well defined.

**Conserving relation.** One accounting identified as describing the process, the identification standing on reproductive outcomes.

**Conserving reach.** All cases the accounting is applied to.

**Reach.** The statement quantifies over which accounting describes the process. Reproductive outcomes, decomposable either way, are what is measured. The two are not the same.

**Unreachability.** The two accountings are proved to give the same predictions under general conditions, so no reproductive outcome separates them. The identification is not reached over the cases by outcomes decomposable either way.

**Persistence.** Conditions under which the two accountings predict differently, or agreement that the rivalry is between accountings rather than hypotheses.

**Addresses.** Keyed property by property.

*The report.*

**Approaches.** Kin selection tests — relatedness predicting cooperation across many taxa, robustly, establishing the pattern under either accounting. Experimental evolution — produces cooperation and cheating dynamics as predicted, under either accounting. Formal equivalence results — the two accountings agreeing under general conditions, the strongest item, dissolving the rivalry rather than settling it. Public goods experiments in humans — establish conditional cooperation and punishment, mechanism-level findings. The eusociality dispute — a large public disagreement with a rebuttal signed by many, the mathematical points not resolved by the exchange.

**Narrowing recorded.** Mechanism-level tests narrow the reach: each mechanism stands confirmed in its own domain rather than across the cases.

**Progress.** The measured relatedness-cooperation association across taxa. The quantified threshold. The formal equivalence result. The conditional cooperation and punishment findings.

**Own record.**

## 144 Transfer of learning

**Conditions prior.** The statement holds across readings. The terms have determinate reference. The accounts hold their truth value while tested. A procedure repeated returns the same outcome independently of what precedes it. Nothing prior enters.

**Statement.** Something learned in one setting is applied in another. When does it transfer?

**The field's account of its hardness.**

**Accounts.** When the settings share elements: near transfer is reliable, measured across a century of studies and predicted well by identical-elements accounts, instruction making shared structure explicit improves it, worked-example and contrasting-case methods produce measurable gains, and expertise transfers readily within a domain.

Far transfer is rarely demonstrated: cognitive training studies show gains on trained tasks and little on untrained ones, the record across brain training, chess, music, and general reasoning shows near transfer and repeated failure of far transfer, and positive results often fail replication.

**Specifications.** Transfer is a property of the relation between two tasks — local. Task similarity is measurable independently of transfer — local.

**Specifications withdrawn.**

**Common requirement.** That similarity is specifiable before the transfer is measured.

**Conserving relation.** Similarity specified before transfer is measured, the specification standing for tasks not yet chosen.

**Conserving reach.** All task pairs, those not yet specified included.

**Reach.** The statement quantifies over application to a task not yet specified. Pairs of tasks selected by the experimenter, whose similarity is judged, are what is measured. The two are not the same.

**Unreachability.** Similarity is characterised after the tasks are chosen, and the far-transfer null record is now the field's own summary while expertise is established as domain-bound. The specification is not reached over unchosen pairs by pairs the experimenter selected.

**Persistence.** A measure of task similarity specified before transfer is measured, or agreement that far transfer is not to be expected.

**Addresses.** Keyed property by property.

*The report.*

**Approaches.** Identical-elements analysis — predicts near transfer well, similarity characterised after the tasks are chosen. Cognitive training trials — large, controlled, and consistently null for far transfer, now the field's own summary position. Expertise studies — expert advantage domain-bound, established across domains. Analogical transfer studies — transfer with hints and little without, establishing the conditions and their fragility. Metacognitive instruction — modest general gains in some studies, effect sizes small and replication partial.

**Narrowing recorded.** Identical-elements analysis narrows the reach: prediction stands for near pairs sharing characterised elements. Analogical transfer narrows the reach: transfer stands where hints are supplied.

**Progress.** The century-long near-transfer record. The consistently null far-transfer trials. The established domain-boundedness of expertise. The measured effect of hints on analogical transfer.

**Own record.**

## 145 Bernstein's degrees of freedom

**Conditions prior.** The statement holds across readings. The terms have determinate reference. The accounts hold their truth value while tested. A procedure repeated returns the same outcome independently of what precedes it. Nothing prior enters.

**Statement.** A limb has many more degrees of freedom than a movement requires. How is one solution selected?

**The field's account of its hardness.**

**Accounts.** By optimisation: movements minimise a cost — jerk, torque change, endpoint variance — and optimal control models reproduce observed trajectories closely across tasks.

The cost is inferred from the movements it accounts for: different costs fit the same trajectories, synergies reduce dimensionality without an optimiser, and observed variability is structured rather than minimised, which the uncontrolled manifold analyses show directly.

**Specifications.** A unique solution is selected — local. Selection is by minimisation of a cost — local.

**Specifications withdrawn.**

**Common requirement.** That the selection has a determinant.

**Conserving relation.** A selection rule identified, the identification standing on a cost specified independently of the movements.

**Conserving reach.** All movements the rule is asked of.

**Reach.** The statement quantifies over the selection rule. Recorded kinematics and muscle activity, fit by several models, are what is measured. The two are not the same.

**Unreachability.** Multiple cost functions fit the same trajectories comparably, which is documented, and inverse optimal control has known identifiability limits. The identification is not reached over the movements by kinematics and muscle activity.

**Persistence.** A cost specified independently of the movements it accounts for, or a measurement distinguishing the candidate costs.

**Addresses.** Keyed property by property.

*The report.*

**Approaches.** Optimal control modelling — fits trajectories well, multiple cost functions fitting comparably, which is documented. Inverse optimal control — recovers a cost from behaviour, identifiability of the cost limited and known to be so. Muscle synergy analysis — finds low-dimensional structure, whether synergies are neural or an artefact of the decomposition disputed, the dispute technical and live. Uncontrolled manifold analysis — variability structured to preserve task-relevant variables, establishing structure without an optimiser. Perturbation studies — rapid task-relevant corrections, supporting feedback control accounts.

**Narrowing recorded.** Synergy analysis narrows the reach: low-dimensional structure stands recovered under a stated decomposition. Uncontrolled manifold analysis narrows the reach: structure stands established for the task-relevant variables specified.

**Progress.** The trajectory fits under several cost functions. The stated identifiability limits. The measured low-dimensional structure. The measured task-relevant variability and rapid corrections.

**Own record.**

## 146 The Whorf question

**Conditions prior.** The statement holds across readings. The terms have determinate reference. The accounts hold their truth value while tested. A procedure repeated returns the same outcome independently of what precedes it. Nothing prior enters.

**Statement.** Does the language a person speaks shape how they think?

**The field's account of its hardness.**

**Accounts.** Yes, measurably: colour categories affect discrimination speed with lateralised effects, grammatical gender affects object descriptions, and spatial frame languages produce different non-linguistic spatial reasoning, replicated across communities.

The effects are real and narrow: they appear largely in tasks where language is available as a strategy and diminish under verbal interference, core cognition — number approximation, object tracking, basic spatial reasoning — appears language-independent, and the strong version has not been supported.

**Specifications.** Thought and language are separable — local. Non-linguistic tasks measure thought without language — local.

**Specifications withdrawn.**

**Common requirement.** That a task can measure thought independently of language.

**Conserving relation.** Thought measured independently of language use, the independence standing in the tasks employed.

**Conserving reach.** All tasks the question is asked of.

**Reach.** The statement quantifies over thought as distinct from language use. Task performance, in participants who have language available, is what is measured. The two are not the same.

**Unreachability.** Verbal interference removes many effects, establishing they are partly strategic rather than absent, and the remaining effects are read oppositely; spatial frame differences are confounded by environment and practice, which the field notes. The independence is not reached over the tasks by performance in participants who have language available.

**Persistence.** A task measuring thought with language unavailable, or agreement on what the interference results establish.

**Addresses.** Keyed property by property.

*The report.*

**Approaches.** Verbal interference designs — reduce or remove many effects, the strongest route, establishing the effects are partly strategic rather than absent. Colour category studies — lateralised effects replicated, interpreted as language-driven categorical perception or as label-based strategy. Spatial frame studies — differences in non-linguistic tasks across communities, robust, confounded by environment and practice, which the field notes. Infant and prelinguistic studies — establish capacities before language, bearing on the strong version only. Bilingual switching studies — within-person shifts by language of task, supporting an online rather than a structural effect.

**Narrowing recorded.** Verbal interference narrows the reach: the residual effects stand for tasks where language is unavailable as a strategy. Infant studies narrow the reach: the capacities stand established before language.

**Progress.** The replicated lateralised colour effects. The measured reduction under verbal interference. The cross-community spatial differences. The prelinguistic capacities. The within-person bilingual shifts.

**Own record.**

## 147 Grandmother cell and distributed coding

**Conditions prior.** The statement holds across readings. The terms have determinate reference. The accounts hold their truth value while tested. A procedure repeated returns the same outcome independently of what precedes it. Nothing prior enters.

**Statement.** Is a concept represented by a small number of highly selective neurons, or by a pattern across many?

**The field's account of its hardness.**

**Accounts.** Sparse and selective: recordings in human medial temporal lobe find single neurons responding to one individual across photographs taken at different angles, line drawings, and the written and spoken name — invariance across format that no low-level feature accounts for. Firing rates from a handful of such cells suffice to decode which of many individuals was seen, and the same cells respond to associated concepts.

Distributed: the recorded cells are the tail of a broad distribution, recording is biased toward responsive units, and population decoding from many weakly tuned neurons outperforms sparse readout in most systems studied.

**Specifications.** The representation has a determinate sparseness — local. Recording samples the population without bias — local.

**Specifications withdrawn.**

**Common requirement.** That the sampled neurons represent the population.

**Conserving relation.** Sparseness determined across a population, the determination standing on an unbiased sample.

**Conserving reach.** The whole population the concept is represented in.

**Reach.** The statement quantifies over sparseness across a population, from a screened sample. Neurons selected by responsiveness during a search, in patients with clinical electrode placement, are what is measured. The two are not the same.

**Unreachability.** Human recordings and animal population recordings give opposite pictures with the sampling procedures differing; and both codes are proved efficient under different constraints, which does not select. The determination is not reached over the population by a screened sample.

**Persistence.** An unbiased sample of the population in the areas where the selective cells are found.

**Addresses.** Keyed property by property.

*The report.*

**Approaches.** Single-unit recording in humans — finds highly selective cells reliably, sampling driven by a screening procedure, which the authors state. Large-scale population recording — finds broad, weak tuning dominating in animal cortex, establishing the distributed picture in accessible areas. Decoding comparisons — population decoding generally outperforming sparse, depending on the recorded set. Sparseness estimation from unbiased sampling — calcium imaging giving less biased samples and finding sparse but not single-cell coding. Theoretical analysis — both codes efficient under different constraints, proved, and not selecting.

**Narrowing recorded.** Calcium imaging narrows the reach: sparseness stands estimated from less biased samples in the areas imaged. Population recording narrows the reach: the distributed picture stands established in the accessible areas.

**Progress.** The invariant selective responses across format. The measured broad tuning in animal cortex. The decoding comparisons. The sparseness estimates from less biased sampling.

**Own record.**

## 148 The faint young sun

**Conditions prior.** The statement holds across readings. The terms have determinate reference. The accounts hold their truth value while tested. A procedure repeated returns the same outcome independently of what precedes it. Nothing prior enters.

**Statement.** Stellar models say the early sun was substantially dimmer. Geological evidence says liquid water was present. How?

**The field's account of its hardness.**

**Accounts.** Greenhouse compensation: higher concentrations of carbon dioxide, methane, or both offset the reduced luminosity, and the carbonate-silicate weathering cycle supplies a feedback that would have maintained whatever concentration was needed, drawing carbon dioxide down as the sun brightened. The mechanism is quantitative, operates on the right timescale, and is the same feedback regulating climate over geological time today.

The proposed concentrations conflict with proxies: paleosol and other constraints bound early carbon dioxide below several proposed values, methane is limited by photochemistry in an anoxic atmosphere, and the remaining candidates — reduced albedo, different cloud behaviour, higher nitrogen pressure — each carry their own difficulties.

**Specifications.** Stellar evolution models are correct for the early sun — universal under stellar astrophysics. Proxies constrain early atmospheric composition — local.

**Specifications withdrawn.**

**Common requirement.** That the early state is reconstructible from present evidence.

**Conserving relation.** Early composition and albedo reconstructed, the reconstruction standing across the proxy families.

**Conserving reach.** The early state, which occurred once.

**Reach.** The statement quantifies over early composition and albedo, reconstructed from proxies calibrated by models. Proxies with wide uncertainty and model-dependent calibration are what is measured. The two are not the same.

**Unreachability.** Proxy bounds and greenhouse requirements conflict, and the proxy families are contested in calibration against each other, with the newer aqueous-chemistry technique returning bounds raised by an order against the classic studies. The reconstruction is not preserved across the proxy families.

**Persistence.** Proxy families agreeing on early composition, or a constraint independent of model-dependent calibration.

**Addresses.** Keyed property by property.

*The report.*

**Approaches.** Carbon dioxide greenhouse — requires concentrations the classic proxy studies bounded below, the newer aqueous-chemistry paleosol technique returning bounds raised by an order and reopening the route, the proxy families remaining contested in calibration against each other. Methane greenhouse — effective in anoxic conditions, photochemical haze formation limiting it and able to cool by shading, modelled both ways. Reduced albedo — less land, fewer cloud nuclei, plausible and hard to constrain from evidence. Higher nitrogen pressure — broadens absorption lines, the proxy estimates having converged low, with vesicle, imprint, and isotope instruments independently bounding early pressure at or below modern, closing the thick-atmosphere version of this route. Revising the solar model — a more massive early sun losing mass, constrained by other observations and not favoured.

**Narrowing recorded.** The nitrogen route stands closed in its thick-atmosphere version by three independent instruments converging low. The aqueous-chemistry technique narrows the reach: the raised bound stands under that technique's calibration.

**Progress.** The quantified weathering feedback. The classic and revised carbon dioxide bounds. The photochemical limits on methane. The convergent low bounds on early nitrogen pressure.

**Own record.**

## 149 The origin of the genetic code

**Conditions prior.** The statement holds across readings. The terms have determinate reference. The accounts hold their truth value while tested. A procedure repeated returns the same outcome independently of what precedes it. Nothing prior enters.

**Statement.** The mapping from codons to amino acids is nearly universal. Why this mapping?

**The field's account of its hardness.**

**Accounts.** Selection for error tolerance: the assignment places chemically similar amino acids at codons differing by one base, so point mutations and mistranslation produce conservative substitutions. Measured against millions of randomly generated alternative codes, the natural code ranks in the extreme tail on every error metric tried, and the effect is strongest at the codon positions where errors are most frequent.

Optimality does not establish origin: stereochemical affinities between codons and amino acids are reported for several pairs, coevolution with biosynthetic pathways accounts for the block structure, the error-minimisation could be a consequence of an origin fixed for other reasons, and the accounts are not separable by the code's structure alone.

**Specifications.** The mapping was fixed by a process — local. The code's present structure carries evidence of it — local.

**Specifications withdrawn.**

**Common requirement.** That present structure constrains the origin.

**Conserving relation.** The origin of the mapping identified, the identification standing on the code's present structure.

**Conserving reach.** The fixing of the mapping, which occurred once.

**Reach.** The statement quantifies over the origin of the mapping, constrained by its present structure. The code's structure, extant variants, and laboratory affinity measurements are what is measured. The two are not the same.

**Unreachability.** Error-minimisation, stereochemical, and coevolutionary accounts each fit the code's structure, which is consistent with several origins, and the accounts are not separable by that structure alone. The identification is not reached over the fixing by present structure.

**Persistence.** A feature of the code that one account predicts and the others do not, or a constraint on the origin independent of present structure.

**Addresses.** Keyed property by property.

*The report.*

**Approaches.** Error-minimisation statistics — the code ranking extremely high among random alternatives, robustly measured, consistent with several origins. Stereochemical affinity experiments — aptamer selection finding codon-related affinities for several amino acids, contested in statistical treatment, covering part of the code. Coevolution accounts — the block structure accounted for by biosynthetic relatedness, with good fit, also consistent with other origins. Code variants — natural and engineered variants showing the code is changeable, establishing it is not frozen absolutely. Ancestral reconstruction — infers earlier smaller codes, model-dependent and not decisive between accounts.

**Narrowing recorded.** Stereochemical experiments narrow the reach: affinities stand measured for the pairs tested. Coevolution accounts narrow the reach: the block structure stands accounted for rather than the whole mapping.

**Progress.** The measured ranking against random alternatives on every metric tried. The measured codon-related affinities. The block-structure fit. The natural and engineered variants.

**Own record.**

## 150 The origin of language

**Conditions prior.** The statement holds across readings. The terms have determinate reference. The accounts hold their truth value while tested. A procedure repeated returns the same outcome independently of what precedes it. Nothing prior enters.

**Statement.** Language appears in one lineage. Was it a gradual extension of prior communication or a discrete change?

**The field's account of its hardness.**

**Accounts.** Gradual: precursors are present in primate communication, vocal learning, and gesture, anatomical and archaeological indicators extend back hundreds of thousands of years, and no evidence supports a single event.

Discrete in at least one respect: recursive combination has no demonstrated animal analogue, accounts of its gradual emergence do not specify intermediate states with a function, and the discontinuity is in the record and in the comparative data alike.

**Specifications.** Language decomposes into components with independent evolutionary histories, or it is a unit — local. The capacity's components are identifiable — local.

**Specifications withdrawn.**

**Common requirement.** That the capacity's components are identifiable.

**Conserving relation.** The emergence of syntactic capacity determined as gradual or discrete, the determination standing on anatomy, artefact, and comparative data.

**Conserving reach.** The emergence, which occurred once.

**Reach.** The statement quantifies over syntactic capacity, indexed by anatomy and artefact. Comparative animal data, anatomy, ancient DNA, and material culture are what is measured. The two are not the same.

**Unreachability.** Precursors are established for every component but recursion, anatomical indicators are contested in inferential value, and archaeological proxies bear on symbolic capacity rather than syntax. The determination is not reached over the emergence by comparative data, anatomy, and material culture.

**Persistence.** An index of syntactic capacity in the record, or an animal analogue of recursive combination.

**Addresses.** Keyed property by property.

*The report.*

**Approaches.** Comparative studies — establish precursors for many components, vocal learning, intention reading, combinatorial signalling, and none for recursion. Anatomical indicators — hyoid and hypoglossal canal evidence, contested in inferential value. Ancient DNA — candidate genes identified, one gene's early story substantially revised by later data, a documented reversal. Archaeological proxies — symbolic artefacts as indirect evidence, bearing on symbolic capacity rather than syntax. Computational modelling of emergence — produces combinatorial systems from communication pressures in simulation, establishing sufficiency in models.

**Narrowing recorded.** Comparative studies narrow the reach: precursors stand established component by component rather than for the capacity. Computational modelling narrows the reach: sufficiency stands established in simulation.

**Progress.** The established precursors for each component but recursion. The anatomical indicators with their stated inferential limits. The documented revision of the candidate-gene account. The combinatorial systems produced in simulation.

**Own record.**

## 151 The Poincaré conjecture

**Conditions prior.** The statement holds across readings. The terms have determinate reference. The accounts hold their truth value while tested. A procedure repeated returns the same outcome independently of what precedes it. Nothing prior enters.

**Statement.** Is every simply connected closed three-manifold a three-sphere?

**The field's account of its hardness.**

**Accounts.** Every such manifold is a three-sphere.

**Specifications.** Three-manifolds admit a geometric decomposition — universal under geometrisation. A flow on the metric can be continued through singularities by surgery — local.

**Specifications withdrawn.**

**Common requirement.**

**Conserving relation.** A proof holding for every simply connected closed three-manifold.

**Conserving reach.** All simply connected closed three-manifolds.

**Reach.** The statement quantifies over all simply connected closed three-manifolds. The flow, its singularities, and the surgery construction are what is worked with. The two are the same: the method reaches the domain the statement quantifies over.

**Unreachability.**

**Persistence.** Settled. The proof stands, verified by several independent groups over years, and is accepted without dissent.

**Addresses.** Keyed property by property.

*The report.*

**Approaches.** The geometrisation programme — frames the conjecture within a decomposition of three-manifolds. Ricci flow — founds the method. The surgery construction — completes it, continuing the flow through singularities. Verification — carried out by several independent groups over the following years.

**Narrowing recorded.**

**Progress.** The proof. The geometrisation theorem it establishes. The verification record.

**Own record.** The difficulty stood in constructing the proof, not in the statement quantifying over a domain the methods do not reach.

## 152 The block universe and the low-entropy past

**Conditions prior.** The statement holds across readings. The terms have determinate reference. The accounts hold their truth value while tested. A procedure repeated returns the same outcome independently of what precedes it. Nothing prior enters.

**Statement.** Is the distinction between past and future a feature of the world or of our position in it?

**The field's account of its hardness.**

**Accounts.** Of our position: the laws are symmetric, the four-dimensional description is complete, and the asymmetry we experience follows from the entropy gradient given a low-entropy boundary condition.

The boundary condition is not derived, and the account accounts for the arrow by stipulating it: attempts to derive the past hypothesis have not succeeded, and accounts positing a dynamically distinguished present remain consistent with the physics.

**Specifications.** Temporal asymmetry is either in the laws, in a boundary condition, or in the observer's position — local. These are exhaustive — local.

**Specifications withdrawn.**

**Common requirement.** That the asymmetry has a locus.

**Conserving relation.** The locus of the asymmetry determined, the determination standing without the boundary condition stipulated.

**Conserving reach.** The status of the boundary condition.

**Reach.** The statement quantifies over the status of the boundary condition. The consequences of whatever condition obtained are what is measured. The two are not the same.

**Unreachability.** The statistical derivation is complete given the past hypothesis, and each cosmological proposal contains a stipulation at the same place. The determination is not reached over the boundary condition by its consequences.

**Persistence.** A derivation of the boundary condition not stipulating it, or agreement that a stipulation is acceptable.

**Addresses.** Keyed property by property.

*The report.*

**Approaches.** Statistical derivation — complete given the past hypothesis. Cosmological derivation of the initial state — proposals existing, each containing a stipulation at the same place. Psychological accounts of temporal experience — the sense of passage accounted for by memory asymmetry, which itself follows from the entropy gradient, consistent and not deriving the condition. Dynamically distinguished present — available and unfalsifiable given relativistic equivalence. Two-time boundary conditions — consistent constructions, not favoured by evidence.

**Narrowing recorded.** Psychological accounts narrow the reach: the sense of passage stands accounted for given the gradient.

**Progress.** The statistical derivation given the hypothesis. The memory-asymmetry account of temporal experience. The consistent two-time constructions.

**Own record.**

## 153 Riemann, Hodge, Birch–Swinnerton-Dyer, Yang–Mills

**Conditions prior.** The statement holds across readings. The terms have determinate reference. The accounts hold their truth value while tested. A procedure repeated returns the same outcome independently of what precedes it. Nothing prior enters.

**Statement.** In each of four conjectures, does the disputed object lie at the fixed set or centre of a proved symmetry? The functional equation taking s to 1−s, and the critical line. Complex conjugation, and balanced classes. The functional equation taking s to 2−s, and the behaviour at the centre. Gauge symmetry, and the existence of a mass gap.

**The field's account of its hardness.**

**Accounts.** In each, the disputed object lies at the fixed set or centre. One account stands in each of the four; no negation is defended.

**Specifications.** The symmetry is proved in each case — universal under the respective field. The location claim is not proved — local.

**Specifications withdrawn.**

**Common requirement.**

**Conserving relation.** A proof of the location claim, standing over the objects the conjecture quantifies over.

**Conserving reach.** All zeros; all classes; all curves; all states of the theory.

**Reach.** The statement quantifies over the location of the disputed object relative to the proved symmetry. Verified instances, partial results, and the symmetry itself are what is measured. The two are not the same.

**Unreachability.**

**Persistence.** In each case, a proof of the location claim, or an object off the symmetric locus: a zero off the critical line, a balanced class with no algebraic representative, two counts differing, a gap shown absent. Each is answerable true or false.

**Addresses.** Keyed property by property.

*The report.*

**Approaches.** The proved symmetries — functional equations and conjugation, standing in the fields' own records. Verified instances and partial results — standing in each case. The structural observation recorded here — that each disputed object sits at the fixed set of a proved symmetry — is the compendium's own, and whether an entry by structure without a defended pair should be admitted is unresolved and recorded rather than decided.

**Narrowing recorded.**

**Progress.** The proved symmetries. The verified instances and partial results in each case.

**Own record.**

## 154 Origin and fate of the universe

**Conditions prior.** The statement holds across readings. The terms have determinate reference. The accounts hold their truth value while tested. A procedure repeated returns the same outcome independently of what precedes it. Nothing prior enters.

**Statement.** Did the universe have a beginning, and will it have an end?

**The field's account of its hardness.**

**Accounts.** A beginning is indicated: expansion extrapolated backward reaches a state of extreme density, singularity theorems show that under stated energy conditions geodesics are incomplete to the past, and inflationary spacetimes are past-incomplete by a proved argument.

## 156 Structure and agency

**Conditions prior.** The statement holds across readings. The terms have determinate reference. The accounts hold their truth value while tested. A procedure repeated returns the same outcome independently of what precedes it. Nothing prior enters.

**Statement.** Social outcomes are produced by people acting and by the structures they act within. Which explains which?

**The field's account of its hardness.**

**Accounts.** Structure: regularities in outcomes persist across changes of personnel, patterned by class, institution, and position, individuals act within constraints they did not choose and mostly reproduce them, and explanation at the level of individual choice misses what is doing the work.

Agency: structures have no existence apart from what people do, they are reproduced only by being enacted, treating them as causes reifies a pattern into a thing, and explanation must run through the actions that constitute the regularity.

**Specifications.** Social outcomes have causes — local. The causes are located either in properties of persons or in properties of arrangements — local. The two levels are distinguishable in measurement — local.

**Specifications withdrawn.**

**Common requirement.** That structure and action are two things with a determination relation between them.

**Conserving relation.** The explanatory level identified, the identification standing where the two levels vary independently.

**Conserving reach.** All outcomes the question is asked of.

**Reach.** The statement quantifies over which level does the explanatory work. Outcomes in which both levels are present, and cannot be varied independently, are what is measured. The two are not the same.

**Unreachability.** Structuration makes the two inseparable, which its critics hold removes the question; multilevel modelling returns a partition conditional on the levels specified; and natural experiments where one level changes and the other does not are rare and confounded by selection into the change. The identification is not reached over the outcomes by measurements in which both levels are present.

**Persistence.** A comparison in which the two levels vary independently, or agreement on a level at which the explanation is owed.

**Addresses.** Keyed property by property.

*The report.*

**Approaches.** Structuration — structure as both medium and outcome of action, widely adopted and criticised as redescribing the relation rather than analysing it, making the two inseparable by definition. Critical realism — separates them analytically over time, structure preceding action and being elaborated by it, requiring a temporal separation its critics hold is stipulated rather than found. Methodological individualism — derives regularities from individual action, facing the aggregation problem, the same regularity following from many individual-level specifications, and the emergence objection. Multilevel statistical modelling — partitions variance between individual and contextual levels, returning a partition conditional on the levels specified. Natural experiments in institutional change — comparison available where arrangements change and personnel do not, or the reverse, rare and confounded by selection into the change.

**Narrowing recorded.** Critical realism narrows the reach: the separation stands over a stipulated temporal sequence. Multilevel modelling narrows the reach: the partition stands conditional on the levels specified.

**Progress.** The measured persistence of regularities across changes of personnel. The variance partitions under specified levels. The natural-experiment comparisons where available.

**Own record.**

## 157 The mechanism of high-temperature superconductivity

**Conditions prior.** The statement holds across readings. The terms have determinate reference. The accounts hold their truth value while tested. A procedure repeated returns the same outcome independently of what precedes it. Nothing prior enters.

**Statement.** What binds electrons into pairs in the copper-oxide superconductors at their observed critical temperatures, and what the anomalous normal state is from which the superconductivity condenses. The field maintains the statement in several standing formulations, carried as the field carries them: the physics of a doped Mott insulator; the pairing interaction of the unconventional superconductors; intertwined orders in the phase diagram; the identification of the underlying ground states; the nature of the strange-metal normal state.

**The field's account of its hardness.** That a qualitative understanding of the superconducting state is achieved while the phase diagram's complexity, the prominence of collective fluctuations, and the normal state's simplicity and material-insensitivity stand unresolved; that certain experiments may probe beyond the single-band model; and that the field's best methods disagree on its minimal model.

**Accounts.** The pairing is electronic, arising from repulsion, with antiferromagnetic spin fluctuations as the mediating channel and the single-band Hubbard model as the stated arena; within the model at interaction strengths relevant to the materials, fluctuation diagnostics identify the spin channel as the pairing glue.

The single-band electronic frame does not close the statement: accounts retaining lattice and phonon involvement stand in the record, classes of experiments are recorded as possibly probing physics beyond the single-band model on the studied clusters, and the intertwined-orders reading holds that no single pairing channel constitutes the account.

**Specifications.** Superconductivity is a paired-electron condensate, with the conventional phonon-mediated mechanism established — universal under superconductivity, and the given against which the statement is posed. Metals are described by Fermi-liquid theory — universal under condensed matter, and the normal-state given against which the strange metal stands. The physics of the copper-oxide planes is captured by the Hubbard model at relevant filling and interaction — local, the field's stated minimal-model commitment.

**Specifications withdrawn.**

**Common requirement.** That there is a mechanism — a fact about what mediates the pairing — expressible within the framework.

**Conserving relation.** A mechanism established for the real materials, the establishment standing from the minimal model to the materials.

**Conserving reach.** The real materials' pairing and normal state.

**Reach.** The statement quantifies over the real materials' pairing and normal state. The field's best numerical methods disagree on the minimal model's ground state at the relevant parameters, by the field's own multi-method benchmark; at strong coupling in the relevant regime, stripe order is established as the model's low-energy state; and the spectroscopic probes reach different regimes of the phase diagram, with their reaches stated in the review literature. The two are not the same.

**Unreachability.** The best methods disagree on the minimal model, by the field's own benchmark, and the model-versus-material gap stands stated in the field's own record; the probes disagree on the pseudogap's onset, a standing characterization dispute recorded in the reviews. The establishment is not reached over the materials by results within the minimal model or by the probes.

**Persistence.** The mechanism established to the field's agreement; a theory with predictive capacity for critical temperatures from composition.

**Addresses.** Keyed property by property.

*The report.*

**Approaches.** Materials search — critical temperatures measured across the copper-oxide families, hydride superconductivity standing at higher temperatures under pressure with the mechanism there recorded as conventional, nickelate superconductivity established, extending the unconventional family. Numerical attack on the minimal model — the multi-method benchmark standing, stripe order established as the model's relevant low-energy state, the pairing glue identified within the model at intermediate coupling, the model-versus-material gap stated in the field's own record. Spectroscopic mapping — the pairing symmetry established, the phase diagram mapped, the pseudogap characterized by multiple probes which disagree on its onset. Quantum simulation — the pseudogap observed in a cold-atom realization of the model, by thermodynamic and spectroscopic measurement at newly attainable temperatures, a new instrument class entering the record. Phenomenology of intertwined orders — the orders characterized and their relations stated, answering adjacent questions by its own account where the mechanism is concerned.

**Narrowing recorded.** Numerical work narrows the reach: the pairing glue stands identified within the minimal model at intermediate coupling rather than for the materials. Quantum simulation narrows the reach: the pseudogap stands realized in a model system.

**Progress.** The established pairing symmetry. The mapped phase diagram across material families. Stripe order established as the minimal model's relevant low-energy state. The superconducting state qualitatively understood, in the field's own summary. The cold-atom realization.

**Own record.**

**Narrowing recorded.** Pluralism narrows the reach: each account stands for the subfield whose usage it fits.

**Progress.** The measured extensions of each account. The recorded cases sorting differently. The documented division of usage by subfield.

**Own record.**

## 168 The physical Church–Turing thesis

**Conditions prior.** The statement holds across readings. The terms have determinate reference. The accounts hold their truth value while tested. A procedure repeated returns the same outcome independently of what precedes it. Nothing prior enters.

**Statement.** Whether every physically realizable process is computable by the machine model — the thesis read as a claim about nature.

**The field's account of its hardness.**

**Accounts.** It holds: known physics supports it, and the quantum model preserves computability while changing complexity.

It is open or fails: spacetimes and processes are describable in which the model is exceeded, and the thesis' physical standing is an empirical question rather than a definition.

**Specifications.** The mathematical thesis standing as the founding agreement on the computable — universal under computability theory. The physical reading standing as its extension to nature — local.

**Specifications withdrawn.**

**Common requirement.** A fact about physical law's computational reach.

**Conserving relation.** A bound on nature's computational reach, standing over physically realizable processes.

**Conserving reach.** All physically realizable processes.

**Reach.** The statement quantifies over nature's computational bound. Realized computations are what is measured — all within the model — and the exceeding constructions stand as descriptions whose physical realizability is disputed. The two are not the same.

**Unreachability.** All realized computation stands within the model, and the exceeding constructions are descriptions whose realizability is disputed, so no realized computation bears on the modal question. The bound is not reached over the realizable processes by realized computations.

**Persistence.** A physically realized process exceeding the model, or a demonstration that the exceeding constructions are unrealizable.

**Addresses.** Keyed property by property.

*The report.*

**Approaches.** Quantum computation — preserves the computability bound, alters complexity, standing as the record's main extension. Hypercomputation constructions — standing described, relativistic and analog, their realizability disputed in the record. Physical formulations — the thesis' formalizations against physical principles standing published, the principles' status disputed.

**Narrowing recorded.** Quantum computation narrows the reach: the complexity claim stands altered while the computability bound stands preserved.

**Progress.** The preserved computability bound under quantum computation. The described exceeding constructions. The published formalizations against physical principles.

**Own record.**

## 169 Micro-to-macro aggregation

**Conditions prior.** The statement holds across readings. The terms have determinate reference. The accounts hold their truth value while tested. A procedure repeated returns the same outcome independently of what precedes it. Nothing prior enters.

**Statement.** Whether aggregate economic behaviour inherits the structure of individual rationality.

**The field's account of its hardness.** The practice stands beside the proof — the field's recorded methodological split.

**Accounts.** Sufficiently, for practice: representative-agent methods stand as the working instrument, defended as approximation.

Not in general, and provably: market excess demand inherits essentially no restrictions from individual rationality, standing proved, and the representative-agent practice stands undermined by it.

**Specifications.** Individual rationality axioms standing as the founding micro theory — universal under microeconomics. Equilibrium as the aggregate framework — universal under general equilibrium theory.

**Specifications withdrawn.**

**Common requirement.** An account of the aggregate's relation to the individuals.

**Conserving relation.** Structure inherited from individuals to the aggregate, the inheritance standing under aggregation.

**Conserving reach.** All aggregates the individuals compose.

**Reach.** The statement quantifies over the inheritance of structure under aggregation. The theorems, and the empirical aggregates the practice fits, are what is measured. The two are not the same.

**Unreachability.** Rationality's restrictions wash out in aggregation, standing proved. The inheritance is not preserved over the aggregates by any restriction on individual behaviour.

**Persistence.** Conditions under which structure is inherited, or agreement on the practice's standing given the theorems.

**Addresses.** Keyed property by property.

*The report.*

**Approaches.** The aggregation theorems — ending in proved limitation, rationality's restrictions washing out in aggregation. Representative-agent practice — standing as the field's working method, with the theorems recorded against its foundations in the field's own methodological literature. Heterogeneous-agent programmes — standing as the recorded response, rebuilding aggregates from distributions.

**Narrowing recorded.** Heterogeneous-agent programmes narrow the reach: the aggregate stands rebuilt from a specified distribution rather than inherited.

**Progress.** The aggregation theorems. The practice's empirical fits. The heterogeneous-agent reconstructions.

**Own record.**

## 170 Ubiquitous one-over-f noise

**Conditions prior.** The statement holds across readings. The terms have determinate reference. The accounts hold their truth value while tested. A procedure repeated returns the same outcome independently of what precedes it. Nothing prior enters.

**Statement.** Why fluctuation spectra with power inversely proportional to frequency stand measured across systems sharing no mechanism — from conductors to rivers to music.

**The field's account of its hardness.**

**Accounts.** A universal mechanism stands to be found, criticality-based accounts proposing the common origin.

No universal mechanism: the spectra are a family of look-alikes with unrelated origins, and the ubiquity is the signature of many roads to the same asymptotics.

**Specifications.** Spectral analysis standing as the shared instrument — universal across the host fields. Each host field's own dynamics standing as its candidate mechanism — local.

**Specifications withdrawn.**

**Common requirement.** An account of the ubiquity — one mechanism, or a classification of many.

**Conserving relation.** An account of the shared spectrum, standing across the systems it is measured in.

**Conserving reach.** All systems the spectrum is measured in.

**Reach.** The statement quantifies over the origin of the shared spectrum. The spectra are measured everywhere, mechanisms are established in particular systems, and no cross-system mechanism stands established by the record. The two are not the same.

**Unreachability.** The mechanisms established are local, and broad classes of processes are shown to yield the spectrum, which supports the many-roads reading and does not select. The account is not preserved across the systems by spectra measured in each.

**Persistence.** A mechanism established across systems sharing no dynamics, or a classification of the roads accepted as complete.

**Addresses.** Keyed property by property.

*The report.*

**Approaches.** Criticality programmes — standing proposed as universal, their scope disputed in the record. System-specific mechanisms — standing established case by case. Mathematical genericity results — standing recorded, broad classes of processes yielding the spectrum, supporting the many-roads reading.

**Narrowing recorded.** System-specific mechanisms narrow the reach: an account stands established per system rather than across them.

**Progress.** The spectra measured across fields. The mechanisms established in particular systems. The genericity results.

**Own record.**

**Accounts.** None stands as a two-position dispute in the field's current record. What stands is a programme with measured parts.

**Specifications.** Axiomatization is the appropriate demand for a physical theory — local, and itself disputed in the field's methodological literature.

**Specifications withdrawn.**

**Common requirement.**

**Conserving relation.** An axiomatization covering physics.

**Conserving reach.** Physics as a whole.

**Reach.** The statement quantifies over physics as a whole. What is established stands per part: probability axiomatized; quantum mechanics carrying its axiomatic reconstructions; field-theoretic programmes partial, with constructive results in lower dimensions and the physical theories standing outside the axioms' reach. The two are not the same.

**Persistence.** Stated per part in each programme's own literature; no whole-physics criterion stands recorded.

Field-theoretic programmes — the founding axiom systems standing, the constructive results holding in lower dimensions, the physical theories standing outside the axioms' reach, the standing gap being the four-dimensional interacting construction.

## 173 The strange metal

**Conditions prior.** The statement holds across readings. The terms have determinate reference. The accounts hold their truth value while tested. A procedure repeated returns the same outcome independently of what precedes it. Nothing prior enters.

**Statement.** What the strange-metal state is — resistivity linear in temperature across regimes where the quasiparticle framework's scales should intervene.

**The field's account of its hardness.**

**Accounts.** A universal state stands behind the linearity: a bound on dissipation, or a new fixed point without quasiparticles, governing across materials.

No universal state: material-specific mechanisms produce the linearity separately, and the universality is apparent.

**Specifications.** Fermi-liquid theory standing as the framework the state defies — universal under condensed matter. The transport measurements standing as the record — local.

**Specifications withdrawn.**

**Common requirement.** An account of the linearity and its cross-material reach.

**Conserving relation.** An account of the state, standing across the material families.

**Conserving reach.** All materials the linearity is measured in.

**Reach.** The statement quantifies over the nature of the state. The transport record across families, the scale analyses, and the proposed bound's saturation — measured in cases and disputed in scope — are what is measured. The two are not the same.

**Unreachability.** The linearity stands measured across families and the material-by-material accounts stand established in cases, while the proposed bound's universality stands disputed in scope. The account is not preserved across the families by the transport record.

**Persistence.** An account of the state holding across the material families, or a demonstration that the linearity has separate origins in each.

**Addresses.** Keyed property by property.

*The report.*

**Approaches.** The dissipation-bound reading — standing proposed and tested, its universality disputed in the record. Exactly solvable models — the correlated models yielding the linearity standing as theoretical anchors, their relation to the materials open. Material-by-material accounts — standing established in cases.

**Narrowing recorded.** Material-by-material accounts narrow the reach: the linearity stands accounted for per material. Solvable models narrow the reach: the linearity stands derived in models whose relation to the materials is open.

**Own record.**
